# üèõÔ∏è SCEAU ‚Äî Bilan de la Version 1 (V1)

## üöÄ Statut : D√©ploy√© et Fonctionnel
L'application SCEAU (V1) est officiellement en ligne. Le code est stable, les interfaces communiquent, et la plomberie de stockage est branch√©e.

## üõ†Ô∏è Stack Technique
- **Frontend & Backend** : Next.js 16 (App Router), React, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS.
- **Base de donn√©es** : PostgreSQL h√©berg√© sur Neon.
- **Stockage de fichiers** : Compatible protocole S3 (Actuellement sur Supabase, pr√™t √† migrer vers Cloudflare R2 ou AWS en 5 minutes via variables d'environnement).
- **Ancrage Cryptographique** : OpenTimestamps (Ancrage des hashs dans la blockchain Bitcoin).
- **S√©curit√©** : Hachage c√¥t√© client avec `@noble/hashes` (le fichier original ne quitte jamais l'appareil sans consentement).

## üß© Fonctionnalit√©s Impl√©ment√©es
1. **L'Atelier** : Scellement de fichiers, textes, audios et photos.
2. **Sceau Flash** : Preuve locale sans h√©bergement (pour les secrets industriels ou id√©es fugaces).
3. **Sceau Myst√®re** : Fichier masqu√© sur le feed jusqu'√† obtention d'un certain nombre de "J'aime".
4. **Sceau √âph√©m√®re** : Dispara√Æt du feed public apr√®s 24h (mais la preuve reste).
5. **Le Feed Global** : R√©actions multiples (Like, Wow, Inspirant), Tendances, et boutons de partage.
6. **Le Cercle** : Syst√®me d'amis complet et syst√®me de "D√©fis" de cr√©ation.
7. **Messagerie** : Messages priv√©s, groupes de discussion et Pactes multi-signatures.
8. **Le Coffre** : 4 compartiments (Public, Amis, Priv√©, Flash) avec possibilit√© de d√©placer les ≈ìuvres.
9. **Le Portfolio** : G√©n√©ration de PDF modulaire (filtrage par date, par compartiment, avec int√©gration des liens).
10. **La preuve** : Manifeste des 3 piliers (Droit d'auteur, Ant√©riorit√©, Journal des acc√®s).

## ‚öñÔ∏è La Philosophie (Rappel)
SCEAU ne r√©invente pas la loi. SCEAU lui donne des yeux.
- **La technique est pr√™te** : Chaque hash est grav√© via OpenTimestamps.
- **La loi fera sa part** : La reconnaissance juridique viendra avec l'adoption et les partenariats (eIDAS, avocats).
- **L'adoption fera le reste** : Comme le cadenas HTTPS, SCEAU deviendra un standard invisible.

## üîú Prochaines √âtapes (V2)
- Mon√©tisation (Stripe) : Activation des murs payants (quotas de stockage, portfolio sans filigrane).
- Extension navigateur (Le badge URNE).
- API publique pour les plateformes tierces.

*G√©n√©r√© avec fiert√© le 06/11/2026.*

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notifications (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  actor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  type TEXT NOT NULL,
  message TEXT NOT NULL,
  link TEXT NOT NULL,
  is_read BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);


--- FICHIER : src/db/schema.sql ---
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  handle TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  email TEXT UNIQUE,
  password_hash TEXT,
  pubkey BYTEA NOT NULL,
  recovery_email TEXT,
  reset_token TEXT,
  reset_expiry TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS seals (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  hash BYTEA NOT NULL,
  timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  author_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  title TEXT,
  description TEXT,
  category TEXT DEFAULT 'Art',
  is_public BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
  is_hosted BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  file_path TEXT,
  file_size INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  CONSTRAINT unique_seal UNIQUE (hash, author_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  sender_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  receiver_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS likes (
  seal_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES seals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  PRIMARY KEY (seal_id, user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS comments (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  seal_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES seals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pacts (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  creator_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  file_path TEXT,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'en_attente',
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pact_signatures (
  pact_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pacts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (pact_id, user_id)
);

ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ots_proof TEXT;

ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ots_upgraded_at TIMESTAMPTZ;

ALTER TABLE pacts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ots_proof TEXT;
ALTER TABLE pacts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS scelle_at TIMESTAMPTZ;

-- Journal des acc√®s
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS access_log (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  seal_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES seals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  viewer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  action TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (action IN ('view','download')),
  context TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'public',
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_access_seal ON access_log(seal_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_access_viewer ON access_log(viewer_id);

--- FICHIER : src/lib/ots.ts ---
const CALENDARS = [
  'https://alice.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://bob.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://finney.calendar.eternitywall.com',
];

export type OtsResult = {
  ok: boolean;
  proof?: Buffer;
  calendars: string[];
  error?: string;
};

export async function submitToOts(hash: Uint8Array): Promise<OtsResult> {
  if (hash.length !== 32) {
    return { ok: false, calendars: [], error: 'hash_invalid_length' };
  }

  const successes: string[] = [];
  let proof: Buffer | undefined;

  for (const url of CALENDARS) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${url}/digest`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream' },
        body: Buffer.from(hash),
      });
      if (!res.ok) continue;

      const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
      const magic = Buffer.from([0x00,0x4f,0x70,0x65,0x6e,0x54,0x69,0x6d,0x65,0x73,0x74,0x61,0x6d,0x70,0x73,0x00,0x00,0x50,0x72,0x6f,0x6f,0x66,0x00]);
      if (!buf.subarray(0, magic.length).equals(magic)) continue;

      successes.push(url);
      if (!proof) proof = buf;
    } catch {}
  }

  if (!proof) {
    return { ok: false, calendars: successes, error: 'all_calendars_failed' };
  }
  return { ok: true, proof, calendars: successes };
}

export async function upgradeOts(hash: Uint8Array): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  const hashHex = Buffer.from(hash).toString('hex');
  for (const url of CALENDARS) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${url}/timestamp/${hashHex}`);
      if (!res.ok) continue;
      const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
      if (buf.length > 0) return buf;
    } catch {}
  }
  return null;
}


--- FICHIER : src/lib/pdf.ts ---
import { jsPDF } from "jspdf";

export function generateCertificate(title: string, author: string, date: string, hash: string, qrCodeDataUrl: string) {
  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: 'landscape', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });
  doc.setFillColor(253, 248, 240); doc.rect(0, 0, 297, 210, 'F');
  doc.setDrawColor(231, 111, 81); doc.setLineWidth(2); doc.rect(10, 10, 277, 190);
  doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(36); doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("CERTIFICAT D'ANT√âRIORIT√â", 148.5, 40, { align: "center" });
  doc.setFontSize(16); doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58); doc.text("D√©livr√© par le protocole SCEAU", 148.5, 50, { align: "center" });
  doc.setFontSize(14); doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text(`Titre : ${title}`, 30, 80); doc.text(`Cr√©ateur : @${author}`, 30, 90); doc.text(`Date : ${new Date(date).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}`, 30, 100);
  doc.setFont("courier", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(10); doc.text(`SHA-256 :`, 30, 120); doc.text(hash.slice(0, 80), 30, 125); doc.text(hash.slice(80), 30, 130);
  if(qrCodeDataUrl) { doc.addImage(qrCodeDataUrl, 'PNG', 210, 70, 60, 60); doc.setFontSize(10); doc.text("Scannez pour v√©rifier", 240, 140, { align: "center" }); }
  doc.save(`SCEAU-${title.replace(/\s+/g, '-')}.pdf`);
}

// LA CORRECTION EST ICI : on accepte un 5√®me argument optionnel
export function generatePortfolioPDF(author: string, seals: any[], startDate?: string, endDate?: string, isPro: boolean = false) {
  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: 'portrait', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });
  
  doc.setFillColor(245, 233, 216); doc.rect(0, 0, 210, 297, 'F');
  doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(40); doc.setTextColor(231, 111, 81);
  doc.text("PORTFOLIO", 105, 120, { align: "center" });
  doc.setFontSize(24); doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text(`@${author}`, 105, 140, { align: "center" });
  doc.setFontSize(12); doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  
  let dateText = `G√©n√©r√© le ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`;
  if (startDate && endDate) dateText += ` | P√©riode : du ${new Date(startDate).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')} au ${new Date(endDate).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`;
  
  doc.text(dateText, 105, 155, { align: "center" });
  doc.text(`${seals.length} ≈ìuvre(s) certifi√©e(s)`, 105, 165, { align: "center" });

  doc.addPage();
  let y = 20;
  
  seals.forEach((seal, index) => {
    if (y > 240) { doc.addPage(); y = 20; }
    doc.setDrawColor(239, 224, 202); doc.setLineWidth(1); doc.rect(15, y, 180, 60);
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold"); doc.setFontSize(16); doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
    doc.text(seal.title, 20, y + 10);
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(10); doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
    const category = seal.category || 'Art';
    const dateStr = new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR');
    doc.text(`Cat√©gorie : ${category} | Scell√© le : ${dateStr}`, 20, y + 17);
    doc.text(`‚ù§Ô∏è ${seal.likes_count || 0} J'aime   |   üí¨ ${seal.comments_count || 0} Commentaires`, 20, y + 23);
    if (seal.description) {
      doc.setFont("helvetica", "italic");
      const splitDesc = doc.splitTextToSize(`"${seal.description}"`, 170);
      doc.text(splitDesc, 20, y + 30);
    }
    doc.setFont("courier", "normal"); doc.setFontSize(8); doc.setTextColor(150, 150, 150);
    doc.text(`Hash : ${seal.hash ? seal.hash.slice(0, 80) + '...' : 'Non disponible'}`, 20, y + 50);
    if (seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path) {
      doc.setTextColor(74, 144, 194);
      doc.textWithLink(`[ Cliquez ici pour ouvrir l'≈ìuvre originale ]`, 130, y + 50, { url: seal.file_path });
    } else if (seal.is_flash) {
       doc.setTextColor(196, 74, 46);
       doc.text(`[ ‚ö° Sceau Flash - Fichier local ]`, 130, y + 50);
    }
    y += 70;
  });

  if (!isPro) {
    const pageCount = doc.getNumberOfPages();
    for (let i = 1; i <= pageCount; i++) {
      doc.setPage(i);
      doc.setFontSize(60);
      doc.setTextColor(200, 180, 160);
      doc.text("SCEAU FREE", 50, 150, { angle: 45 });
      doc.text("Passez PRO pour retirer", 50, 170, { angle: 45 });
    }
  }

  doc.save(`Portfolio-${author}.pdf`);
}

import { S3Client } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";

// Le .trim() enl√®ve les espaces invisibles qui causent l'erreur "Invalid character"

  },
  forcePathStyle: true,
});

--- FICHIER : src/app/layout.tsx ---
'use client';
import './globals.css'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation'
import Manifeste from '@/components/Manifeste'

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(false);
  
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [unread, setUnread] = useState(0);
  const [showNotifs, setShowNotifs] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHandle(localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'));
    const theme = localStorage.getItem('sceau_theme');
    if (theme === 'dark') { setIsDark(true); document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); }
  }, [pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!handle) return;
    const fetchNotifs = () => {
      fetch(`/api/notifications?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
        setNotifications(d.notifications || []);
        setUnread(d.unread || 0);
      });
    };
    fetchNotifs();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchNotifs, 10000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [handle]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    const next = !isDark; setIsDark(next);
    if (next) { document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); localStorage.setItem('sceau_theme', 'dark'); }
    else { document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark'); localStorage.setItem('sceau_theme', 'light'); }
  };

  const markAsRead = () => {
    if (unread > 0 && handle) {
      fetch('/api/notifications/read', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: handle }) });
      setUnread(0);
    }
  };

  const isActive = (path: string) => pathname === path;
  const isToolsActive = pathname === '/seal' || pathname === '/verify';

  // NOUVEAU : On d√©finit sur quelles pages le Manifeste doit s'afficher
  const showManifeste = ['/profil', '/seal', '/verify', '/settings'].includes(pathname);

  return (
    <html lang="fr">
      <body className="min-h-screen pb-24">
        <nav className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-b border-[var(--border-color)] px-6 py-4 flex justify-between items-center sticky top-0 z-50">
          <Link href="/feed" className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51]">.</span></Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <button onClick={toggleTheme} className="text-xl cursor-pointer">{isDark ? '‚òÄÔ∏è' : 'üåô'}</button>
            
            {handle && (
              <div className="relative">
                <button onClick={() => { setShowNotifs(!showNotifs); if (!showNotifs) markAsRead(); }} className="text-xl relative">
                  üîî
                  {unread > 0 && <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-red-500 text-white text-[0.6rem] font-bold w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full">{unread}</span>}
                </button>
                
                {showNotifs && (
                  <div className="absolute right-0 mt-4 w-72 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden z-50">
                    <div className="p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] font-bold text-sm">Notifications</div>
                    <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
                      {notifications.length === 0 ? <p className="p-4 text-center text-xs opacity-50">Aucune notification.</p> : 
                        notifications.map((n: any) => (
                          <Link key={n.id} href={n.link} onClick={() => setShowNotifs(false)} className={`block p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition ${!n.is_read ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0]' : ''}`}>
                            <p className="text-xs" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}><span className="font-bold text-[#E76F51]">@{n.actor}</span> {n.message}</p>
                            <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">{new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')}</span>
                          </Link>
                        ))
                      }
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {!handle ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Link href="/pricing" className="text-sm font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--text-main)] transition hidden sm:block">Tarifs</Link>
                <Link href="/signup" className="text-sm font-bold bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg">Cr√©er un compte</Link>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <Link href="/profil" className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{handle}</Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </nav>
        
        <main className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">{children}</main>
        
        {/* LE MANIFESTE S'AFFICHE CONDITIONNELLEMENT ICI */}
        {showManifeste && <Manifeste />}

        <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-t border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-around py-3 z-50">
          <Link href="/feed" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isActive('/feed') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">üåê</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Feed</span>
          </Link>
          
          <Link href="/friends" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isActive('/friends') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">üë•</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Communaut√©</span>
          </Link>
          
          <Link href="/seal" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isToolsActive ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">üõ†Ô∏è</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Outils</span>

=== SOUS-PAGES ===

--- FICHIER : src/app/api/attachments/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const sealId = searchParams.get('seal_id');
  if (!sealId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Manquant' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT a.id, a.type, a.content, a.media_url, a.created_at, u.handle as author
      FROM attachments a JOIN users u ON a.user_id = u.id
      WHERE a.seal_id = $1 ORDER BY a.created_at ASC
    `, [sealId]);
    return NextResponse.json({ attachments: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, content, type, media_url } = await req.json();
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO attachments (seal_id, user_id, content, type, media_url) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)",
      [seal_id, user.rows[0].id, content, type || 'text', media_url || null]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/forgot-password/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import crypto from 'crypto';
import { Resend } from 'resend';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { email } = await req.json();
  if (!email) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email requis' }, { status: 400 });

  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

  const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
  const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 3600000); 

  await pool.query("UPDATE users SET reset_token = $1, reset_expiry = $2 WHERE email = $3", [token, expiry, email]);

  const resetLink = `${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || 'http://localhost:3000'}/reset-password?token=${token}`;
  
  try {
    const resendKey = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY;
    if (!resendKey) {
      // Si pas de cl√©, on simule l'envoi pour le d√©veloppement
      console.log("‚ö†Ô∏è Pas de cl√© Resend. Lien de test :", resetLink);
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Lien g√©n√©r√© (voir console)" });
    }

    const resend = new Resend(resendKey);
    await resend.emails.send({
      from: 'SCEAU <onboarding@resend.dev>',
      to: email,
      subject: "R√©initialisation de votre mot de passe",
      html: `<p>Bonjour,</p><p>Voici votre lien : <a href="${resetLink}">R√©initialiser mon mot de passe</a></p>`,
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Email envoy√© !" });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Erreur d'envoi d'email" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { email, password } = await req.json();

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email ou mot de passe incorrect' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.rows[0].password_hash);
    if (!isValid) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email ou mot de passe incorrect' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    // On renvoie le handle et la cl√© publique (convertie en base64)
    return NextResponse.json({
      handle: user.rows[0].handle,
      pubkey: Buffer.from(user.rows[0].pubkey).toString('base64')
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/reset-password/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { token, newPassword } = await req.json();
  if (!token || !newPassword) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs requis' }, { status: 400 });

  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE reset_token = $1 AND reset_expiry > NOW()", [token]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Lien invalide ou expir√©' }, { status: 400 });

  const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
  const password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(newPassword, salt);

  await pool.query("UPDATE users SET password_hash = $1, reset_token = NULL, reset_expiry = NULL WHERE id = $2", [password_hash, user.rows[0].id]);

  return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/signup/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { handle, email, password, pubkey } = await req.json();

    if (!handle || !email || !password || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Tous les champs sont requis' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
    const password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(password, salt);

    const result = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO users (handle, email, password_hash, pubkey) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, decode($4, 'base64')) RETURNING id, handle", 
      [handle, email, password_hash, pubkey]
    );
    
    return NextResponse.json(result.rows[0]);
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Erreur d'inscription:", error);
    if (error.code === '23505') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ce pseudo ou cet email est d√©j√† pris' }, { status: 409 });
    }
    // On renvoie le VRAI message d'erreur pour le d√©bogage
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message || 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/challenges/[id]/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  try {
    const challenge = await pool.query("SELECT * FROM challenges WHERE id = $1", [id]);
    if (challenge.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    const entries = await pool.query(`
      SELECT e.id as entry_id, s.id as seal_id, s.title, s.description, s.file_path, s.category, 
      CASE WHEN $2 = true THEN 'Anonyme' ELSE u.handle END as author,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM challenge_votes WHERE entry_id = e.id) as votes
      FROM challenge_entries e
      JOIN seals s ON e.seal_id = s.id
      JOIN users u ON e.user_id = u.id
      WHERE e.challenge_id = $1
      ORDER BY votes DESC
    `, [id, challenge.rows[0].is_anonymous]);

    return NextResponse.json({ challenge: challenge.rows[0], entries: entries.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/challenges/create/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { title, description, pubkey, is_anonymous } = await req.json();
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    // Date limite dans 7 jours par d√©faut
    const deadline = new Date();
    deadline.setDate(deadline.getDate() + 7);

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO challenges (title, description, creator_id, deadline, is_anonymous) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)",
      [title, description, user.rows[0].id, deadline, is_anonymous]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT c.id, c.title, c.deadline, c.is_anonymous, u.handle as creator,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM challenge_entries WHERE challenge_id = c.id) as entries_count
      FROM challenges c JOIN users u ON c.creator_id = u.id
      ORDER BY c.created_at DESC
    `);
    return NextResponse.json({ challenges: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/challenges/vote/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { challenge_id, entry_id, pubkey } = await req.json();
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const voterId = user.rows[0].id;

    // V√©rifier qu'il ne vote pas pour lui-m√™me
    const entry = await pool.query("SELECT user_id FROM challenge_entries WHERE id = $1", [entry_id]);
    if (entry.rows.length > 0 && entry.rows[0].user_id === voterId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Tu ne peux pas voter pour ta propre ≈ìuvre !' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO challenge_votes (challenge_id, voter_id, entry_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",
      [challenge_id, voterId, entry_id]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

--- FICHIER : src/app/api/comments/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

// Lire les commentaires d'une ≈ìuvre
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const sealId = searchParams.get('seal_id');
  if (!sealId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'ID manquant' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT c.id, c.content, c.created_at, u.handle as author
      FROM comments c JOIN users u ON c.user_id = u.id
      WHERE c.seal_id = $1 ORDER BY c.created_at ASC
    `, [sealId]);
    return NextResponse.json({ comments: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

// Ajouter un commentaire
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, content } = await req.json();
  if (!seal_id || !pubkey || !content) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const myId = user.rows[0].id;

    await pool.query("INSERT INTO comments (seal_id, user_id, content) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [seal_id, myId, content]);

    // Cr√©er une notification pour l'auteur de l'≈ìuvre
    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id, title FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [seal_id]);
    if (seal.rows.length > 0 && seal.rows[0].author_id !== myId) {
      await pool.query(
        "INSERT INTO notifications (user_id, actor_id, type, message, link) VALUES ($1, $2, 'comment', $3, $4)",
        [seal.rows[0].author_id, myId, `a comment√© ton ≈ìuvre "${seal.rows[0].title}"`, `/o/${seal_id}`]
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/favorites/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cl√© manquante' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ favorites: [] });
    
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.category, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM favorites f
      JOIN seals s ON f.seal_id = s.id
      JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE f.user_id = $1 ORDER BY f.created_at DESC
    `, [user.rows[0].id]);
    return NextResponse.json({ favorites: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ favorites: [] }); }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, action } = await req.json(); // action: 'add' | 'remove'
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    if (action === 'remove') {
      await pool.query("DELETE FROM favorites WHERE user_id = $1 AND seal_id = $2", [user.rows[0].id, seal_id]);
    } else {
      await pool.query("INSERT INTO favorites (user_id, seal_id) VALUES ($1, $2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [user.rows[0].id, seal_id]);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/feed/friends/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');

  if (!myHandle) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });

  try {
    // 1. On trouve l'ID de l'utilisateur
    const me = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
    if (me.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });
    const myId = me.rows[0].id;

    // 2. On trouve les gens avec qui j'ai discut√© (mes contacts)
    const contactsQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT DISTINCT receiver_id as contact_id FROM messages WHERE sender_id = $1
      UNION
      SELECT DISTINCT sender_id as contact_id FROM messages WHERE receiver_id = $1
    `, [myId]);

    if (contactsQuery.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });
    const contactIds = contactsQuery.rows.map(r => r.contact_id);

    // 3. On r√©cup√®re leurs derni√®res ≈ìuvres publiques (5 max)
    const seals = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.author_id = ANY($1) AND s.is_public = TRUE
      ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 5
    `, [contactIds]);

    return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: seals.rows });
  } catch (error) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/feed/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');
  const category = searchParams.get('category');

  try {
    let params: any[] = [];
    let whereClause = "s.visibility = 'public'";

    if (category && category !== 'all') {
      params.push(category);
      whereClause += ` AND s.category = $${params.length}`;
    }

    if (myHandle) {
      const me = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
      if (me.rows.length > 0) {
        const myId = me.rows[0].id;
        const friendsRes = await pool.query(`
          SELECT requester_id as id FROM friendships WHERE addressee_id = $1 AND status = 'accepted'
          UNION
          SELECT addressee_id as id FROM friendships WHERE requester_id = $1 AND status = 'accepted'
        `, [myId]);
        const friendIds = friendsRes.rows.map(r => r.id);
        if (friendIds.length > 0) {
          params.push(friendIds);
          whereClause = `(s.visibility = 'public' OR (s.visibility = 'friends' AND s.author_id = ANY($${params.length})))`;
          if (category && category !== 'all') {
             whereClause += ` AND s.category = $${params.length - 1}`; // On garde le filtre cat√©gorie
          }
        }
      }
    }

    const sql = `
      SELECT s.id, 
        CASE WHEN s.is_mystery = true THEN 'üîí Sceau Myst√®re' ELSE s.title END as title,
        CASE WHEN s.is_mystery = true AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id) < s.unlock_likes THEN null ELSE s.file_path END as file_path,
        s.is_mystery, s.unlock_likes, s.is_flash, s.ots_proof,
        s.description, s.category, s.timestamp, s.visibility, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author,
        s.project_id, s.version_number,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'like') as likes_like,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'wow') as likes_wow,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'inspired') as likes_inspired
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE ${whereClause} ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 30
    `;
    
    const result = await pool.query(sql, params);
    return NextResponse.json({ seals: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/feed/trends/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    // On r√©cup√®re les ≈ìuvres publiques de la semaine, tri√©es par nombre de likes
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.category, s.timestamp, s.location, u.handle as author,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id) as likes_count
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.is_public = TRUE AND s.timestamp >= NOW() - INTERVAL '7 days'
      ORDER BY likes_count DESC, s.timestamp DESC
      LIMIT 3
    `);
    return NextResponse.json({ trends: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/friends/respond/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { me, target, action } = await req.json(); // action: 'accept' | 'reject'
  try {
    const meId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0].id;
    const targetId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [target])).rows[0].id;

    if (action === 'accept') {
      await pool.query(`
        UPDATE friendships SET status = 'accepted' 
        WHERE requester_id = $1 AND addressee_id = $2
      `, [targetId, meId]);
    } else {
      await pool.query(`
        DELETE FROM friendships WHERE requester_id = $1 AND addressee_id = $2
      `, [targetId, meId]);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/friends/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    let me = searchParams.get('me');
    const search = searchParams.get('search');
    
    if (!me) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non connect√©' }, { status: 401 });
    // NOUVEAU : On nettoie le pseudo au cas o√π il y aurait un @
    me = me.replace('@', '');

    const myIdRes = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me]);
    if (myIdRes.rows.length === 0) {
      // Message plus clair pour le d√©bogage
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Ton compte n'existe pas dans la base de donn√©es. D√©connecte-toi et reconnecte-toi." }, { status: 404 });
    }
    const myId = myIdRes.rows[0].id;

    if (search) {
      const users = await pool.query(`
        SELECT handle, role FROM users 
        WHERE handle ILIKE $1 AND id != $2 LIMIT 10
      `, [`%${search.replace('@', '')}%`, myId]);
      return NextResponse.json({ searchResults: users.rows });
    }

    const friendsQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT 
        u.id, 
        u.handle,
        (SELECT title FROM seals WHERE author_id = u.id AND visibility = 'public' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_seal,
        (SELECT id FROM seals WHERE author_id = u.id AND visibility = 'public' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_seal_id
      FROM friendships f
      JOIN users u ON u.id = CASE WHEN f.requester_id = $1 THEN f.addressee_id ELSE f.requester_id END
      WHERE (f.requester_id = $1 OR f.addressee_id = $1) AND f.status = 'accepted'
    `, [myId]);

    const receivedQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT u.handle FROM friendships f 
      JOIN users u ON u.id = f.requester_id
      WHERE f.addressee_id = $1 AND f.status = 'pending'
    `, [myId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      friends: friendsQuery.rows, 
      received: receivedQuery.rows 
    });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Erreur API Friends:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Erreur base de donn√©es : " + error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { me, target } = await req.json();
    
    // Nettoyage des pseudos
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me.replace('@', '')])).rows[0]?.id;
    const targetId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [target.replace('@', '')])).rows[0]?.id;
    
    if (!myId || !targetId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur introuvable dans la base' }, { status: 404 });

    await pool.query(`
      INSERT INTO friendships (requester_id, addressee_id, status) 
      VALUES ($1, $2, 'pending') ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
    `, [myId, targetId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error: any) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/github/sync/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { pubkey, github_username } = await req.json();
    
    // 1. Sauvegarder le pseudo GitHub
    await pool.query("UPDATE users SET github_username = $1 WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $2", [github_username, pubkey]);
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;

    // 2. R√©cup√©rer les derniers commits publics via l'API GitHub
    const ghRes = await fetch(`https://api.github.com/users/${github_username}/events/public`, {
      headers: { 'User-Agent': 'SCEAU-App' }
    });
    const events = await ghRes.json();
    
    let commitsScelles = 0;
    for (const event of events) {
      if (event.type === 'PushEvent' && event.payload.commits.length > 0) {
        for (const commit of event.payload.commits) {
          // On cr√©e un hash bas√© sur le message et l'ID du commit
          const hashStr = `${commit.sha}-${commit.message}`;
          const hash = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(hashStr));
          let hashB64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...hash));

          // On ins√®re dans la base (ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING pour √©viter les doublons)
          await pool.query(
            `INSERT INTO seals (hash, timestamp, author_id, title, category, work_type, is_public) 
             VALUES (decode($1, 'base64'), $2, $3, $4, 'Code', 'commit', true) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`,
            [hashB64, event.created_at, userId, `Commit: ${commit.message.slice(0, 50)}`]
          );
          commitsScelles++;
        }
      }
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, commitsScelles });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur GitHub' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/groups/create/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { name, creator_pubkey } = await req.json();
  try {
    const creator = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [creator_pubkey]);
    if (creator.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const creatorId = creator.rows[0].id;

    const group = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO groups (name, creator_id) VALUES ($1, $2) RETURNING id",
      [name, creatorId]
    );
    const groupId = group.rows[0].id;

    // Le cr√©ateur est automatiquement membre
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO group_members (group_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)", [groupId, creatorId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ group_id: groupId });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/groups/list/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const me = searchParams.get('me');
  if (!me) return NextResponse.json({ groups: [] });

  try {
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0]?.id;
    if (!myId) return NextResponse.json({ groups: [] });

    const groups = await pool.query(`
      SELECT g.id, g.name, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM group_members WHERE group_id = g.id) as members_count
      FROM groups g
      JOIN group_members gm ON g.id = gm.group_id
      WHERE gm.user_id = $1 ORDER BY g.created_at DESC
    `, [myId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ groups: groups.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const groupId = searchParams.get('id');
  if (!groupId) return NextResponse.json({ messages: [] });

  try {
    const messages = await pool.query(`
      SELECT m.content, m.created_at, u.handle as sender
      FROM group_messages m JOIN users u ON m.sender_id = u.id
      WHERE m.group_id = $1 ORDER BY m.created_at ASC
    `, [groupId]);
    return NextResponse.json({ messages: messages.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO group_messages (group_id, sender_id, content) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)",
      [group_id, senderId, content]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/like/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, reaction_type } = await req.json();
  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
  const myId = user.rows[0].id;

  try {
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO likes (seal_id, user_id, reaction_type) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [seal_id, myId, reaction_type || 'like']);
    
    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id, title FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [seal_id]);
    if (seal.rows.length > 0 && seal.rows[0].author_id !== myId) {
      await pool.query(
        "INSERT INTO notifications (user_id, actor_id, type, message, link) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)",
        [seal.rows[0].author_id, myId, 'like', `a r√©agi √† ton ≈ìuvre "${seal.rows[0].title}"`, `/o/${seal_id}`]
      );
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (e) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/messages/[handle]/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ handle: string }> }) {
  const { handle } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');

  try {
    const myIdResult = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
    const otherIdResult = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [handle]);
    
    if (myIdResult.rows.length === 0 || otherIdResult.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 400 });
    
    const myId = myIdResult.rows[0].id;
    const otherId = otherIdResult.rows[0].id;

    const messages = await pool.query(`
      SELECT m.content, m.created_at, u.handle as sender
      FROM messages m
      JOIN users u ON m.sender_id = u.id
      WHERE (m.sender_id = $1 AND m.receiver_id = $2) OR (m.sender_id = $2 AND m.receiver_id = $1)
      ORDER BY m.created_at ASC
    `, [myId, otherId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ messages: messages.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/messages/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  try {
    const users = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users ORDER BY handle ASC");
    return NextResponse.json({ contacts: users.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { sender_handle, receiver_handle, content } = await req.json();
    if (!sender_handle || !receiver_handle || !content) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const sender = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [sender_handle]);
    const receiver = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [receiver_handle]);
    
    if (sender.rows.length === 0 || receiver.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur inconnu' }, { status: 404 });
    }

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO messages (sender_id, receiver_id, content) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)",
      [sender.rows[0].id, receiver.rows[0].id, content]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error: any) { 
    console.error("Erreur DB message:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { me } = await req.json();
  try {
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0]?.id;
    if (myId) await pool.query("UPDATE notifications SET is_read = true WHERE user_id = $1", [myId]);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/notifications/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const me = searchParams.get('me');
  if (!me) return NextResponse.json({ notifications: [], unread: 0 });

  try {
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0]?.id;
    if (!myId) return NextResponse.json({ notifications: [], unread: 0 });

    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT n.id, n.type, n.message, n.link, n.is_read, n.created_at, u.handle as actor
      FROM notifications n JOIN users u ON n.actor_id = u.id
      WHERE n.user_id = $1 ORDER BY n.created_at DESC LIMIT 20
    `, [myId]);

    const unread = result.rows.filter(n => !n.is_read).length;
    return NextResponse.json({ notifications: result.rows, unread });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ notifications: [], unread: 0 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/o/[id]/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const viewerPubkey = searchParams.get('viewer');

  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.category, s.timestamp,
             s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.ots_proof,
             encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.id = $1 AND s.visibility = 'public'
    `, [id]);

    if (result.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    if (viewerPubkey) {
      const viewer = await pool.query(
        "SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1",
        [viewerPubkey]
      );
      if (viewer.rows.length > 0) {
        await pool.query(
          "INSERT INTO access_log (seal_id, viewer_id, action, context) VALUES ($1, $2, 'view', 'public')",
          [id, viewer.rows[0].id]
        );
      }
    }

    const { ots_proof, ...seal } = result.rows[0];
    return NextResponse.json({ seal: { ...seal, has_ots_proof: !!ots_proof } });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/pacts/[id]/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const pact = await pool.query(`
    SELECT p.*, u.handle as creator, encode(p.hash, 'base64') as hash_str
    FROM pacts p JOIN users u ON p.creator_id = u.id 
    WHERE p.id = $1
  `, [id]);
  
  if (pact.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

  const signatures = await pool.query("SELECT u.handle FROM pact_signatures s JOIN users u ON s.user_id = u.id WHERE s.pact_id = $1", [id]);
  
  return NextResponse.json({ 
    ...pact.rows[0], 
    signataires: signatures.rows.map(r => r.handle) 
  });
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/pacts/create/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { PutObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const data = await req.formData();
    const title = data.get('title') as string;
    const description = data.get('description') as string;
    const pubkey = data.get('pubkey') as string;
    const groupId = data.get('group_id') as string;
    const requiredSignatures = parseInt(data.get('required_signatures') as string || '2');
    
    // Nouveaux champs
    const hash = data.get('hash') as string;
    const category = data.get('category') as string || 'Art';
    const isHosted = data.get('isHosted') === 'true';
    const file = data.get('file') as File;

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const creatorId = user.rows[0].id;

    let filePath = null;
    if (isHosted && file) {
      const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const filename = `${Date.now()}-${file.name}`;
      await r2Client.send(new PutObjectCommand({ Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET, Key: filename, Body: buffer, ContentType: file.type }));
      filePath = `${process.env.AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3}/${process.env.S3_BUCKET}/${filename}`;
    }

    const pact = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO pacts (title, description, creator_id, group_id, required_signatures, hash, file_path, is_hosted, category) 
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, decode($6, 'base64'), $7, $8, $9) RETURNING id`,
      [title, description, creatorId, groupId || null, requiredSignatures, hash, filePath, isHosted, category]
    );
    
    // Le cr√©ateur signe automatiquement
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO pact_signatures (pact_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)", [pact.rows[0].id, creatorId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ id: pact.rows[0].id, title });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/pacts/sign/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import { submitToOts } from '@/lib/ots';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { pact_id, pubkey } = await req.json();
  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO pact_signatures (pact_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",
      [pact_id, user.rows[0].id]
    );

    const pact = await pool.query(
      "SELECT title, description, required_signatures, status FROM pacts WHERE id = $1",
      [pact_id]
    );
    const sigs = await pool.query(
      `SELECT u.handle, s.created_at FROM pact_signatures s JOIN users u ON s.user_id = u.id
       WHERE s.pact_id = $1 ORDER BY s.created_at ASC`,
      [pact_id]
    );

    const required = pact.rows[0].required_signatures;
    const current = sigs.rows.length;

    if (current >= required && pact.rows[0].status !== 'scelle') {
      const canonical = JSON.stringify({
        title: pact.rows[0].title,
        description: pact.rows[0].description || '',
        signers: sigs.rows.map((r: any) => ({ handle: r.handle, signed_at: new Date(r.created_at).toISOString() })),
      });
      const pactHash = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(canonical));

      let otsProof: string | null = null;
      try {
        const ots = await submitToOts(new Uint8Array(pactHash));
        if (ots.ok && ots.proof) otsProof = ots.proof.toString('base64');
      } catch (e) { console.error('[Pact OTS]', e); }

      await pool.query(
        `UPDATE pacts SET status='scelle', scelle_at=NOW(), pact_hash=$1, ots_proof=$2 WHERE id=$3`,
        [Buffer.from(pactHash), otsProof, pact_id]
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, current, required });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/profil/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cl√© manquante' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle, bio, role, is_pro FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 404 });
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;
    const isPro = user.rows[0].is_pro || false;

    const stats = await pool.query(`
      SELECT COUNT(id) as total_sealed, SUM(CASE WHEN is_hosted = true THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as total_hosted
      FROM seals WHERE author_id = $1
    `, [userId]);

    const seals = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.timestamp, s.visibility, s.is_flash, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id) as likes_count,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM comments WHERE seal_id = s.id) as comments_count
      FROM seals s WHERE s.author_id = $1 ORDER BY s.created_at DESC
    `, [userId]);

    const pacts = await pool.query("SELECT COUNT(*) as total_pacts FROM pact_signatures WHERE user_id = $1", [userId]);

    const totalSealed = parseInt(stats.rows[0].total_sealed || 0);
    const totalPacts = parseInt(pacts.rows[0].total_pacts || 0);

    // --- CALCUL DU STREAK (version propre) ---
    const uniqueDatesSet = new Set(
      seals.rows.map((s: any) => new Date(s.timestamp).toISOString().split('T')[0])
    );
    let streak = 0;
    let cursor = new Date();
    cursor.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    if (!uniqueDatesSet.has(cursor.toISOString().split('T')[0])) {
      cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
    }
    while (uniqueDatesSet.has(cursor.toISOString().split('T')[0])) {
      streak++;
      cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
    }

    // --- FAVORIS ---
    const favorites = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.category, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM favorites f JOIN seals s ON f.seal_id = s.id JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE f.user_id = $1 ORDER BY f.created_at DESC
    `, [userId]);

    // --- BADGES ---
    const badges = [];
    if (totalSealed >= 1) badges.push({ icon: 'üå±', name: 'Apprenti', desc: 'Premi√®re ≈ìuvre scell√©e' });
    if (totalSealed >= 10) badges.push({ icon: 'ü•â', name: 'Archiviste', desc: '10 ≈ìuvres scell√©es' });
    if (totalSealed >= 50) badges.push({ icon: 'ü•á', name: 'Gardien', desc: '50 ≈ìuvres scell√©es' });
    if (isPro) badges.push({ icon: 'üëë', name: 'PRO', desc: 'Compte v√©rifi√© PRO' });
    if (streak >= 3) badges.push({ icon: 'üî•', name: 'En Feu', desc: '3 jours cons√©cutifs' });
    if (totalPacts >= 1) badges.push({ icon: 'ü§ù', name: 'N√©gociateur', desc: 'A sign√© un pacte' });

    return NextResponse.json({
      handle: user.rows[0].handle,
      bio: user.rows[0].bio,
      role: user.rows[0].role,
      is_pro: isPro,
      streak: streak,
      favorites: favorites.rows,
      sealed: totalSealed,
      hosted: parseInt(stats.rows[0].total_hosted || 0),
      pacts: totalPacts,
      badges: badges,
      seals: seals.rows
    });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/profil/update/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { pubkey, old_handle, new_handle, bio, role } = await req.json();
    
    // 1. V√©rifier que l'utilisateur existe bien
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;

    // 2. Si le pseudo change, v√©rifier qu'il n'est pas d√©j√† pris
    if (new_handle && new_handle !== old_handle) {
      const check = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [new_handle]);
      if (check.rows.length > 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ce pseudo est d√©j√† pris' }, { status: 409 });
    }

    // 3. Mettre √† jour
    const finalHandle = new_handle || old_handle;
    await pool.query(
      "UPDATE users SET handle = $1, bio = $2, role = $3 WHERE id = $4",
      [finalHandle, bio, role, userId]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, new_handle: finalHandle });
  } catch (error) { 
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/[id]/access-log/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');

  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cl√© manquante' }, { status: 401 });
  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

  const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [id]);
  if (seal.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
  if (seal.rows[0].author_id !== user.rows[0].id) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non autoris√©' }, { status: 403 });
  }

  const log = await pool.query(`
    SELECT a.action, a.context, a.created_at, u.handle as viewer_handle,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM seals WHERE author_id = u.id) as viewer_seals,
      u.created_at as viewer_account_created_at
    FROM access_log a JOIN users u ON a.viewer_id = u.id
    WHERE a.seal_id = $1 ORDER BY a.created_at DESC LIMIT 500
  `, [id]);

  return NextResponse.json({
    entries: log.rows.map((r: any) => ({
      action: r.action,
      context: r.context,
      at: r.created_at,
      viewer: r.viewer_handle,
      viewer_profile: {
        account_age_days: Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(r.viewer_account_created_at).getTime()) / 86400000),
        seals_count: parseInt(r.viewer_seals || 0),
      },
    })),
  });
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/[id]/ots/refresh/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { upgradeOts } from '@/lib/ots';

export async function POST(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  try {
    const res = await pool.query("SELECT hash FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [id]);
    if (res.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    const upgraded = await upgradeOts(new Uint8Array(res.rows[0].hash));
    if (!upgraded) {
      return NextResponse.json({ status: 'pending', message: 'Pas encore confirm√©. R√©essaie dans quelques heures.' });
    }
    await pool.query(
      "UPDATE seals SET ots_proof = $1, ots_upgraded_at = NOW() WHERE id = $2",
      [upgraded.toString('base64'), id]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ status: 'upgraded', size: upgraded.length });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/[id]/ots/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  try {
    const res = await pool.query("SELECT ots_proof, title FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [id]);
    if (res.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    const row = res.rows[0];
    if (!row.ots_proof) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Pas de preuve .ots' }, { status: 404 });

    const buffer = Buffer.from(row.ots_proof, 'base64');
    const safeTitle = (row.title || 'sceau').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '-').slice(0, 40);
    return new NextResponse(buffer, {
      status: 200,
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream',
        'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="${safeTitle}.ots"`,
      },
    });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/delete/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { DeleteObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';

export async function DELETE(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const sealId = searchParams.get('id');
    const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');

    if (!sealId || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'ID ou cl√© manquant' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    
    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id, file_path FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [sealId]);
    if (seal.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Sceau introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    
    if (seal.rows[0].author_id !== user.rows[0].id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non autoris√©' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    // CORRECTION : On supprime le fichier de Cloudflare R2, pas du disque local
    if (seal.rows[0].file_path) {
      try {
        const filename = seal.rows[0].file_path.split('/').pop();
        await r2Client.send(new DeleteObjectCommand({
          Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET,
          Key: filename
        }));
      } catch (e) { console.log("Fichier introuvable sur R2 ou d√©j√† supprim√©"); }
    }

    await pool.query("DELETE FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [sealId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { PutObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { submitToOts } from '@/lib/ots';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const data = await req.formData();
    const hash = data.get('hash') as string;
    const title = data.get('title') as string;
    const description = data.get('description') as string;
    const isFlash = data.get('isFlash') === 'true';
    const isMystery = data.get('isMystery') === 'true';
    const isEphemeral = data.get('isEphemeral') === 'true';
    const unlockLikes = parseInt(data.get('unlockLikes') as string || '0');
    const pubkey = data.get('pubkey') as string;
    const file = data.get('file') as File;

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const authorId = user.rows[0].id;

    let filePath: string | null = null;
    let fileSize = 0;
    let otsProof: string | null = null;
    let otsCalendars: string[] = [];

    try {
      const hashBytes = new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(hash, 'base64'));
      const ots = await submitToOts(hashBytes);
      if (ots.ok && ots.proof) {
        otsProof = ots.proof.toString('base64');
        otsCalendars = ots.calendars;
        console.log(`[OTS] ancr√© via ${ots.calendars.length} calendar(s)`);
      } else {
        console.warn('[OTS] √©chec :', ots.error);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[OTS] exception :', e);
    }

    if (!isFlash && file) {
      const quotaCheck = await pool.query(
        "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(file_size), 0) as used FROM seals WHERE author_id = $1 AND is_hosted = true",
        [authorId]
      );
      const currentUsage = parseInt(quotaCheck.rows[0].used);
      if (currentUsage + file.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Quota de 50 Mo d√©pass√©.' }, { status: 403 });
      }

      fileSize = file.size;
      const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const filename = `${hash.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 20)}-${file.name}`;
      await r2Client.send(new PutObjectCommand({
        Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET,
        Key: filename,
        Body: buffer,
        ContentType: file.type,
      }));
      filePath = `${process.env.AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3}/${process.env.S3_BUCKET}/${filename}`;
    }

    const result = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO seals (
        hash, timestamp, author_id, title, description, is_public, visibility,
        is_hosted, file_path, file_size, is_flash, is_mystery, unlock_likes, is_ephemeral,
        ots_proof, ots_submitted_at
      ) VALUES (
        decode($1, 'base64'), NOW(), $2, $3, $4, true, 'public',
        $5, $6, $7, $8, $9, $10, $11, $12, $13
      ) RETURNING id`,
      [hash, authorId, title, description,
       !isFlash && filePath ? true : false, filePath, fileSize,
       isFlash, isMystery, unlockLikes, isEphemeral,
       otsProof, otsProof ? new Date() : null]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({
      seal_id: result.rows[0].id,
      ots_secured: otsProof !== null,
      ots_calendars: otsCalendars,
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message || 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/update-visibility/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { seal_id, pubkey, visibility } = await req.json();

    if (!seal_id || !pubkey || !visibility) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // V√©rifier que l'utilisateur est bien le propri√©taire
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [seal_id]);
    if (seal.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Sceau introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    
    if (seal.rows[0].author_id !== user.rows[0].id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non autoris√©' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    // Mettre √† jour la visibilit√© (et is_public pour la compatibilit√©)
    const isPublic = visibility === 'public';
    await pool.query(
      "UPDATE seals SET visibility = $1, is_public = $2 WHERE id = $3",
      [visibility, isPublic, seal_id]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/signup/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { handle, pubkey } = await req.json();

    // 1. V√©rification des donn√©es
    if (!handle || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Pseudo et cl√© requis' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 2. Insertion
    const result = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO users (handle, pubkey) VALUES ($1, decode($2, 'base64')) RETURNING id, handle", 
      [handle, pubkey]
    );
    
    return NextResponse.json(result.rows[0]);

  } catch (error: any) {
    // Si le pseudo existe d√©j√† (Code PostgreSQL 23505 = violation d'unicit√©)
    if (error.code === '23505') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ce pseudo est d√©j√† pris' }, { status: 409 });
    }
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur lors de l\'inscription' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/user-profile/[handle]/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ handle: string }> }) {
  const { handle } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const viewerHandle = searchParams.get('viewer');

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [handle]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    const targetUserId = user.rows[0].id;

    let relationship = 'none';
    let viewerId = null;

    if (viewerHandle) {
      const viewer = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [viewerHandle]);
      if (viewer.rows.length > 0) {
        viewerId = viewer.rows[0].id;
        const friend = await pool.query(
          "SELECT status FROM friendships WHERE (requester_id = $1 AND addressee_id = $2) OR (requester_id = $2 AND addressee_id = $1)",
          [viewerId, targetUserId]
        );
        if (friend.rows.length > 0) {
          if (friend.rows[0].status === 'accepted') relationship = 'friend';
          else relationship = 'pending';
        }
      }
    }

    let visFilter = "s.visibility = 'public'";
    if (viewerId === targetUserId) visFilter = "1=1"; 
    else if (relationship === 'friend') visFilter = "s.visibility IN ('public', 'friends')";

    const seals = await pool.query(`
      SELECT id, title, description, timestamp, is_hosted, file_path, visibility 
      FROM seals s WHERE author_id = $1 AND ${visFilter} ORDER BY created_at DESC
    `, [targetUserId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ handle: user.rows[0].handle, relationship, seals: seals.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/verify/pulse/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    // On r√©cup√®re les 15 derniers sceaux publics
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.category, s.timestamp, s.location, u.handle as author
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.is_public = TRUE
      ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 15
    `);
    return NextResponse.json({ pulse: result.rows });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/verify/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { hash, query } = await req.json();
    
    // 1. Recherche par Hash (V√©rification classique)
    if (hash) {
      const result = await pool.query(`
        SELECT s.title, s.timestamp, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, u.handle as author
        FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
        WHERE encode(s.hash, 'base64') = $1
        ORDER BY s.timestamp ASC
      `, [hash]);
      if (result.rows.length > 0) return NextResponse.json({ found: true, seals: result.rows });
      return NextResponse.json({ found: false });
    }

    // 2. Recherche par Titre (D√©couverte publique)
    if (query) {
      const result = await pool.query(`
        SELECT s.title, s.timestamp, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author
        FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
        WHERE s.title ILIKE $1 AND s.is_public = TRUE
        ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 20
      `, [`%${query}%`]);
      
      return NextResponse.json({ found: result.rows.length > 0, seals: result.rows });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Requ√™te invalide' }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
