'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function ChallengeDetail() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const myPubkey = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey') : null;

  const load = () => {
    if (params.id) {
      fetch(`/api/challenges/${params.id}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
        if (d.error) setError(d.error); else setData(d);
      });
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [params.id]);

  async function vote(entryId: string) {
    if (!myPubkey) return alert("Connecte-toi pour voter !");
    const res = await fetch('/api/challenges/vote', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ challenge_id: params.id, entry_id: entryId, pubkey: myPubkey })
    });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) alert(d.error);
    else { alert("Vote enregistr√© !"); load(); }
  }

  if (error) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-red-500 font-bold">{error}</div>;
  if (!data) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement du d√©fi...</div>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif">
      <Link href="/challenges" className="text-sm font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-4 inline-block hover:underline">‚Üê Retour aux d√©fis</Link>
      
      <div className="text-center mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{data.challenge.title}</h1>
        <p className="text-sm italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">{data.challenge.description}</p>
        <div className="mt-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">
          Fin le {new Date(data.challenge.deadline).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4">
        {data.entries.map((entry: any) => (
          <div key={entry.entry_id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-4 rounded-xl flex justify-between items-center shadow-sm">
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-bold text-lg" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{entry.title}</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{entry.author}</p>
              {entry.file_path && (
                 <a href={entry.file_path} target="_blank" className="text-xs text-[#4A90C2] font-bold hover:underline mt-2 block">Voir l'≈ìuvre ‚Üó</a>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="text-center">
              <button onClick={() => vote(entry.entry_id)} className="bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] border border-[#F4D0A8] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-[#FCE0D6] transition">
                Voter
              </button>
              <div className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2">{entry.votes} votes</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
        {data.entries.length === 0 && <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Personne n'a encore particip√©.</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Challenges() {
  const [challenges, setChallenges] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState('');
  const [isAnon, setIsAnon] = useState(false);

  const load = () => {
    fetch('/api/challenges/list').then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
      setChallenges(d.challenges || []);
      setLoading(false);
    });
  };

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  async function createChallenge() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return alert("Connecte-toi !");
    if (!title) return alert("Titre requis");

    const res = await fetch('/api/challenges/create', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ title, description: desc, pubkey, is_anonymous: isAnon })
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      setShowModal(false); setTitle(''); setDesc(''); load();
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üèÜ D√©fis de Preuve.</h1>
        <button onClick={() => setShowModal(true)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-md">+ Cr√©er un d√©fi</button>
      </div>

      {loading ? <p className="text-center font-mono">Chargement...</p> : (
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          {challenges.map(c => (
            <Link href={`/challenges/${c.id}`} key={c.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition block">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{c.title}</h2>
                {c.is_anonymous && <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 px-2 py-1 rounded">Anonyme</span>}
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">Lanc√© par @{c.creator}</p>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs font-mono text-[#A89070]">
                <span>üë• {c.entries_count} participations</span>
                <span>‚è± Fin le {new Date(c.deadline).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</span>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
          {challenges.length === 0 && <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Aucun d√©fi en cours.</p>}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modale de cr√©ation */}
      {showModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cr√©er un D√©fi</h2>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Titre (ex: Meilleur design de l'√©t√©)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={title} onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea placeholder="Description du d√©fi..." className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={desc} onChange={e => setDesc(e.target.value)} />
            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isAnon} onChange={e => setIsAnon(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cacher les noms (Vote √† l'aveugle)</span>
            </label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={createChallenge} className="flex-1 bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Lancer</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Feed() {
  const [seals, setSeals] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [selectedSeal, setSelectedSeal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('all');
  
  const [attachments, setAttachments] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newAttachment, setNewAttachment] = useState('');
  const [attachmentType, setAttachmentType] = useState('text'); 
  
  const myHandle = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle') : null;
  const myPubkey = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey') : null;

  const loadFeed = () => {
    setLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/feed?me=${myHandle || ''}&category=${activeCategory}`)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(data => { setSeals(data.seals || []); setLoading(false); });
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadFeed(); }, [myHandle, activeCategory]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedSeal) {
      fetch(`/api/attachments?seal_id=${selectedSeal.id}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => setAttachments(d.attachments || []));
    }
  }, [selectedSeal]);

  async function submitAttachment() {
    if (!newAttachment.trim() || !myPubkey || !selectedSeal) return;
    
    const payload = {
      seal_id: selectedSeal.id,
      pubkey: myPubkey,
      content: attachmentType === 'text' ? newAttachment : "Regarde √ßa :",
      type: attachmentType,
      media_url: attachmentType === 'link' ? newAttachment : null
    };

    await fetch('/api/attachments', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    
    setNewAttachment('');
    fetch(`/api/attachments?seal_id=${selectedSeal.id}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setAttachments(d.attachments || []));
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen p-6 font-serif max-w-3xl mx-auto relative">
      <button onClick={() => window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })} className="fixed bottom-24 right-6 bg-[#2C1810] text-white w-10 h-10 rounded-full shadow-lg flex items-center justify-center z-40 hover:bg-black transition" title="Retour en haut">‚Üë</button>
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-black" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51] italic">.</span></h1>
        <Link href="/seal" className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-md">+ Sceller</Link>
      </div>

      {/* FILTRES DE CAT√âGORIES */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-4 mb-2 snap-x">
        {['all', 'Art', 'Musique', 'Code', '√âcrit'].map(cat => (
          <button 
            key={cat} 
            onClick={() => setActiveCategory(cat)}
            className={`snap-start shrink-0 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition border ${activeCategory === cat ? 'bg-[#E76F51] text-white border-[#E76F51] shadow-md' : 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] text-[var(--text-muted)] border-[var(--border-color)] hover:border-[#E76F51]'}`}
          >
            {cat === 'all' ? 'üåç Tout' : cat}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {loading ? (
         <p className="text-center font-mono py-10">Connexion...</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-6 mb-10">
          {seals.map((seal) => (
            <div key={seal.id} onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(seal)} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">

                {seal.ots_proof && <span title="Ancr√© sur Bitcoin" className="text-lg">‚õìÔ∏è</span>}
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2">Par @{seal.author} ‚Ä¢ {seal.category}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
          {seals.length === 0 && <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Aucune ≈ìuvre dans cette cat√©gorie pour le moment.</p>}
        </div>
      )}

      {selectedSeal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(null)}>
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-lg rounded-3xl relative max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            
            <div className="p-6 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-start">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{selectedSeal.title}</h2>

              </div>

                  <div className="mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-[#F5F9EE] border border-[#A8C77E] text-[#5D7A3A] px-2 py-1 rounded text-[0.6rem] font-bold">

                )}

              <div className="space-y-3 mb-6">
                {attachments.map((a, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">

                        üîó Voir le lien

                    ) : (

                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
                {attachments.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Sois le premier √† r√©agir √† cette ≈ìuvre.</p>}
              </div>
            </div>

            {myHandle && (
              <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] bg-[var(--bg-surface)]">
                <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-lg mb-2">
                  <button onClick={() => setAttachmentType('text')} className={`flex-1 py-1 text-xs font-bold rounded ${attachmentType === 'text' ? 'bg-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üí¨ Texte</button>
                  <button onClick={() => setAttachmentType('link')} className={`flex-1 py-1 text-xs font-bold rounded ${attachmentType === 'link' ? 'bg-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üîó Lien</button>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input type="text" value={newAttachment} onChange={e => setNewAttachment(e.target.value)} placeholder={attachmentType === 'text' ? "Ajouter un commentaire..." : "Coller un lien (https://...)"} className="flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && submitAttachment()} />
                  <button onClick={submitAttachment} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 rounded-xl font-bold">Publier</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/forgot-password/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [link, setLink] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  async function handleReset() {
    setLink(''); setError('');
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/forgot-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ email }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) setLink(data.link);
    else setError(data.error);
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[#FFFCF7] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[#EFE0CA] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4 font-serif">Mot de passe oubli√©</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-[#7A5A3A] mb-6">Entre ton email pour recevoir un lien de r√©initialisation.</p>
      
      <input type="email" placeholder="Ton email" className="w-full border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4" value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={handleReset} className="w-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold mb-4">Envoyer le lien</button>
      
      {link && (
        <div className="bg-[#F5F9EE] border border-[#7A9B5A] p-4 rounded-xl text-sm break-all">
          <p className="font-bold text-[#5D7A3A] mb-2">‚úÖ (Mode Test) Voici ton lien :</p>
          <a href={link} className="text-blue-600 underline">{link}</a>
        </div>
      )}
      {error && <p className="text-red-500 font-bold text-center">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/friends/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Friends() {
  // √âtat pour basculer entre "Amis" et "D√©fis"
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'network' | 'challenges'>('network');

  // √âtats pour les Amis
  const [friendsData, setFriendsData] = useState<any>({ friends: [], received: [] });
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [friendsLoading, setFriendsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [friendsError, setFriendsError] = useState('');
  
  // √âtats pour les D√©fis
  const [challenges, setChallenges] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [challengesLoading, setChallengesLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showChallengeModal, setShowChallengeModal] = useState(false);
  const [challengeTitle, setChallengeTitle] = useState('');
  const [challengeDesc, setChallengeDesc] = useState('');
  const [challengeAnon, setChallengeAnon] = useState(false);

  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
    setMyHandle(h ? h.replace('@', '') : null);
  }, []);

  // --- LOGIQUE AMIS ---
  const loadFriends = () => {
    if (!myHandle) return;
    setFriendsLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/friends?me=${myHandle}`)
      .then(async (r) => {
        const d = await r.json();
        if (!r.ok) throw new Error(d.error || 'Erreur de chargement');
        return d;
      })
      .then(d => { 
        setFriendsData({ friends: d.friends || [], received: d.received || [] }); 
        setFriendsLoading(false);
      })
      .catch((err) => { 
        setFriendsError(err.message); 
        setFriendsLoading(false); 
      });
  };

  useEffect(() => { 
    if (myHandle && activeTab === 'network') loadFriends(); 
  }, [myHandle, activeTab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!searchQuery.trim() || !myHandle) { setSearchResults([]); return; }
    const delay = setTimeout(() => {
      fetch(`/api/friends?me=${myHandle}&search=${searchQuery}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => setSearchResults(d.searchResults || []))
        .catch(() => {});
    }, 500);
    return () => clearTimeout(delay);
  }, [searchQuery, myHandle]);

  async function respond(target: string, action: string) {
    await fetch('/api/friends/respond', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: myHandle, target, action }) });
    loadFriends();
  }

  async function addFriend(target: string) {
    await fetch('/api/friends', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: myHandle, target }) });
    alert(`Demande envoy√©e √† @${target}`);
    setSearchQuery('');
  }

  // --- LOGIQUE D√âFIS ---
  const loadChallenges = () => {
    setChallengesLoading(true);
    fetch('/api/challenges/list')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        setChallenges(d.challenges || []);
        setChallengesLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setChallengesLoading(false));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab === 'challenges') loadChallenges();
  }, [activeTab]);

  async function createChallenge() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return alert("Connecte-toi !");
    if (!challengeTitle) return alert("Titre requis");

    const res = await fetch('/api/challenges/create', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ title: challengeTitle, description: challengeDesc, pubkey, is_anonymous: challengeAnon })
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      setShowChallengeModal(false); 
      setChallengeTitle(''); 
      setChallengeDesc(''); 
      loadChallenges();
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif min-h-screen">
      
      {/* EN-T√äTE ET BOUTONS DE NAVIGATION */}
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Communaut√©.</h1>
        
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl">
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('network')} 
            className={`flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'network' ? 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}
          >
            üë• Le R√©seau
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('challenges')} 
            className={`flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'challenges' ? 'bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}
          >
            üèÜ Les D√©fis
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ========================================== */}
      {/* CONTENU : R√âSEAU (AMIS) */}
      {/* ========================================== */}
      {activeTab === 'network' && (
        <div className="animate-fade-in">
          {friendsError ? (
            <div className="text-center p-10 font-mono">
              <p className="text-red-500 font-bold mb-4">‚ùå {friendsError}</p>
              <button onClick={() => { localStorage.clear(); window.location.href = '/login'; }} className="bg-[#2C1810] text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Se reconnecter</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="mb-8 relative">
                <input 
                  type="text" 
                  placeholder="üîç Chercher un cr√©ateur par pseudo..." 
                  className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none bg-[var(--bg-surface)]"
                  style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
                  value={searchQuery}
                  onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                />
                {searchResults.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="mt-2 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-lg absolute w-full z-10">
                    {searchResults.map(u => (
                      <div key={u.handle} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)]">
                        <div>
                          <Link href={`/u/${u.handle}`} className="font-bold hover:underline" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{u.handle}</Link>
                          <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] ml-2">{u.role}</span>
                        </div>
                        <button onClick={() => addFriend(u.handle)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">+ Ajouter</button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {friendsData.received.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-8">
                  <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#E76F51] mb-3">Demandes en attente ({friendsData.received.length})</h2>
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {friendsData.received.map((r: any) => (
                      <div key={r.handle} className="bg-[#FDF4F0] p-3 rounded-xl border border-[#FCE0D6] flex justify-between items-center">
                        <Link href={`/u/${r.handle}`} className="font-bold hover:underline" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{r.handle}</Link>
                        <div className="flex gap-2">
                          <button onClick={() => respond(r.handle, 'accept')} className="bg-green-500 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">Accepter</button>
                          <button onClick={() => respond(r.handle, 'reject')} className="bg-red-500 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">Refuser</button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-3">Mes Amis ({friendsData.friends.length})</h2>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {friendsData.friends.map((f: any) => (
                  <div key={f.handle} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                      <Link href={`/u/${f.handle}`} className="font-bold hover:underline" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{f.handle}</Link>
                      <span className="text-xs bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] px-2 py-1 rounded">Ami</span>
                    </div>
                    {f.last_seal ? (
                      <Link href={`/o/${f.last_seal_id}`} className="block text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-2 rounded border border-[var(--border-color)] hover:border-[#E76F51] transition">
                        Derni√®re ≈ìuvre : <span className="font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>"{f.last_seal}"</span>
                      </Link>
                    ) : (
                      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Aucune ≈ìuvre publique r√©cente.</p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
                {friendsData.friends.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic col-span-2 text-center py-4">Ta communaut√© est vide. Cherche des cr√©ateurs ci-dessus !</p>}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ========================================== */}
      {/* CONTENU : D√âFIS */}
      {/* ========================================== */}
      {activeTab === 'challenges' && (
        <div className="animate-fade-in">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Lancez des duels de cr√©ativit√©, votez pour le meilleur.</p>
            <button onClick={() => setShowChallengeModal(true)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-md whitespace-nowrap">+ Cr√©er un d√©fi</button>
          </div>

          {challengesLoading ? <p className="text-center font-mono">Chargement...</p> : (
            <div className="grid gap-4">
              {challenges.map(c => (
                <Link href={`/challenges/${c.id}`} key={c.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition block">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{c.title}</h2>
                    {c.is_anonymous && <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 px-2 py-1 rounded">Vote √† l'aveugle</span>}
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">Lanc√© par @{c.creator}</p>
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs font-mono text-[#A89070]">
                    <span>üë• {c.entries_count} participations</span>
                    <span>‚è± Fin le {new Date(c.deadline).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</span>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              ))}
              {challenges.length === 0 && <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Aucun d√©fi en cours. Sois le premier √† en lancer un !</p>}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODALE DE CR√âATION DE D√âFI */}
      {showChallengeModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cr√©er un D√©fi</h2>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Titre (ex: Meilleur design de l'√©t√©)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={challengeTitle} onChange={e => setChallengeTitle(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea placeholder="Description du d√©fi..." className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={challengeDesc} onChange={e => setChallengeDesc(e.target.value)} />
            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={challengeAnon} onChange={e => setChallengeAnon(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cacher les noms (Vote √† l'aveugle)</span>
            </label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowChallengeModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={createChallenge} className="flex-1 bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Lancer</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/login/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Login() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleLogin() {
    if (!email || !password) return setStatus("Email et mot de passe requis");
    setStatus("Connexion...");
    
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    if (res.ok) {
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_pubkey', data.pubkey);
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', data.handle);
      setStatus("‚úÖ Connect√© !");
      setTimeout(() => router.push('/feed'), 500);
    } else {
      setStatus("‚ùå " + data.error);
    }
  }

  // La fonction magique pour "Entr√©e"
  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleLogin(); };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Se connecter</h1>
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Email</label>
      <input type="email" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Mot de passe</label>
      <div className="relative mb-2">
        <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-3 opacity-50">{showPassword ? "üôà" : "üëÅÔ∏è"}</button>
      </div>
      
      <div className="text-right mb-6">
        <Link href="/forgot-password" className="text-sm text-[#E76F51] hover:underline font-bold">Mot de passe oubli√© ?</Link>
      </div>
      
      <button onClick={handleLogin} className="w-full bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl font-bold">Connexion</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      
      <p className="text-center mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Pas de compte ? <Link href="/signup" className="underline font-bold">Cr√©er un compte</Link></p>
    </div>
  );
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/messages/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export default function Messages() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState('');
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState<any[]>([]);
  
  const [activeChat, setActiveChat] = useState<any>(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('');
  
  const [showGroupModal, setShowGroupModal] = useState(false);
  const [groupName, setGroupName] = useState('');
  const [activePact, setActivePact] = useState<any>(null);

  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const prevMessagesLength = useRef(0);
  const myPubkey = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey') : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    setMyHandle(localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle') || '');
    fetch('/api/messages').then(r => r.json()).then(d => setContacts(d.contacts || []));
    fetch(`/api/groups/list?me=${localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle')}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setGroups(d.groups || []));
  }, []);

  // Charger les messages avec FUSION intelligente
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeChat || !myHandle) return;
    const load = () => {
      const endpoint = activeChat.type === 'dm' 
        ? `/api/messages/${activeChat.id}?me=${myHandle}` 
        : `/api/groups/messages?id=${activeChat.id}`;

      fetch(endpoint).then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
        const serverMessages = data.messages || [];
        
        setMessages(prev => {
          // On garde les messages temporaires qui ne sont pas encore arriv√©s du serveur
          const tempMessages = prev.filter(m => m.isTemp);
          
          // On fusionne : les messages du serveur + les messages temporaires qui n'ont pas encore de correspondance
          const merged = [...serverMessages];
          
          tempMessages.forEach(temp => {
            // Si le message temporaire n'existe pas d√©j√† dans la r√©ponse serveur (comparaison de contenu et auteur)
            const isAlreadySaved = serverMessages.some(
              (sm: any) => sm.content === temp.content && sm.sender === temp.sender
            );
            if (!isAlreadySaved) {
              merged.push(temp);
            }
          });

          return merged;
        });
      });
    };
    
    load();
    const interval = setInterval(load, 3000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [activeChat, myHandle]);

  useEffect(() => { prevMessagesLength.current = 0; }, [activeChat]);

  useEffect(() => { 
    if (messages.length > prevMessagesLength.current) {
      messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" }); 
      prevMessagesLength.current = messages.length;
    }
  }, [messages]);

  async function sendMessage() {
    if (!newMessage.trim() || !activeChat) return;
    const msg = newMessage;
    setNewMessage('');
    
    // On ajoute un ID temporaire unique pour le suivi
    const tempId = `temp-${Date.now()}`;
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, { content: msg, sender: myHandle, created_at: new Date().toISOString(), isTemp: true, id: tempId }]);

    try {
      if (activeChat.type === 'dm') {
        await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: myHandle, receiver_handle: activeChat.id, content: msg }) });
      } else {
        await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: activeChat.id, sender_handle: myHandle, content: msg }) });
      }
      // Une fois le message envoy√© avec succ√®s, on force un rechargement imm√©diat
      // pour remplacer le message temporaire par le vrai message du serveur
      setTimeout(() => {
        setMessages(prev => prev.filter(m => m.id !== tempId));
      }, 500);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Erreur d'envoi", e);
    }
  }

  async function createGroup() {
    if (!groupName) return;
    const res = await fetch('/api/groups/create', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ name: groupName, creator_pubkey: myPubkey }) });
    if (res.ok) { alert("Groupe cr√©√© !"); setShowGroupModal(false); window.location.reload(); }
  }

  function startPact() {
    router.push(`/seal?pact=true&chat_id=${activeChat.id}&chat_type=${activeChat.type}`);
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 h-[calc(100vh-140px)] flex gap-4 font-serif max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-4 overflow-y-auto ${activeChat ? 'hidden md:block md:w-1/3' : 'w-full md:w-1/3'}`}>
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Messages</h1>
          <button onClick={() => setShowGroupModal(true)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-lg">+ Groupe</button>
        </div>
        {groups.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] uppercase mb-2">Groupes</h2>
            {groups.map(g => (
              <div key={g.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'group', id: g.id, name: g.name })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#4A90C2] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm">G</div>
                <div className="font-bold text-sm truncate" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{g.name}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
        <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] uppercase mb-2">Messages Priv√©s</h2>
        {contacts.filter(c => c.handle !== myHandle).map(c => (
          <div key={c.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: c.handle, name: c.handle })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm">{c.handle[0].toUpperCase()}</div>
            <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{c.handle}</div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {activeChat ? (
        <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl flex flex-col overflow-hidden w-full md:w-2/3`}>
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-center">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button onClick={() => setActiveChat(null)} className="md:hidden text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>‚Üê</button>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                {activeChat.type === 'group' ? `üë• ${activeChat.name}` : `@${activeChat.name}`}
              </h2>
            </div>
            <button onClick={startPact} className="bg-[#4A90C2] text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg">+ Pacte</button>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto space-y-3">
            {messages.map((m, i) => {
              if (m.content.startsWith('[PACTE]')) {
                const [id, title] = m.content.replace('[PACTE]', '').split('|');
                return (
                  <div key={i} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] border-2 border-[#4A90C2] p-4 rounded-xl text-center my-4">
                    <div className="font-bold text-lg mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>ü§ù {title}</div>
                    <button onClick={() => setActivePact(id)} className="bg-[#4A90C2] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">Voir / Signer le Pacte</button>
                  </div>
                );
              }
              return (
                <div key={i} className={`max-w-[80%] p-3 rounded-xl text-sm ${m.sender === myHandle ? 'bg-[#E76F51] text-white ml-auto rounded-br-none' : 'bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-bl-none'} ${m.isTemp ? 'opacity-60' : ''}`} style={{color: m.sender === myHandle ? '#FFF' : 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                  {activeChat.type === 'group' && m.sender !== myHandle && <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold opacity-70 mb-1">@{m.sender}</div>}
                  {m.content}
                </div>
              );
            })}
            <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] flex gap-2">
            <input type="text" value={newMessage} onChange={e => setNewMessage(e.target.value)} placeholder="√âcrire..." className="flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && sendMessage()} />
            <button onClick={sendMessage} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 rounded-xl font-bold">‚Üí</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="hidden md:flex flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl items-center justify-center text-[var(--text-muted)]">
          S√©lectionnez une discussion.
        </div>
      )}

      {showGroupModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cr√©er un Groupe</h2>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Nom du groupe" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={groupName} onChange={e => setGroupName(e.target.value)} />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowGroupModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={createGroup} className="flex-1 bg-[#4A90C2] text-white py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Cr√©er</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {activePact && <PactModal pactId={activePact} onClose={() => setActivePact(null)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function PactModal({ pactId, onClose }: { pactId: string, onClose: () => void }) {
  const [pact, setPact] = useState<any>(null);
  const loadPact = () => fetch(`/api/pacts/${pactId}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setPact(d));
  useEffect(() => { loadPact(); }, [pactId]);

  async function signPact() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    const res = await fetch('/api/pacts/sign', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ pact_id: pactId, pubkey }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.isNowSealed) alert("üéâ Le pacte est complet ! Il a √©t√© grav√© sur la blockchain pour tous les signataires.");
    loadPact();
  }

  if (!pact) return null;
  const myHandle = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
  const hasSigned = pact.signataires.includes(myHandle);
  const isSealed = pact.status === 'scelle';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-8 border-2 ${isSealed ? 'border-[#7A9B5A]' : 'border-[#4A90C2]'}`}>
        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{pact.title}</h2>
          {isSealed && <span className="bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-bold">üîí SCELL√â</span>}
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm mb-4 text-[var(--text-muted)]">Propos√© par @{pact.creator}</p>
        <p className="italic mb-4 p-3 bg-[var(--bg-main)] rounded-xl text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{pact.description}</p>
        
        {pact.file_path && (
          <a href={pact.file_path} target="_blank" className="block bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] border border-[#C9D8E8] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 text-center font-bold text-sm hover:underline">
            üìÅ Voir le fichier joint au pacte
          </a>
        )}

        <div className="mb-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold mb-2 flex justify-between" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
            Signatures : <span>{pact.signataires.length} / {pact.required_signatures}</span>
          </h3>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {pact.signataires.map((s: string) => <span key={s} className="bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] border border-[#A8C77E] px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold">‚úÖ @{s}</span>)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button onClick={onClose} className="flex-1 py-3 font-bold opacity-50" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Fermer</button>
          {!hasSigned && !isSealed && <button onClick={signPact} className="flex-1 bg-[#4A90C2] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold">‚úçÔ∏è Contresigner</button>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export default function PublicSealPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [seal, setSeal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (params.id) {
      fetch(`/api/o/${params.id}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
        if (d.seal) setSeal(d.seal);
        setLoading(false);
      });
    }
  }, [params.id]);

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement de l'≈ìuvre...</div>;
  if (!seal) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono text-red-500">≈íuvre introuvable ou priv√©e.</div>;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-main)] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 font-serif">
      
      <div className="w-full max-w-xl bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]"></div>
        
        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <span className="inline-block bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#EFE0CA] text-[#A89070] text-xs font-mono font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-widest mb-4">
            Preuve d'ant√©riorit√©

        {seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path && (
          <a href={seal.file_path} target="_blank" className="block w-full text-center bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:opacity-80 transition mb-6">

          <Link href="/seal" className="inline-block bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transition transform hover:-translate-y-1">
            Sceller mon ≈ìuvre gratuitement

          </div>

--- FICHIER : src/app/profil/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { generatePortfolioPDF } from '@/lib/pdf';

export default function Profil() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('public');

  const [showPortfolioModal, setShowPortfolioModal] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSeals, setSelectedSeals] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('all');
  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState('');
  const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState('');

  async function loadProfil() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) { router.push('/login'); return; }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/profil?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`);
      const d = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && d.handle) setData({ ...d, seals: d.seals || [], badges: d.badges || [], favorites: d.favorites || [] });
      else setError("Profil introuvable.");
    } catch (e) { setError("Erreur serveur."); }
  }

  useEffect(() => { loadProfil(); }, [router]);

  const openPortfolioModal = () => {
    setShowPortfolioModal(true);
    setSelectedSeals(data.seals.map((s: any) => s.id));
    setFilterType('all');
    setStartDate('');
    setEndDate('');
  };

  const applyFilters = (type: string, start: string, end: string) => {
    let filtered = data.seals;
    if (type !== 'all') {
      if (type === 'flash') filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => s.is_flash);
      else filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => s.visibility === type && !s.is_flash);
    }
    if (start) filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => new Date(s.timestamp) >= new Date(start));
    if (end) filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => new Date(s.timestamp) <= new Date(end));
    setSelectedSeals(filtered.map((s: any) => s.id));
  };

  const handleTypeChange = (type: string) => { setFilterType(type); applyFilters(type, startDate, endDate); };
  const handleStartDate = (date: string) => { setStartDate(date); applyFilters(filterType, date, endDate); };
  const handleEndDate = (date: string) => { setEndDate(date); applyFilters(filterType, startDate, date); };

  const toggleSeal = (id: string) => {
    if (selectedSeals.includes(id)) setSelectedSeals(selectedSeals.filter(sid => sid !== id));
    else setSelectedSeals([...selectedSeals, id]);
  };

  const generatePDF = () => {
    const sealsToInclude = data.seals.filter((s: any) => selectedSeals.includes(s.id));
    if (sealsToInclude.length === 0) return alert("S√©lectionne au moins une ≈ìuvre !");
    generatePortfolioPDF(data.handle, sealsToInclude, startDate, endDate, data.is_pro);
    setShowPortfolioModal(false);
  };

  async function changeVisibility(sealId: string, newVisibility: string) {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    await fetch('/api/seal/update-visibility', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ seal_id: sealId, pubkey, visibility: newVisibility })
    });
    loadProfil();
  }

  if (error) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono text-red-500">{error}</div>;
  if (!data) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;

  const counts = {
    public: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.visibility === 'public' && !s.is_flash).length,
    friends: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.visibility === 'friends' && !s.is_flash).length,
    private: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.visibility === 'private' && !s.is_flash).length,
    flash: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.is_flash).length,
  };

  // ONGLET FAVORIS
  if (activeTab === 'favorites') {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif">
        <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('public')} className="text-sm font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-4 hover:underline">‚Üê Retour au coffre</button>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üìå Mes Favoris</h2>
        <div className="grid gap-3">
          {data.favorites?.map((fav: any) => (
            <Link href={`/o/${fav.id}`} key={fav.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] block hover:border-[#E76F51]">
              <div className="font-bold text-lg" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{fav.title}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{fav.author} ‚Ä¢ {new Date(fav.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</div>
            </Link>
          ))}
          {(!data.favorites || data.favorites.length === 0) && <p className="italic text-[var(--text-muted)] text-center py-10">Aucune ≈ìuvre sauvegard√©e.</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const filteredSeals = data.seals.filter((seal: any) => {
    if (activeTab === 'flash') return seal.is_flash === true;
    if (activeTab === 'public') return seal.visibility === 'public' && !seal.is_flash;
    if (activeTab === 'friends') return seal.visibility === 'friends' && !seal.is_flash;
    if (activeTab === 'private') return seal.visibility === 'private' && !seal.is_flash;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif relative">
      
      <div className="text-center mb-6 relative">
        <Link href="/settings" className="absolute top-0 right-0 text-2xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition" title="Param√®tres">‚öôÔ∏è</Link>
        
        <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-3xl mx-auto mb-4 shadow-lg relative">
          {data.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
          {data.sealed >= 10 && <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 bg-green-500 border-2 border-white rounded-full w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center text-xs">‚úì</div>}
        </div>
        
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
          @{data.handle}
          {data.is_pro && <span className="text-sm bg-[#2C1810] text-[#E9C46A] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-[#E9C46A] tracking-widest">üëë PRO</span>}
          {data.streak > 0 && <span className="text-sm bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-[#FCE0D6]">üî• {data.streak} jours</span>}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase tracking-wider">{data.role || 'Identit√© souveraine'}</p>
        {data.bio && <p className="mt-3 text-sm italic opacity-80 max-w-md mx-auto" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>"{data.bio}"</p>}
      </div>

      {data.badges.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mb-8 bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#EFE0CA] p-4 rounded-2xl">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] uppercase tracking-widest mb-3 text-center">Badges</h2>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
            {data.badges.map((badge: any, i: number) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-white border border-[#EFE0CA] w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl shadow-sm cursor-help" title={badge.desc}>
                <span className="text-2xl">{badge.icon}</span>
                <span className="text-[0.55rem] font-bold text-[#7A5A3A] mt-1">{badge.name}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-6 rounded-2xl justify-around mb-8 shadow-sm">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.sealed}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">Scell√©s</div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.hosted}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">H√©berg√©s</div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.pacts}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">Pactes</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-4 mb-8">
        <button onClick={openPortfolioModal} className="flex-1 bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-md hover:opacity-80 transition">üìÑ Exporter mon Portfolio</button>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üìö Mon Coffre</h2>
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap gap-1">
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('public')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'public' ? 'bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üåç Public ({counts.public})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('friends')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'friends' ? 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üë• Amis ({counts.friends})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('private')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'private' ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üîí Priv√© ({counts.private})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('flash')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'flash' ? 'bg-[#2C1810] text-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>‚ö° Flash ({counts.flash})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('favorites')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'favorites' ? 'bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üìå Favoris ({data.favorites?.length || 0})</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3 mb-10">
        {filteredSeals.length === 0 && <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic text-sm p-4 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl text-center">Aucune ≈ìuvre dans ce compartiment.</p>}
        
        {filteredSeals.map((seal: any) => (
          <div key={seal.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between sm:items-center shadow-sm gap-3">
            <div className="flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                <div className="font-bold text-lg" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                {seal.is_flash && <span className="text-xs bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] px-2 py-0.5 rounded">Flash</span>}
              </div>
              {seal.description && <div className="text-xs italic text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2">"{seal.description}"</div>}
              <div className="flex gap-3 text-xs font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                <span>{new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</span>
                <span>‚ù§Ô∏è {seal.likes_count || 0}</span>
                <span>üí¨ {seal.comments_count || 0}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <select value={seal.is_flash ? 'flash' : seal.visibility} onChange={(e) => changeVisibility(seal.id, e.target.value)} className="text-xs border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-2 bg-[var(--bg-main)] font-bold outline-none cursor-pointer" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} disabled={seal.is_flash}>
                <option value="public">üåç Public</option>
                <option value="friends">üë• Amis</option>
                <option value="private">üîí Priv√©</option>
                {seal.is_flash && <option value="flash">‚ö° Flash</option>}
              </select>
              <button onClick={async () => {
                if (!confirm("Supprimer d√©finitivement cette ≈ìuvre ?")) return;
                const p = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
                await fetch(`/api/seal/delete?id=${seal.id}&pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(p!)}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
                loadProfil();
              }} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 p-2 text-xl">üóëÔ∏è</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {showPortfolioModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6 flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Configurer le Portfolio</h2>
            <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-3 text-[0.65rem] font-mono font-bold">
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('all')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'all' ? 'bg-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Tout</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('public')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'public' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#5D7A3A]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Public</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('private')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'private' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#C44A2E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Priv√©</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('flash')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'flash' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#C44A2E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Flash</button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
              <div className="flex-1">
                <label className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 block">Du</label>
                <input type="date" value={startDate} onChange={e => handleStartDate(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded-lg text-xs bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1">
                <label className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 block">Au</label>
                <input type="date" value={endDate} onChange={e => handleEndDate(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded-lg text-xs bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 mb-4 pr-1">
              {data.seals.map((seal: any) => (
                <label key={seal.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] cursor-pointer transition">
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={selectedSeals.includes(seal.id)} onChange={() => toggleSeal(seal.id)} className="w-5 h-5 accent-[#E76F51]" />
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="font-bold text-sm truncate" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                    <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">{new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</div>
                  </div>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowPortfolioModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-3 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={generatePDF} className="flex-1 bg-[var(--text-main)] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md">G√©n√©rer ({selectedSeals.length})</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/reset-password/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useState, Suspense } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

function ResetForm() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const token = searchParams.get('token');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleReset() {
    if (!password) return setStatus('Mot de passe requis');
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/reset-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ token, newPassword: password }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) {
      setStatus('‚úÖ Mot de passe modifi√© !');
      setTimeout(() => router.push('/login'), 1500);
    } else setStatus('‚ùå ' + data.error);
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[#FFFCF7] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[#EFE0CA] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif">Nouveau mot de passe</h1>
      <div className="relative mb-4">
        <input type={show ? "text" : "password"} placeholder="Nouveau mot de passe" className="w-full border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10" value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button onClick={() => setShow(!show)} className="absolute right-3 top-3">{show ? "üôà" : "üëÅÔ∏è"}</button>
      </div>
      <button onClick={handleReset} className="w-full bg-[#2C1810] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold">Valider</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold">{status}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ResetPassword() {
  return <Suspense fallback={<div>Chargement...</div>}><ResetForm /></Suspense>;
}
