'use client';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export default function Reset() {
  const router = useRouter();
  
  useEffect(() => {
    // On vide toute la m√©moire du t√©l√©phone
    localStorage.clear();
    // On attend 2 secondes et on renvoie vers l'accueil
    setTimeout(() => router.push('/'), 2000);
  }, [router]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[60vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center font-serif">
      <div className="text-5xl mb-4 animate-spin">‚è≥</div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Nettoyage en cours...</h1>
      <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Fermeture de la session pr√©c√©dente. Redirection...</p>
    </div>
  );
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/seal/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useState, useRef, Suspense } from 'react';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';

function SealContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const isPactMode = searchParams.get('pact') === 'true';
  const chatId = searchParams.get('chat_id');
  const chatType = searchParams.get('chat_type');

  const [isFlashMode, setIsFlashMode] = useState(false);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'file' | 'text' | 'media' | 'audio'>('file');
  
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState('');
  const [textContent, setTextContent] = useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  
  const [isHosted, setIsHosted] = useState(true);
  const [isMystery, setIsMystery] = useState(false);     // RESTAUR√â
  const [isEphemeral, setIsEphemeral] = useState(false); // RESTAUR√â
  const [unlockLikes, setUnlockLikes] = useState(10);    // RESTAUR√â
  const [requiredSignatures, setRequiredSignatures] = useState(2);
  
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const [sealedResult, setSealedResult] = useState<any>(null);

  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [audioUrl, setAudioUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const audioChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);

  async function startRecording() {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      mediaRecorderRef.current = mediaRecorder;
      audioChunksRef.current = [];
      mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (event) => { if (event.data.size > 0) audioChunksRef.current.push(event.data); };
      mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
        const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunksRef.current, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        setFile(new File([audioBlob], `audio-${Date.now()}.webm`, { type: 'audio/webm' }));
        setAudioUrl(URL.createObjectURL(audioBlob));
      };
      mediaRecorder.start(); setIsRecording(true);
    } catch (err) { alert("Impossible d'acc√©der au micro."); }
  }

  function stopRecording() {
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && isRecording) {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
      setIsRecording(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSeal() {
    if (!title) return setStatus("Un titre est requis");
    if (!isFlashMode && mode === 'file' && !file) return setStatus("Choisis un fichier");
    if (!isFlashMode && mode === 'text' && !textContent) return setStatus("√âcris quelque chose");
    if (!isFlashMode && mode === 'media' && !file) return setStatus("Prends une photo ou vid√©o");
    if (!isFlashMode && mode === 'audio' && !file) return setStatus("Enregistre un audio");

    setStatus(isPactMode ? "Pr√©paration du pacte..." : "Calcul de l'empreinte...");
    try {
      if (isFlashMode && !file) {
        const hash = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(desc || title));
        let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);
        return sendToServer(btoa(hashStr), null);
      }

      let fileToSeal: File;
      
      if (mode === 'text') {
        const blob = new Blob([textContent], { type: 'text/plain' });
        fileToSeal = new File([blob], `${title.replace(/\s+/g, '-').toLowerCase()}.txt`, { type: 'text/plain' });
      } else {
        if (!file) return setStatus("Fichier manquant");
        fileToSeal = file;
      }

      const buffer = await fileToSeal.arrayBuffer();
      const hash = sha256(new Uint8Array(buffer));
      let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);
      
      sendToServer(btoa(hashStr), fileToSeal);
    } catch (e) { setStatus("‚ùå Erreur de gravure"); }
  }

  async function sendToServer(hashB64: string, fileToUpload: File | null) {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return setStatus("Connecte-toi d'abord !");

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('hash', hashB64);
    formData.append('title', title);
    formData.append('description', mode === 'text' ? textContent : desc);
    formData.append('pubkey', pubkey);
    formData.append('isHosted', (isHosted && fileToUpload !== null).toString());
    if (fileToUpload) formData.append('file', fileToUpload);

    let endpoint = '/api/seal';

    if (isPactMode) {
      endpoint = '/api/pacts/create';
      formData.append('group_id', chatType === 'group' ? (chatId as string) : '');
      formData.append('required_signatures', requiredSignatures.toString());
    } else {
      formData.append('isFlash', isFlashMode.toString());
      // ON RENVOIE LES OPTIONS MYST√àRE ET √âPH√âM√àRE AU SERVEUR
      formData.append('isMystery', isMystery.toString());
      formData.append('isEphemeral', isEphemeral.toString());
      formData.append('unlockLikes', unlockLikes.toString());
    }

    setStatus(isPactMode ? "Envoi du pacte..." : "Ancrage dans la blockchain...");
    const res = await fetch(endpoint, { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    if (res.ok) {
      if (isPactMode) {
        const msg = `[PACTE]${data.id}|${data.title}`;
        if (chatType === 'dm') {
          await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'), receiver_handle: chatId, content: msg }) });
        } else {
          await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: chatId, sender_handle: localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'), content: msg }) });
        }
        window.location.href = '/messages';
      } else {
        setStatus("‚úÖ Scell√© avec succ√®s !");
        setSealedResult({ title, hash: hashB64, date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR'), isFlash: isFlashMode, otsSecured: data.ots_secured });
      }
    } else {
      setStatus("‚ùå " + data.error);
    }
  }

  if (sealedResult) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 font-serif text-center flex flex-col items-center">
        <div className="text-6xl mb-4">{sealedResult.isFlash ? '‚ö°' : '‚úÖ'}</div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>C'est dans le marbre.</h1>
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] w-full max-w-sm text-left shadow-lg mt-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold mb-2" style={{color: '#2C1810'}}>{sealedResult.title}</h3>
          <p className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[#A89070] break-all bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-2 rounded border border-[var(--border-color)]">{sealedResult.hash}</p>
          {sealedResult.otsSecured && <div className="mt-3 bg-[#F5F9EE] border border-[#A8C77E] p-2 rounded-lg text-[#5D7A3A] text-xs font-bold">‚õìÔ∏è Preuve Bitcoin ancr√©e</div>}
          <p className="text-xs text-right mt-3 text-[#7A5A3A]">Scell√© le {sealedResult.date}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2 mt-8 w-full max-w-sm">
          <button onClick={() => window.location.reload()} className="flex-1 bg-[#2C1810] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm">Nouveau</button>
          <Link href="/feed" className="flex-1 border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] text-[#2C1810] p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm text-center">Voir le Feed</Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 font-serif max-w-md mx-auto">
      
      {!isPactMode && (
        <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
          <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
            <Link href="/seal" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]">‚úçÔ∏è Sceller</Link>
            <Link href="/verify" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition">üîç V√©rifier</Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="text-center mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
          {isPactMode ? "Nouveau Pacte." : (isFlashMode ? "Flash." : "L'Atelier.")}
        </h1>
        {isPactMode && <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Scellement collectif diff√©r√©</p>}
      </div>

      {!isPactMode && (
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-6">
          <button onClick={() => setIsFlashMode(false)} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition ${!isFlashMode ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üìÅ Normal</button>
          <button onClick={() => setIsFlashMode(true)} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition ${isFlashMode ? 'bg-[var(--text-main)] shadow text-white' : 'opacity-50'}`}>‚ö° Flash</button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-4 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">
        
        {!isFlashMode && (
          <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4 text-xs font-mono">
            <button onClick={() => setMode('file')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'file' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üìÅ Fichier</button>
            <button onClick={() => setMode('text')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'text' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>‚úçÔ∏è Texte</button>
            <button onClick={() => setMode('media')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'media' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üì∏ Direct</button>
            <button onClick={() => setMode('audio')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'audio' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üéôÔ∏è Audio</button>
          </div>
        )}

        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">{isFlashMode ? "Ton id√©e" : "Titre de l'≈ìuvre"}</label>
          <input type="text" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent" placeholder="Titre..." value={title} onChange={e=>setTitle(e.target.value)} autoFocus={isFlashMode} />
        </div>

        {!isFlashMode && mode !== 'text' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Description</label>
            <textarea className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent" rows={2} value={desc} onChange={e=>setDesc(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        )}

        {!isFlashMode && mode === 'file' && (
          <div><label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Fichier</label><input type="file" className="w-full text-sm mt-2" onChange={e=>setFile(e.target.files?.[0]||null)} /></div>
        )}

        {!isFlashMode && mode === 'media' && (
          <div><label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Capture</label><input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" capture="environment" className="w-full text-sm mt-2" onChange={e=>setFile(e.target.files?.[0]||null)} /></div>
        )}

        {!isFlashMode && mode === 'audio' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Audio</label>
            <div className="mt-2">
              {!isRecording && !audioUrl && <button onClick={startRecording} className="w-full bg-red-500 text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs">üî¥ Enregistrer</button>}
              {isRecording && <button onClick={stopRecording} className="w-full bg-black text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs animate-pulse">‚èπ Arr√™ter</button>}
              {audioUrl && !isRecording && <audio controls src={audioUrl} className="w-full h-10" />}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {!isFlashMode && mode === 'text' && (
          <div><label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Texte</label><textarea className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-4 outline-none bg-transparent min-h-[100px] font-mono text-sm" value={textContent} onChange={e=>setTextContent(e.target.value)} /></div>
        )}

        {!isFlashMode && (
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] space-y-3 mt-4">
            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isHosted} onChange={e=>setIsHosted(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>‚òÅÔ∏è H√©berger le fichier (Cloud)</span>
            </label>

            {/* OPTIONS RESTAUR√âES */}
            {!isPactMode && (
              <>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={isMystery} onChange={e=>setIsMystery(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üé≠ Sceau Myst√®re</span>
                </label>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={isEphemeral} onChange={e=>setIsEphemeral(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>‚è≥ √âph√©m√®re (24h)</span>
                </label>
              </>
            )}

            {isPactMode && (
              <div className="border-t border-[var(--border-color)] pt-3 mt-3">
                <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Signatures requises pour sceller</label>
                <input type="number" min="2" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded-lg text-center font-bold text-lg bg-transparent mt-1" value={requiredSignatures} onChange={e=>setRequiredSignatures(parseInt(e.target.value))} />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        <button onClick={handleSeal} className={`w-full p-4 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg mt-6 text-white ${isPactMode ? 'bg-[#4A90C2]' : (isFlashMode ? 'bg-[#2C1810]' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]')}`}>
          {isPactMode ? 'CR√âER LE PACTE' : (isFlashMode ? 'GRAVER EN 1 CLIC' : 'SCELLER MON ≈íUVRE')}
        </button>
        {status && <p className="text-center font-bold mt-2 text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Seal() {
  return <Suspense fallback={<div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>}><SealContent /></Suspense>;
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/settings/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Settings() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  
  const [oldHandle, setOldHandle] = useState('');
  const [newHandle, setNewHandle] = useState('');
  const [bio, setBio] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState('createur');
  const [pubkey, setPubkey] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
    const p = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!h || !p) { router.push('/login'); return; }
    
    setOldHandle(h);
    setNewHandle(h);
    setPubkey(p);

    // On charge les infos actuelles depuis l'API profil
    fetch(`/api/profil?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(p)}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        if (d.handle) {
          setBio(d.bio || '');
          setRole(d.role || 'createur');
        }
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, [router]);

  async function saveProfile() {
    setSaving(true); setMsg('');
    const res = await fetch('/api/profil/update', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ pubkey, old_handle: oldHandle, new_handle: newHandle, bio, role })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    if (res.ok) {
      setMsg('‚úÖ Profil mis √† jour !');
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', data.new_handle);
      setOldHandle(data.new_handle);
    } else {
      setMsg('‚ùå ' + data.error);
    }
    setSaving(false);
  }

  function logout() {
    localStorage.clear();
    router.push('/login');
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
        <Link href="/profil" className="text-2xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition">‚Üê</Link>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Param√®tres.</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-6">
        {/* SECTION IDENTIT√â */}
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üë§ Identit√© publique</h2>
          
          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Pseudo</label>
          <input type="text" value={newHandle} onChange={e => setNewHandle(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, ''))} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
          
          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Bio / Description</label>
          <textarea value={bio} onChange={e => setBio(e.target.value)} rows={3} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} placeholder="Parle de toi en quelques mots..." />

          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Ton r√¥le</label>
          <select value={role} onChange={e => setRole(e.target.value)} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl bg-transparent mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
            <option value="createur">Cr√©ateur / Artiste</option>
            <option value="freelance">Freelance</option>
            <option value="entreprise">Entreprise / Agence</option>
            <option value="etudiant">√âtudiant</option>
          </select>

          <button onClick={saveProfile} disabled={saving} className="w-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold disabled:opacity-50">
            {saving ? 'Sauvegarde...' : 'Sauvegarder les modifications'}
          </button>
          {msg && <p className="text-center font-bold mt-3" style={{color: msg.includes('‚úÖ') ? '#7A9B5A' : '#C44A2E'}}>{msg}</p>}
        </div>

        {/* SECTION CL√â PRIV√âE */}
        <div className="bg-[#FDF8F0] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#EFE0CA]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-2" style={{color: '#A88620'}}>üîë Cl√© cryptographique</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#7A5A3A] mb-3">C'est l'empreinte de ton identit√© souveraine sur SCEAU. Garde-la secr√®te.</p>
          <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-[#EFE0CA] overflow-x-auto">
            <code className="text-xs font-mono text-gray-600 whitespace-nowrap">{pubkey}</code>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* SECTION DANGER */}
        <div className="bg-[#FDF4F0] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#D64545]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-2 text-[#A02E2E]">‚ö†Ô∏è Zone sensible</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#A02E2E] mb-4">D√©connecte-toi de cet appareil. Ton compte restera intact, mais tu devras te reconnecter.</p>
          <button onClick={logout} className="w-full border-2 border-[#D64545] text-[#D64545] py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-[#D64545] hover:text-white transition">
            Se d√©connecter
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/signup/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { ed25519 } from '@noble/curves/ed25519';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Signup() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleSignup() {
    if (!handle || !email || !password) return setStatus("Tous les champs sont requis");
    setStatus("Cr√©ation en cours...");
    
    try {
      const secret = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
      const pubkey = ed25519.getPublicKey(secret);
      let pubkeyStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < pubkey.length; i++) pubkeyStr += String.fromCharCode(pubkey[i]);
      let secretStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < secret.length; i++) secretStr += String.fromCharCode(secret[i]);
      const pubkeyB64 = btoa(pubkeyStr);

      localStorage.setItem('sceau_secret', btoa(secretStr));

      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/signup', { 
        method: 'POST', 
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
        body: JSON.stringify({ handle, email, password, pubkey: pubkeyB64 }) 
      });
      
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (res.ok) {
        localStorage.setItem('sceau_pubkey', pubkeyB64);
        localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', handle);
        setStatus("‚úÖ Compte cr√©√© !");
        setTimeout(() => router.push('/feed'), 500);
      } else {
        setStatus("‚ùå " + (data.error || "Une erreur est survenue"));
      }
    } catch (e) { setStatus("‚ùå Impossible de contacter le serveur. V√©rifiez votre connexion."); }
  }

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSignup(); };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cr√©er un compte</h1>
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Pseudo</label>
      <input type="text" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={handle} onChange={(e) => setHandle(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Email</label>
      <input type="email" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Mot de passe</label>
      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-3 opacity-50">{showPassword ? "üôà" : "üëÅÔ∏è"}</button>
      </div>
      
      <button onClick={handleSignup} className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white p-4 rounded-xl font-bold">S'inscrire</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      
      <p className="text-center mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">D√©j√† un compte ? <Link href="/login" className="underline font-bold">Se connecter</Link></p>
    </div>
  );
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/u/[handle]/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function PublicProfile() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
    setMyHandle(h);
    if (params.handle) {
      fetch(`/api/user-profile/${params.handle}?viewer=${h || ''}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => { setData(d); setLoading(false); });
    }
  }, [params.handle]);

  async function addFriend() {
    await fetch('/api/friends', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: myHandle, target: data.handle }) });
    alert(`Demande d'ami envoy√©e √† @${data.handle} !`);
    // Recharger pour mettre √† jour le bouton
    window.location.reload();
  }

  // Couleurs par cat√©gorie pour rendre la grille vivante
  const getCategoryStyle = (category: string) => {
    switch (category) {
      case 'Art': return 'border-[#E9C46A] bg-[#FDF8F0]';
      case 'Musique': return 'border-[#4A90C2] bg-[#F0F6FC]';
      case 'Code': return 'border-[#2A9D8F] bg-[#F0F9F7]';
      case '√âcrit': return 'border-[#A78BC8] bg-[#F5F0FA]';
      default: return 'border-[var(--border-color)] bg-[var(--bg-surface)]';
    }
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement du profil...</div>;
  if (data?.error) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono text-red-500">{data.error}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-main)] pb-20 font-serif">
      
      {/* EN-T√äTE DU PROFIL PUBLIC */}
      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-b border-[var(--border-color)] pt-12 pb-8 px-6 text-center relative">
        <div className="w-28 h-28 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-4xl mx-auto mb-4 shadow-xl">
          {data.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-1" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{data.handle}</h1>
        <p className="text-sm font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">{data.role || 'Cr√©ateur sur SCEAU'}</p>
        
        {myHandle !== data.handle && (
          <div className="mb-4">
            {data.relationship === 'friend' && <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 px-4 py-2 rounded-full font-bold text-sm">‚úÖ Vous √™tes amis</span>}
            {data.relationship === 'pending' && <span className="bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800 px-4 py-2 rounded-full font-bold text-sm">‚è≥ Demande en attente</span>}
            {data.relationship === 'none' && <button onClick={addFriend} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-8 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-[#F4A261] transition">+ Ajouter √† mon cercle</button>}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex justify-center gap-6 text-sm font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)]">
          <span>{data.seals.length} ≈íuvres</span>
          <span>{data.seals.filter((s:any)=>s.is_hosted).length} Fichiers</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* LA VITRINE (GRILLE) */}
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-6 mt-8">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
          üñºÔ∏è Galerie Publique
        </h2>
        
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {data.seals.map((seal: any) => (
            <Link href={`/o/${seal.id}`} key={seal.id} className={`block p-5 rounded-2xl border-2 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:-translate-y-1 transition duration-200 ${getCategoryStyle(seal.category)}`}>
              
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
                <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider opacity-60" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                  {seal.category}
                </span>
                {seal.is_hosted ? <span title="Fichier h√©berg√©">üìÅ</span> : <span title="Preuve seule">üîí</span>}
              </div>

              <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-2 leading-tight" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</h3>
              
              {seal.description && (
                <p className="text-xs italic opacity-70 line-clamp-2 mb-3" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                  "{seal.description}"
                </p>
              )}

              <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono opacity-50 border-t border-black/5 pt-2 mt-auto" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
          
          {data.seals.length === 0 && (
            <div className="col-span-full text-center py-10 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
              <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Ce cr√©ateur n'a pas encore publi√© d'≈ìuvre publique.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/verify/page.tsx ---
'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link'; // L'IMPORT MANQUANT EST ICI
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export default function Verify() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('file');
  const [result, setResult] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [titleQuery, setTitleQuery] = useState('');
  
  const [pulseData, setPulseData] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [pulseLoading, setPulseLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tab !== 'pulse') return;
    const fetchPulse = () => {
      fetch('/api/verify/pulse').then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
        setPulseData(data.pulse || []);
        setPulseLoading(false);
      });
    };
    fetchPulse();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchPulse, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [tab]);

  async function verifyFile(file: File) {
    setLoading(true);
    const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
    const hash = sha256(new Uint8Array(buffer));
    let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash: btoa(hashStr) }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  async function verifyHash(input: string) {
    if(!input) return; setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash: input }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  async function searchByTitle() {
    if(!titleQuery) return; setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ query: titleQuery }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  const timeAgo = (dateStr: string) => {
    const diff = Math.floor((new Date().getTime() - new Date(dateStr).getTime()) / 60000);
    if (diff < 1) return "√Ä l'instant";
    if (diff === 1) return "Il y a 1 minute";
    if (diff < 60) return `Il y a ${diff} minutes`;
    return new Date(dateStr).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR');
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif">
      <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
          <Link href="/seal" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition">‚úçÔ∏è Sceller</Link>
          <Link href="/verify" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]">üîç V√©rifier</Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2 text-center" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>V√©rifier & D√©couvrir.</h1>
      <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-6 text-center">Prouvez l'authenticit√© ou d√©couvrez le monde.</p>
      
      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-6">
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('file');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='file'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Fichier</button>
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('hash');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='hash'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Hash</button>
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('title');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='title'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Titre</button>
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('pulse');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='pulse'?'bg-[#E76F51] text-white shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: tab==='pulse' ? '#FFF' : 'var(--text-main)'}}>üåç Pouls</button>
      </div>

      {tab === 'file' && (
        <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-[#4A90C2] bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-xl text-center">
          <input type="file" onChange={e=>e.target.files?.[0] && verifyFile(e.target.files[0])} className="w-full text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'hash' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <input type="text" placeholder="Collez le hash (ex: b2e7...)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none font-mono bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} onChange={e=>verifyHash(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'title' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <input type="text" placeholder="Titre de l'≈ìuvre (ex: Mon premier roman)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={titleQuery} onChange={e=>setTitleQuery(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && searchByTitle()} />
          <button onClick={searchByTitle} className="w-full bg-[#4A90C2] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold">Rechercher</button>
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'pulse' && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 text-xs font-mono text-[#7A9B5A] justify-end">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#7A9B5A] animate-pulse"></span> Connexion en direct
          </div>
          {pulseLoading ? (
            <p className="text-center font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Connexion au r√©seau...</p>
          ) : pulseData.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Le r√©seau est calme...</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-3">
              {pulseData.map((item) => (
                <div key={item.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between animate-fade-in">
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{item.title}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">
                      Par <span className="font-bold text-[#E76F51]">@{item.author}</span> {item.location && `üìç ${item.location}`}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-mono font-bold bg-[var(--bg-main)] px-2 py-1 rounded text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                    {timeAgo(item.timestamp)}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {tab !== 'pulse' && result && (
        <div className={`mt-8 p-6 rounded-2xl border-2 ${result.found ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-[#7A9B5A]' : 'bg-[#FDF4F0] border-[#D64545]'}`}>
          {result.found ? (
            <div>
              <div className="text-center mb-6">
                <div className="text-3xl mb-2">‚úÖ</div>
                <h2 className="font-bold text-xl" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{tab === 'title' ? '≈íUVRES TROUV√âES' : 'FICHIER AUTHENTIQUE'}</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">{result.seals.length} preuve(s)</p>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {result.seals.map((seal: any, index: number) => (
                  <div key={index} className={`p-4 rounded-xl border ${index === 0 ? 'border-[#E76F51] bg-[var(--bg-main)]' : 'border-[var(--border-color)]'}`}>
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">
                          Par <span className="font-bold text-[#E76F51]">@{seal.author}</span> le {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}
                        </div>
                        {seal.hash && <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2 break-all">Hash: {seal.hash}</div>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path && (
                      <a href={seal.file_path} target="_blank" className="mt-3 inline-block bg-[#4A90C2] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">‚¨áÔ∏è T√©l√©charger l'≈ìuvre</a>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-center font-bold text-[#A02E2E]">‚ùå Aucune ≈ìuvre trouv√©e.</p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- FICHIER : src/app/api/attachments/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const sealId = searchParams.get('seal_id');
  if (!sealId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Manquant' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT a.id, a.type, a.content, a.media_url, a.created_at, u.handle as author
      FROM attachments a JOIN users u ON a.user_id = u.id
      WHERE a.seal_id = $1 ORDER BY a.created_at ASC
    `, [sealId]);
    return NextResponse.json({ attachments: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, content, type, media_url } = await req.json();
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO attachments (seal_id, user_id, content, type, media_url) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)",
      [seal_id, user.rows[0].id, content, type || 'text', media_url || null]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/forgot-password/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import crypto from 'crypto';
import { Resend } from 'resend';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { email } = await req.json();
  if (!email) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email requis' }, { status: 400 });

  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

  const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
  const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 3600000); 

  await pool.query("UPDATE users SET reset_token = $1, reset_expiry = $2 WHERE email = $3", [token, expiry, email]);

  const resetLink = `${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || 'http://localhost:3000'}/reset-password?token=${token}`;
  
  try {
    const resendKey = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY;
    if (!resendKey) {
      // Si pas de cl√©, on simule l'envoi pour le d√©veloppement
      console.log("‚ö†Ô∏è Pas de cl√© Resend. Lien de test :", resetLink);
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Lien g√©n√©r√© (voir console)" });
    }

    const resend = new Resend(resendKey);
    await resend.emails.send({
      from: 'SCEAU <onboarding@resend.dev>',
      to: email,
      subject: "R√©initialisation de votre mot de passe",
      html: `<p>Bonjour,</p><p>Voici votre lien : <a href="${resetLink}">R√©initialiser mon mot de passe</a></p>`,
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Email envoy√© !" });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Erreur d'envoi d'email" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { email, password } = await req.json();

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email ou mot de passe incorrect' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.rows[0].password_hash);
    if (!isValid) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email ou mot de passe incorrect' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    // On renvoie le handle et la cl√© publique (convertie en base64)
    return NextResponse.json({
      handle: user.rows[0].handle,
      pubkey: Buffer.from(user.rows[0].pubkey).toString('base64')
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/reset-password/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { token, newPassword } = await req.json();
  if (!token || !newPassword) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs requis' }, { status: 400 });

  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE reset_token = $1 AND reset_expiry > NOW()", [token]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Lien invalide ou expir√©' }, { status: 400 });

  const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
  const password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(newPassword, salt);

  await pool.query("UPDATE users SET password_hash = $1, reset_token = NULL, reset_expiry = NULL WHERE id = $2", [password_hash, user.rows[0].id]);

  return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/signup/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { handle, email, password, pubkey } = await req.json();

    if (!handle || !email || !password || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Tous les champs sont requis' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
    const password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(password, salt);

    const result = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO users (handle, email, password_hash, pubkey) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, decode($4, 'base64')) RETURNING id, handle", 
      [handle, email, password_hash, pubkey]
    );
    
    return NextResponse.json(result.rows[0]);
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Erreur d'inscription:", error);
    if (error.code === '23505') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ce pseudo ou cet email est d√©j√† pris' }, { status: 409 });
    }
    // On renvoie le VRAI message d'erreur pour le d√©bogage
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message || 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/challenges/[id]/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  try {
    const challenge = await pool.query("SELECT * FROM challenges WHERE id = $1", [id]);
    if (challenge.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    const entries = await pool.query(`
      SELECT e.id as entry_id, s.id as seal_id, s.title, s.description, s.file_path, s.category, 
      CASE WHEN $2 = true THEN 'Anonyme' ELSE u.handle END as author,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM challenge_votes WHERE entry_id = e.id) as votes
      FROM challenge_entries e
      JOIN seals s ON e.seal_id = s.id
      JOIN users u ON e.user_id = u.id
      WHERE e.challenge_id = $1
      ORDER BY votes DESC
    `, [id, challenge.rows[0].is_anonymous]);

    return NextResponse.json({ challenge: challenge.rows[0], entries: entries.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/challenges/create/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { title, description, pubkey, is_anonymous } = await req.json();
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    // Date limite dans 7 jours par d√©faut
    const deadline = new Date();
    deadline.setDate(deadline.getDate() + 7);

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO challenges (title, description, creator_id, deadline, is_anonymous) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)",
      [title, description, user.rows[0].id, deadline, is_anonymous]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT c.id, c.title, c.deadline, c.is_anonymous, u.handle as creator,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM challenge_entries WHERE challenge_id = c.id) as entries_count
      FROM challenges c JOIN users u ON c.creator_id = u.id
      ORDER BY c.created_at DESC
    `);
    return NextResponse.json({ challenges: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/challenges/vote/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { challenge_id, entry_id, pubkey } = await req.json();
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const voterId = user.rows[0].id;

    // V√©rifier qu'il ne vote pas pour lui-m√™me
    const entry = await pool.query("SELECT user_id FROM challenge_entries WHERE id = $1", [entry_id]);
    if (entry.rows.length > 0 && entry.rows[0].user_id === voterId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Tu ne peux pas voter pour ta propre ≈ìuvre !' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO challenge_votes (challenge_id, voter_id, entry_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",
      [challenge_id, voterId, entry_id]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

  await pool.query("INSERT INTO comments (seal_id, user_id, content) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [seal_id, user.rows[0].id, content]);

}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/comments/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

// Lire les commentaires d'une ≈ìuvre
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const sealId = searchParams.get('seal_id');
  if (!sealId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'ID manquant' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT c.id, c.content, c.created_at, u.handle as author
      FROM comments c JOIN users u ON c.user_id = u.id
      WHERE c.seal_id = $1 ORDER BY c.created_at ASC
    `, [sealId]);
    return NextResponse.json({ comments: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

// Ajouter un commentaire
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, content } = await req.json();
  if (!seal_id || !pubkey || !content) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const myId = user.rows[0].id;

    await pool.query("INSERT INTO comments (seal_id, user_id, content) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [seal_id, myId, content]);

    // Cr√©er une notification pour l'auteur de l'≈ìuvre
    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id, title FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [seal_id]);
    if (seal.rows.length > 0 && seal.rows[0].author_id !== myId) {
      await pool.query(
        "INSERT INTO notifications (user_id, actor_id, type, message, link) VALUES ($1, $2, 'comment', $3, $4)",
        [seal.rows[0].author_id, myId, `a comment√© ton ≈ìuvre "${seal.rows[0].title}"`, `/o/${seal_id}`]
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/favorites/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cl√© manquante' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ favorites: [] });
    
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.category, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM favorites f
      JOIN seals s ON f.seal_id = s.id
      JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE f.user_id = $1 ORDER BY f.created_at DESC
    `, [user.rows[0].id]);
    return NextResponse.json({ favorites: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ favorites: [] }); }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, action } = await req.json(); // action: 'add' | 'remove'
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    if (action === 'remove') {
      await pool.query("DELETE FROM favorites WHERE user_id = $1 AND seal_id = $2", [user.rows[0].id, seal_id]);
    } else {
      await pool.query("INSERT INTO favorites (user_id, seal_id) VALUES ($1, $2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [user.rows[0].id, seal_id]);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/feed/friends/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');

  if (!myHandle) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });

  try {
    // 1. On trouve l'ID de l'utilisateur
    const me = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
    if (me.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });
    const myId = me.rows[0].id;

    // 2. On trouve les gens avec qui j'ai discut√© (mes contacts)
    const contactsQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT DISTINCT receiver_id as contact_id FROM messages WHERE sender_id = $1
      UNION
      SELECT DISTINCT sender_id as contact_id FROM messages WHERE receiver_id = $1
    `, [myId]);

    if (contactsQuery.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });
    const contactIds = contactsQuery.rows.map(r => r.contact_id);

    // 3. On r√©cup√®re leurs derni√®res ≈ìuvres publiques (5 max)
    const seals = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.author_id = ANY($1) AND s.is_public = TRUE
      ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 5
    `, [contactIds]);

    return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: seals.rows });
  } catch (error) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/feed/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');
  const category = searchParams.get('category');

  try {
    let params: any[] = [];
    let whereClause = "s.visibility = 'public'";

    if (category && category !== 'all') {
      params.push(category);
      whereClause += ` AND s.category = $${params.length}`;
    }

    if (myHandle) {
      const me = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
      if (me.rows.length > 0) {
        const myId = me.rows[0].id;
        const friendsRes = await pool.query(`
          SELECT requester_id as id FROM friendships WHERE addressee_id = $1 AND status = 'accepted'
          UNION
          SELECT addressee_id as id FROM friendships WHERE requester_id = $1 AND status = 'accepted'
        `, [myId]);
        const friendIds = friendsRes.rows.map(r => r.id);
        if (friendIds.length > 0) {
          params.push(friendIds);
          whereClause = `(s.visibility = 'public' OR (s.visibility = 'friends' AND s.author_id = ANY($${params.length})))`;
          if (category && category !== 'all') {
             whereClause += ` AND s.category = $${params.length - 1}`; // On garde le filtre cat√©gorie
          }
        }
      }
    }

    const sql = `
      SELECT s.id, 
        CASE WHEN s.is_mystery = true THEN 'üîí Sceau Myst√®re' ELSE s.title END as title,
        CASE WHEN s.is_mystery = true AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id) < s.unlock_likes THEN null ELSE s.file_path END as file_path,
        s.is_mystery, s.unlock_likes, s.is_flash, s.ots_proof,
        s.description, s.category, s.timestamp, s.visibility, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author,
        s.project_id, s.version_number,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'like') as likes_like,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'wow') as likes_wow,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'inspired') as likes_inspired
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE ${whereClause} ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 30
    `;
    
    const result = await pool.query(sql, params);
    return NextResponse.json({ seals: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/feed/trends/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    // On r√©cup√®re les ≈ìuvres publiques de la semaine, tri√©es par nombre de likes
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.category, s.timestamp, s.location, u.handle as author,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id) as likes_count
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.is_public = TRUE AND s.timestamp >= NOW() - INTERVAL '7 days'
      ORDER BY likes_count DESC, s.timestamp DESC
      LIMIT 3
    `);
    return NextResponse.json({ trends: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/friends/respond/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { me, target, action } = await req.json(); // action: 'accept' | 'reject'
  try {
    const meId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0].id;
    const targetId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [target])).rows[0].id;

    if (action === 'accept') {
      await pool.query(`
        UPDATE friendships SET status = 'accepted' 
        WHERE requester_id = $1 AND addressee_id = $2
      `, [targetId, meId]);
    } else {
      await pool.query(`
        DELETE FROM friendships WHERE requester_id = $1 AND addressee_id = $2
      `, [targetId, meId]);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/friends/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    let me = searchParams.get('me');
    const search = searchParams.get('search');
    
    if (!me) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non connect√©' }, { status: 401 });
    // NOUVEAU : On nettoie le pseudo au cas o√π il y aurait un @
    me = me.replace('@', '');

    const myIdRes = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me]);
    if (myIdRes.rows.length === 0) {
      // Message plus clair pour le d√©bogage
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Ton compte n'existe pas dans la base de donn√©es. D√©connecte-toi et reconnecte-toi." }, { status: 404 });
    }
    const myId = myIdRes.rows[0].id;

    if (search) {
      const users = await pool.query(`
        SELECT handle, role FROM users 
        WHERE handle ILIKE $1 AND id != $2 LIMIT 10
      `, [`%${search.replace('@', '')}%`, myId]);
      return NextResponse.json({ searchResults: users.rows });
    }

    const friendsQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT 
        u.id, 
        u.handle,
        (SELECT title FROM seals WHERE author_id = u.id AND visibility = 'public' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_seal,
        (SELECT id FROM seals WHERE author_id = u.id AND visibility = 'public' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_seal_id
      FROM friendships f
      JOIN users u ON u.id = CASE WHEN f.requester_id = $1 THEN f.addressee_id ELSE f.requester_id END
      WHERE (f.requester_id = $1 OR f.addressee_id = $1) AND f.status = 'accepted'
    `, [myId]);

    const receivedQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT u.handle FROM friendships f 
      JOIN users u ON u.id = f.requester_id
      WHERE f.addressee_id = $1 AND f.status = 'pending'
    `, [myId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      friends: friendsQuery.rows, 
      received: receivedQuery.rows 
    });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Erreur API Friends:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Erreur base de donn√©es : " + error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { me, target } = await req.json();
    
    // Nettoyage des pseudos
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me.replace('@', '')])).rows[0]?.id;
    const targetId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [target.replace('@', '')])).rows[0]?.id;
    
    if (!myId || !targetId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur introuvable dans la base' }, { status: 404 });

    await pool.query(`
      INSERT INTO friendships (requester_id, addressee_id, status) 
      VALUES ($1, $2, 'pending') ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
    `, [myId, targetId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error: any) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/github/sync/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { pubkey, github_username } = await req.json();
    
    // 1. Sauvegarder le pseudo GitHub
    await pool.query("UPDATE users SET github_username = $1 WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $2", [github_username, pubkey]);
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;

    // 2. R√©cup√©rer les derniers commits publics via l'API GitHub
    const ghRes = await fetch(`https://api.github.com/users/${github_username}/events/public`, {
      headers: { 'User-Agent': 'SCEAU-App' }
    });
    const events = await ghRes.json();
    
    let commitsScelles = 0;
    for (const event of events) {
      if (event.type === 'PushEvent' && event.payload.commits.length > 0) {
        for (const commit of event.payload.commits) {
          // On cr√©e un hash bas√© sur le message et l'ID du commit
          const hashStr = `${commit.sha}-${commit.message}`;
          const hash = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(hashStr));
          let hashB64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...hash));

          // On ins√®re dans la base (ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING pour √©viter les doublons)
          await pool.query(
            `INSERT INTO seals (hash, timestamp, author_id, title, category, work_type, is_public) 
             VALUES (decode($1, 'base64'), $2, $3, $4, 'Code', 'commit', true) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`,
            [hashB64, event.created_at, userId, `Commit: ${commit.message.slice(0, 50)}`]
          );
          commitsScelles++;
        }
      }
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, commitsScelles });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur GitHub' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { name, creator_pubkey } = await req.json();
  try {
    const creator = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [creator_pubkey]);
    if (creator.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const creatorId = creator.rows[0].id;

    const group = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO groups (name, creator_id) VALUES ($1, $2) RETURNING id",
      [name, creatorId]
    );
    const groupId = group.rows[0].id;

    // Le cr√©ateur est automatiquement membre
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO group_members (group_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)", [groupId, creatorId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ group_id: groupId });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const me = searchParams.get('me');
  if (!me) return NextResponse.json({ groups: [] });

  try {
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0]?.id;
    if (!myId) return NextResponse.json({ groups: [] });

    const groups = await pool.query(`
      SELECT g.id, g.name, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM group_members WHERE group_id = g.id) as members_count
      FROM groups g
      JOIN group_members gm ON g.id = gm.group_id
      WHERE gm.user_id = $1 ORDER BY g.created_at DESC
    `, [myId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ groups: groups.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

--- FICHIER : src/app/api/like/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, reaction_type } = await req.json();
  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
  const myId = user.rows[0].id;

  try {
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO likes (seal_id, user_id, reaction_type) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [seal_id, myId, reaction_type || 'like']);
    
    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id, title FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [seal_id]);
    if (seal.rows.length > 0 && seal.rows[0].author_id !== myId) {
      await pool.query(
        "INSERT INTO notifications (user_id, actor_id, type, message, link) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)",
        [seal.rows[0].author_id, myId, 'like', `a r√©agi √† ton ≈ìuvre "${seal.rows[0].title}"`, `/o/${seal_id}`]
      );
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (e) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/messages/[handle]/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ handle: string }> }) {
  const { handle } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');

  try {
    const myIdResult = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
    const otherIdResult = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [handle]);
    
    if (myIdResult.rows.length === 0 || otherIdResult.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 400 });
    
    const myId = myIdResult.rows[0].id;
    const otherId = otherIdResult.rows[0].id;

    const messages = await pool.query(`
      SELECT m.content, m.created_at, u.handle as sender
      FROM messages m
      JOIN users u ON m.sender_id = u.id
      WHERE (m.sender_id = $1 AND m.receiver_id = $2) OR (m.sender_id = $2 AND m.receiver_id = $1)
      ORDER BY m.created_at ASC
    `, [myId, otherId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ messages: messages.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { sender_handle, receiver_handle, content } = await req.json();
    if (!sender_handle || !receiver_handle || !content) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const sender = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [sender_handle]);
    const receiver = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [receiver_handle]);
    
    if (sender.rows.length === 0 || receiver.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur inconnu' }, { status: 404 });
    }

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO messages (sender_id, receiver_id, content) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)",
      [sender.rows[0].id, receiver.rows[0].id, content]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error: any) { 
    console.error("Erreur DB message:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}
