--- FICHIER : src/app/api/notifications/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const me = searchParams.get('me');
  if (!me) return NextResponse.json({ notifications: [], unread: 0 });

  try {
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0]?.id;
    if (!myId) return NextResponse.json({ notifications: [], unread: 0 });

    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT n.id, n.type, n.message, n.link, n.is_read, n.created_at, u.handle as actor
      FROM notifications n JOIN users u ON n.actor_id = u.id
      WHERE n.user_id = $1 ORDER BY n.created_at DESC LIMIT 20
    `, [myId]);

    const unread = result.rows.filter(n => !n.is_read).length;
    return NextResponse.json({ notifications: result.rows, unread });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ notifications: [], unread: 0 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/o/[id]/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const viewerPubkey = searchParams.get('viewer');

  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.category, s.timestamp,
             s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.ots_proof,
             encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.id = $1 AND s.visibility = 'public'
    `, [id]);

    if (result.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    if (viewerPubkey) {
      const viewer = await pool.query(
        "SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1",
        [viewerPubkey]
      );
      if (viewer.rows.length > 0) {
        await pool.query(
          "INSERT INTO access_log (seal_id, viewer_id, action, context) VALUES ($1, $2, 'view', 'public')",
          [id, viewer.rows[0].id]
        );
      }
    }

    const { ots_proof, ...seal } = result.rows[0];
    return NextResponse.json({ seal: { ...seal, has_ots_proof: !!ots_proof } });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/pacts/[id]/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const pact = await pool.query(`
    SELECT p.*, u.handle as creator, encode(p.hash, 'base64') as hash_str
    FROM pacts p JOIN users u ON p.creator_id = u.id 
    WHERE p.id = $1
  `, [id]);
  
  if (pact.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

  const signatures = await pool.query("SELECT u.handle FROM pact_signatures s JOIN users u ON s.user_id = u.id WHERE s.pact_id = $1", [id]);
  
  return NextResponse.json({ 
    ...pact.rows[0], 
    signataires: signatures.rows.map(r => r.handle) 
  });
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/pacts/create/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { PutObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const data = await req.formData();
    const title = data.get('title') as string;
    const description = data.get('description') as string;
    const pubkey = data.get('pubkey') as string;
    const groupId = data.get('group_id') as string;
    const requiredSignatures = parseInt(data.get('required_signatures') as string || '2');
    
    // Nouveaux champs
    const hash = data.get('hash') as string;
    const category = data.get('category') as string || 'Art';
    const isHosted = data.get('isHosted') === 'true';
    const file = data.get('file') as File;

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const creatorId = user.rows[0].id;

    let filePath = null;
    if (isHosted && file) {
      const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const filename = `${Date.now()}-${file.name}`;
      await r2Client.send(new PutObjectCommand({ Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET, Key: filename, Body: buffer, ContentType: file.type }));
      filePath = `${process.env.AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3}/${process.env.S3_BUCKET}/${filename}`;
    }

    const pact = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO pacts (title, description, creator_id, group_id, required_signatures, hash, file_path, is_hosted, category) 
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, decode($6, 'base64'), $7, $8, $9) RETURNING id`,
      [title, description, creatorId, groupId || null, requiredSignatures, hash, filePath, isHosted, category]
    );
    
    // Le cr√©ateur signe automatiquement
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO pact_signatures (pact_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)", [pact.rows[0].id, creatorId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ id: pact.rows[0].id, title });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/pacts/sign/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import { submitToOts } from '@/lib/ots';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { pact_id, pubkey } = await req.json();
  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO pact_signatures (pact_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",
      [pact_id, user.rows[0].id]
    );

    const pact = await pool.query(
      "SELECT title, description, required_signatures, status FROM pacts WHERE id = $1",
      [pact_id]
    );
    const sigs = await pool.query(
      `SELECT u.handle, s.created_at FROM pact_signatures s JOIN users u ON s.user_id = u.id
       WHERE s.pact_id = $1 ORDER BY s.created_at ASC`,
      [pact_id]
    );

    const required = pact.rows[0].required_signatures;
    const current = sigs.rows.length;

    if (current >= required && pact.rows[0].status !== 'scelle') {
      const canonical = JSON.stringify({
        title: pact.rows[0].title,
        description: pact.rows[0].description || '',
        signers: sigs.rows.map((r: any) => ({ handle: r.handle, signed_at: new Date(r.created_at).toISOString() })),
      });
      const pactHash = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(canonical));

      let otsProof: string | null = null;
      try {
        const ots = await submitToOts(new Uint8Array(pactHash));
        if (ots.ok && ots.proof) otsProof = ots.proof.toString('base64');
      } catch (e) { console.error('[Pact OTS]', e); }

      await pool.query(
        `UPDATE pacts SET status='scelle', scelle_at=NOW(), pact_hash=$1, ots_proof=$2 WHERE id=$3`,
        [Buffer.from(pactHash), otsProof, pact_id]
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, current, required });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/profil/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cl√© manquante' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle, bio, role, is_pro FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 404 });
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;
    const isPro = user.rows[0].is_pro || false;

    const stats = await pool.query(`
      SELECT COUNT(id) as total_sealed, SUM(CASE WHEN is_hosted = true THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as total_hosted
      FROM seals WHERE author_id = $1
    `, [userId]);

    const seals = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.timestamp, s.visibility, s.is_flash, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id) as likes_count,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM comments WHERE seal_id = s.id) as comments_count
      FROM seals s WHERE s.author_id = $1 ORDER BY s.created_at DESC
    `, [userId]);

    const pacts = await pool.query("SELECT COUNT(*) as total_pacts FROM pact_signatures WHERE user_id = $1", [userId]);

    const totalSealed = parseInt(stats.rows[0].total_sealed || 0);
    const totalPacts = parseInt(pacts.rows[0].total_pacts || 0);

    // --- CALCUL DU STREAK (version propre) ---
    const uniqueDatesSet = new Set(
      seals.rows.map((s: any) => new Date(s.timestamp).toISOString().split('T')[0])
    );
    let streak = 0;
    let cursor = new Date();
    cursor.setUTCHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    if (!uniqueDatesSet.has(cursor.toISOString().split('T')[0])) {
      cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
    }
    while (uniqueDatesSet.has(cursor.toISOString().split('T')[0])) {
      streak++;
      cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
    }

    // --- FAVORIS ---
    const favorites = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.category, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM favorites f JOIN seals s ON f.seal_id = s.id JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE f.user_id = $1 ORDER BY f.created_at DESC
    `, [userId]);

    // --- BADGES ---
    const badges = [];
    if (totalSealed >= 1) badges.push({ icon: 'üå±', name: 'Apprenti', desc: 'Premi√®re ≈ìuvre scell√©e' });
    if (totalSealed >= 10) badges.push({ icon: 'ü•â', name: 'Archiviste', desc: '10 ≈ìuvres scell√©es' });
    if (totalSealed >= 50) badges.push({ icon: 'ü•á', name: 'Gardien', desc: '50 ≈ìuvres scell√©es' });
    if (isPro) badges.push({ icon: 'üëë', name: 'PRO', desc: 'Compte v√©rifi√© PRO' });
    if (streak >= 3) badges.push({ icon: 'üî•', name: 'En Feu', desc: '3 jours cons√©cutifs' });
    if (totalPacts >= 1) badges.push({ icon: 'ü§ù', name: 'N√©gociateur', desc: 'A sign√© un pacte' });

    return NextResponse.json({
      handle: user.rows[0].handle,
      bio: user.rows[0].bio,
      role: user.rows[0].role,
      is_pro: isPro,
      streak: streak,
      favorites: favorites.rows,
      sealed: totalSealed,
      hosted: parseInt(stats.rows[0].total_hosted || 0),
      pacts: totalPacts,
      badges: badges,
      seals: seals.rows
    });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/profil/update/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { pubkey, old_handle, new_handle, bio, role } = await req.json();
    
    // 1. V√©rifier que l'utilisateur existe bien
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;

    // 2. Si le pseudo change, v√©rifier qu'il n'est pas d√©j√† pris
    if (new_handle && new_handle !== old_handle) {
      const check = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [new_handle]);
      if (check.rows.length > 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ce pseudo est d√©j√† pris' }, { status: 409 });
    }

    // 3. Mettre √† jour
    const finalHandle = new_handle || old_handle;
    await pool.query(
      "UPDATE users SET handle = $1, bio = $2, role = $3 WHERE id = $4",
      [finalHandle, bio, role, userId]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, new_handle: finalHandle });
  } catch (error) { 
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/[id]/access-log/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');

  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cl√© manquante' }, { status: 401 });
  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

  const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [id]);
  if (seal.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
  if (seal.rows[0].author_id !== user.rows[0].id) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non autoris√©' }, { status: 403 });
  }

  const log = await pool.query(`
    SELECT a.action, a.context, a.created_at, u.handle as viewer_handle,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM seals WHERE author_id = u.id) as viewer_seals,
      u.created_at as viewer_account_created_at
    FROM access_log a JOIN users u ON a.viewer_id = u.id
    WHERE a.seal_id = $1 ORDER BY a.created_at DESC LIMIT 500
  `, [id]);

  return NextResponse.json({
    entries: log.rows.map((r: any) => ({
      action: r.action,
      context: r.context,
      at: r.created_at,
      viewer: r.viewer_handle,
      viewer_profile: {
        account_age_days: Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(r.viewer_account_created_at).getTime()) / 86400000),
        seals_count: parseInt(r.viewer_seals || 0),
      },
    })),
  });
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/[id]/ots/refresh/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { upgradeOts } from '@/lib/ots';

export async function POST(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  try {
    const res = await pool.query("SELECT hash FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [id]);
    if (res.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    const upgraded = await upgradeOts(new Uint8Array(res.rows[0].hash));
    if (!upgraded) {
      return NextResponse.json({ status: 'pending', message: 'Pas encore confirm√©. R√©essaie dans quelques heures.' });
    }
    await pool.query(
      "UPDATE seals SET ots_proof = $1, ots_upgraded_at = NOW() WHERE id = $2",
      [upgraded.toString('base64'), id]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ status: 'upgraded', size: upgraded.length });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/[id]/ots/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  try {
    const res = await pool.query("SELECT ots_proof, title FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [id]);
    if (res.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    const row = res.rows[0];
    if (!row.ots_proof) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Pas de preuve .ots' }, { status: 404 });

    const buffer = Buffer.from(row.ots_proof, 'base64');
    const safeTitle = (row.title || 'sceau').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '-').slice(0, 40);
    return new NextResponse(buffer, {
      status: 200,
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream',
        'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="${safeTitle}.ots"`,
      },
    });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/delete/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { DeleteObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';

export async function DELETE(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const sealId = searchParams.get('id');
    const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');

    if (!sealId || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'ID ou cl√© manquant' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    
    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id, file_path FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [sealId]);
    if (seal.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Sceau introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    
    if (seal.rows[0].author_id !== user.rows[0].id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non autoris√©' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    // CORRECTION : On supprime le fichier de Cloudflare R2, pas du disque local
    if (seal.rows[0].file_path) {
      try {
        const filename = seal.rows[0].file_path.split('/').pop();
        await r2Client.send(new DeleteObjectCommand({
          Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET,
          Key: filename
        }));
      } catch (e) { console.log("Fichier introuvable sur R2 ou d√©j√† supprim√©"); }
    }

    await pool.query("DELETE FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [sealId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { PutObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { submitToOts } from '@/lib/ots';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const data = await req.formData();
    const hash = data.get('hash') as string;
    const title = data.get('title') as string;
    const description = data.get('description') as string;
    const isFlash = data.get('isFlash') === 'true';
    const isMystery = data.get('isMystery') === 'true';
    const isEphemeral = data.get('isEphemeral') === 'true';
    const unlockLikes = parseInt(data.get('unlockLikes') as string || '0');
    const pubkey = data.get('pubkey') as string;
    const file = data.get('file') as File;

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const authorId = user.rows[0].id;

    let filePath: string | null = null;
    let fileSize = 0;
    let otsProof: string | null = null;
    let otsCalendars: string[] = [];

    try {
      const hashBytes = new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(hash, 'base64'));
      const ots = await submitToOts(hashBytes);
      if (ots.ok && ots.proof) {
        otsProof = ots.proof.toString('base64');
        otsCalendars = ots.calendars;
        console.log(`[OTS] ancr√© via ${ots.calendars.length} calendar(s)`);
      } else {
        console.warn('[OTS] √©chec :', ots.error);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[OTS] exception :', e);
    }

    if (!isFlash && file) {
      const quotaCheck = await pool.query(
        "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(file_size), 0) as used FROM seals WHERE author_id = $1 AND is_hosted = true",
        [authorId]
      );
      const currentUsage = parseInt(quotaCheck.rows[0].used);
      if (currentUsage + file.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Quota de 50 Mo d√©pass√©.' }, { status: 403 });
      }

      fileSize = file.size;
      const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const filename = `${hash.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 20)}-${file.name}`;
      await r2Client.send(new PutObjectCommand({
        Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET,
        Key: filename,
        Body: buffer,
        ContentType: file.type,
      }));
      filePath = `${process.env.AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3}/${process.env.S3_BUCKET}/${filename}`;
    }

    const result = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO seals (
        hash, timestamp, author_id, title, description, is_public, visibility,
        is_hosted, file_path, file_size, is_flash, is_mystery, unlock_likes, is_ephemeral,
        ots_proof, ots_submitted_at
      ) VALUES (
        decode($1, 'base64'), NOW(), $2, $3, $4, true, 'public',
        $5, $6, $7, $8, $9, $10, $11, $12, $13
      ) RETURNING id`,
      [hash, authorId, title, description,
       !isFlash && filePath ? true : false, filePath, fileSize,
       isFlash, isMystery, unlockLikes, isEphemeral,
       otsProof, otsProof ? new Date() : null]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({
      seal_id: result.rows[0].id,
      ots_secured: otsProof !== null,
      ots_calendars: otsCalendars,
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message || 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/update-visibility/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { seal_id, pubkey, visibility } = await req.json();

    if (!seal_id || !pubkey || !visibility) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // V√©rifier que l'utilisateur est bien le propri√©taire
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [seal_id]);
    if (seal.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Sceau introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    
    if (seal.rows[0].author_id !== user.rows[0].id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non autoris√©' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    // Mettre √† jour la visibilit√© (et is_public pour la compatibilit√©)
    const isPublic = visibility === 'public';
    await pool.query(
      "UPDATE seals SET visibility = $1, is_public = $2 WHERE id = $3",
      [visibility, isPublic, seal_id]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/signup/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { handle, pubkey } = await req.json();

    // 1. V√©rification des donn√©es
    if (!handle || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Pseudo et cl√© requis' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 2. Insertion
    const result = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO users (handle, pubkey) VALUES ($1, decode($2, 'base64')) RETURNING id, handle", 
      [handle, pubkey]
    );
    
    return NextResponse.json(result.rows[0]);

  } catch (error: any) {
    // Si le pseudo existe d√©j√† (Code PostgreSQL 23505 = violation d'unicit√©)
    if (error.code === '23505') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ce pseudo est d√©j√† pris' }, { status: 409 });
    }
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur lors de l\'inscription' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--- FICHIER : src/app/api/user-profile/[handle]/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ handle: string }> }) {
  const { handle } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const viewerHandle = searchParams.get('viewer');

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [handle]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    const targetUserId = user.rows[0].id;

    let relationship = 'none';
    let viewerId = null;

    if (viewerHandle) {
      const viewer = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [viewerHandle]);
      if (viewer.rows.length > 0) {
        viewerId = viewer.rows[0].id;
        const friend = await pool.query(
          "SELECT status FROM friendships WHERE (requester_id = $1 AND addressee_id = $2) OR (requester_id = $2 AND addressee_id = $1)",
          [viewerId, targetUserId]
        );
        if (friend.rows.length > 0) {
          if (friend.rows[0].status === 'accepted') relationship = 'friend';
          else relationship = 'pending';
        }
      }
    }

    let visFilter = "s.visibility = 'public'";
    if (viewerId === targetUserId) visFilter = "1=1"; 
    else if (relationship === 'friend') visFilter = "s.visibility IN ('public', 'friends')";

    const seals = await pool.query(`
      SELECT id, title, description, timestamp, is_hosted, file_path, visibility 
      FROM seals s WHERE author_id = $1 AND ${visFilter} ORDER BY created_at DESC
    `, [targetUserId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ handle: user.rows[0].handle, relationship, seals: seals.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

--- FICHIER : src/app/api/verify/route.ts ---
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { hash, query } = await req.json();
    
    // 1. Recherche par Hash (V√©rification classique)
    if (hash) {
      const result = await pool.query(`
        SELECT s.title, s.timestamp, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, u.handle as author
        FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
        WHERE encode(s.hash, 'base64') = $1
        ORDER BY s.timestamp ASC
      `, [hash]);
      if (result.rows.length > 0) return NextResponse.json({ found: true, seals: result.rows });
      return NextResponse.json({ found: false });
    }

    // 2. Recherche par Titre (D√©couverte publique)
    if (query) {
      const result = await pool.query(`
        SELECT s.title, s.timestamp, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author
        FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
        WHERE s.title ILIKE $1 AND s.is_public = TRUE
        ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 20
      `, [`%${query}%`]);
      
      return NextResponse.json({ found: result.rows.length > 0, seals: result.rows });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Requ√™te invalide' }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

@import "tailwindcss";

@layer base {
  :root {
    --bg-main: #F5E9D8;
    --bg-surface: #FFFCF7;
    --text-main: #2C1810;
    --text-muted: #A89070;
    --border-color: #EFE0CA;
  }
  
  .dark {
    --bg-main: #121212;
    --bg-surface: #1E1E1E;
    --text-main: #F5E9D8;
    --text-muted: #888;
    --border-color: #333;
  }

  body {
    background-color: var(--bg-main);
    color: var(--text-main);
    transition: background-color 0.3s, color 0.3s;
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/layout.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import './globals.css'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation'

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHandle(localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'));
    const theme = localStorage.getItem('sceau_theme');
    if (theme === 'dark') { setIsDark(true); document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); }
  }, [pathname]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    const next = !isDark;
    setIsDark(next);
    if (next) { document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); localStorage.setItem('sceau_theme', 'dark'); }
    else { document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark'); localStorage.setItem('sceau_theme', 'light'); }
  };

  return (
    <html lang="fr">
      <body className="min-h-screen pb-24">
        <nav className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-b border-[var(--border-color)] px-6 py-4 flex justify-between items-center sticky top-0 z-50">
          <Link href="/feed" className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51]">.</span></Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <button onClick={toggleTheme} className="text-xl cursor-pointer">{isDark ? '‚òÄÔ∏è' : 'üåô'}</button>
            {!handle ? (
              <Link href="/signup" className="text-sm font-bold bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg">Cr√©er un compte</Link>
            ) : (
              <Link href="/profil" className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{handle}</Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </nav>
        <main className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">{children}</main>
        <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-t border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-around py-3 z-50">
          <Link href="/feed" className="flex flex-col items-center text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]"><span className="text-xl">üåê</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono">R√©seau</span></Link>
          <Link href="/seal" className="flex flex-col items-center text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]"><span className="text-xl">‚úçÔ∏è</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono">Sceller</span></Link>
          <Link href="/verify" className="flex flex-col items-center text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]"><span className="text-xl">üîç</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono">V√©rifier</span></Link>
          <Link href="/messages" className="flex flex-col items-center text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]"><span className="text-xl">üí¨</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono">Messages</span></Link>
          <Link href="/profil" className="flex flex-col items-center text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]"><span className="text-xl">üë§</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono">Profil</span></Link>
        </div>
      </body>
    </html>
  )
}

FICHIER : src/app/page.tsx

import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Home() {
  return (
    <div className="text-center py-20">
      <h1 className="text-5xl md:text-6xl font-bold text-[#2C1810] mb-6 leading-tight" style={{fontFamily: 'Cormorant Garamond'}}>
        Prouvez que vous l'avez<br/>cr√©√© <span className="text-[#E76F51] italic">en premier.</span>
      </h1>
      <p className="text-lg text-[#7A5A3A] max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-10 italic" style={{fontFamily: 'Cormorant Garamond'}}>
        SCEAU est la couche temps du web. Scellez vos ≈ìuvres gratuitement, avant de les publier. La preuve existe. Pour toujours.
      </p>
      <div className="flex justify-center gap-4">
        <Link href="/seal" className="bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transition">
          Sceller une ≈ìuvre
        </Link>
        <Link href="/feed" className="bg-[#FFFCF7] border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] text-[#2C1810] px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:border-[#E76F51] transition">
          Explorer le r√©seau
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/signup/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { handle, pubkey } = await req.json();

    // 1. V√©rification des donn√©es
    if (!handle || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Pseudo et cl√© requis' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 2. Insertion
    const result = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO users (handle, pubkey) VALUES ($1, decode($2, 'base64')) RETURNING id, handle", 
      [handle, pubkey]
    );
    
    return NextResponse.json(result.rows[0]);

  } catch (error: any) {
    // Si le pseudo existe d√©j√† (Code PostgreSQL 23505 = violation d'unicit√©)
    if (error.code === '23505') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ce pseudo est d√©j√† pris' }, { status: 409 });
    }
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur lors de l\'inscription' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/delete/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { unlink } from 'fs/promises';
import { join } from 'path';

export async function DELETE(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const sealId = searchParams.get('id');
    const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');

    if (!sealId || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'ID ou cl√© manquant' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // 1. V√©rifier que l'utilisateur est bien le propri√©taire
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    
    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id, file_path FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [sealId]);
    if (seal.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Sceau introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    
    if (seal.rows[0].author_id !== user.rows[0].id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non autoris√©' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    // 2. Supprimer le fichier physique s'il √©tait h√©berg√©
    if (seal.rows[0].file_path) {
      try {
        const filePath = join(process.cwd(), 'public', seal.rows[0].file_path);
        await unlink(filePath); // Supprime le fichier du disque
      } catch (e) { console.log("Fichier d√©j√† supprim√© ou introuvable"); }
    }

    // 3. Supprimer la ligne de la base de donn√©es
    await pool.query("DELETE FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [sealId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/seal/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { PutObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const data = await req.formData();
    const hash = data.get('hash') as string;
    const title = data.get('title') as string;
    const description = data.get('description') as string;
    const category = data.get('category') as string;
    const isHosted = data.get('isHosted') === 'true';
    const isPublic = data.get('isPublic') === 'true';
    const pubkey = data.get('pubkey') as string;
    const file = data.get('file') as File;

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const authorId = user.rows[0].id;

    // V√©rification du quota (100 Mo)
    if (isHosted && file) {
      const quotaCheck = await pool.query("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(file_size), 0) as used FROM seals WHERE author_id = $1 AND is_hosted = true", [authorId]);
      const currentUsage = parseInt(quotaCheck.rows[0].used);
      if (currentUsage + file.size > 100 * 1024 * 1024) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Quota de 100 Mo d√©pass√©.' }, { status: 403 });
      }
    }

    let filePath = null;
    let fileSize = 0;
    
    if (isHosted && file) {
      fileSize = file.size;
      const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const filename = `${hash.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 20)}-${file.name}`;
      
      try {
        await r2Client.send(new PutObjectCommand({
          Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET,
          Key: filename,
          Body: buffer,
          ContentType: file.type,
        }));
        filePath = `${process.env.AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3}/${process.env.S3_BUCKET}/${filename}`;
      } catch (s3Error) {
        console.error("Erreur S3:", s3Error);
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "Erreur lors de l'h√©bergement du fichier" }, { status: 500 });
      }
    }

    // On utilise simplement l'heure du serveur pour le MVP
    let timestamp = new Date().toISOString();

    await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO seals (hash, timestamp, author_id, title, description, category, is_public, is_hosted, file_path, file_size) 
       VALUES (decode($1, 'base64'), $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9, $10)`,
      [hash, timestamp, authorId, title, description, category, isPublic, isHosted, filePath, fileSize]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ seal_id: Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 10), timestamp });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur interne du serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/feed/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const query = searchParams.get('q') || '';
  const category = searchParams.get('cat') || '';

  try {
    let sql = `
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.category, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.timestamp, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id) as likes_count,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM comments WHERE seal_id = s.id) as comments_count
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.is_public = TRUE
    `;
    const params: any[] = [];
    
    if (query) {
      sql += ` AND (s.title ILIKE $${params.length + 1} OR s.description ILIKE $${params.length + 1} OR u.handle ILIKE $${params.length + 1})`;
      params.push(`%${query}%`);
    }
    if (category && category !== 'Tout') {
      sql += ` AND s.category = $${params.length + 1}`;
      params.push(category);
    }

    sql += ` ORDER BY s.created_at DESC LIMIT 20`;
    const result = await pool.query(sql, params);
    return NextResponse.json({ seals: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { 
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { hash } = await req.json();
    if (!hash) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Hash manquant' }, { status: 400 });

    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.title, s.timestamp, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, u.handle as author
      FROM seals s 
      JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE encode(s.hash, 'base64') = $1
      ORDER BY s.timestamp ASC
    `, [hash]);

    if (result.rows.length > 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ found: true, seals: result.rows });
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ found: false });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FICHIER : src/app/api/profil/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cl√© manquante' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle, recovery_email FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 404 });
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;

    const stats = await pool.query(`
      SELECT COUNT(id) as total_sealed, SUM(CASE WHEN is_hosted = true THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as total_hosted
      FROM seals WHERE author_id = $1
    `, [userId]);

    const seals = await pool.query(`
      SELECT id, title, timestamp, is_hosted, file_path 
      FROM seals WHERE author_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC
    `, [userId]);

    return NextResponse.json({
      handle: user.rows[0].handle,
      recovery_email: user.rows[0].recovery_email,
      sealed: parseInt(stats.rows[0].total_sealed || 0),
      hosted: parseInt(stats.rows[0].total_hosted || 0),
      seals: seals.rows
    });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

    return NextResponse.json({ contacts: users.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { sender_handle, receiver_handle, content } = await req.json();
    const sender = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [sender_handle]);
    const receiver = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [receiver_handle]);
    
    if (sender.rows.length === 0 || receiver.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur inconnu' }, { status: 404 });

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO messages (sender_id, receiver_id, content) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)",
      [sender.rows[0].id, receiver.rows[0].id, content]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/messages/[handle]/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ handle: string }> }) {
  const { handle } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');

  try {
    const myIdResult = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
    const otherIdResult = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [handle]);
    
    if (myIdResult.rows.length === 0 || otherIdResult.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 400 });
    
    const myId = myIdResult.rows[0].id;
    const otherId = otherIdResult.rows[0].id;

    const messages = await pool.query(`
      SELECT m.content, m.created_at, u.handle as sender
      FROM messages m
      JOIN users u ON m.sender_id = u.id
      WHERE (m.sender_id = $1 AND m.receiver_id = $2) OR (m.sender_id = $2 AND m.receiver_id = $1)
      ORDER BY m.created_at ASC
    `, [myId, otherId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ messages: messages.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/signup/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { handle, email, password, pubkey } = await req.json();

    if (!handle || !email || !password || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Tous les champs sont requis' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
    const password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(password, salt);

    const result = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO users (handle, email, password_hash, pubkey) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, decode($4, 'base64')) RETURNING id, handle", 
      [handle, email, password_hash, pubkey]
    );
    
    return NextResponse.json(result.rows[0]);
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Erreur d'inscription:", error);
    if (error.code === '23505') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ce pseudo ou cet email est d√©j√† pris' }, { status: 409 });
    }
    // On renvoie le VRAI message d'erreur pour le d√©bogage
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message || 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { email, password } = await req.json();

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email ou mot de passe incorrect' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.rows[0].password_hash);
    if (!isValid) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email ou mot de passe incorrect' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    // On renvoie le handle et la cl√© publique (convertie en base64)
    return NextResponse.json({
      handle: user.rows[0].handle,
      pubkey: Buffer.from(user.rows[0].pubkey).toString('base64')
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/forgot-password/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import crypto from 'crypto';
import { Resend } from 'resend';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { email } = await req.json();
  if (!email) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email requis' }, { status: 400 });

  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

  const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
  const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 3600000); 

  await pool.query("UPDATE users SET reset_token = $1, reset_expiry = $2 WHERE email = $3", [token, expiry, email]);

  const resetLink = `${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || 'http://localhost:3000'}/reset-password?token=${token}`;
  
  try {
    const resendKey = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY;
    if (!resendKey) {
      // Si pas de cl√©, on simule l'envoi pour le d√©veloppement
      console.log("‚ö†Ô∏è Pas de cl√© Resend. Lien de test :", resetLink);
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Lien g√©n√©r√© (voir console)" });
    }

    const resend = new Resend(resendKey);
    await resend.emails.send({
      from: 'SCEAU <onboarding@resend.dev>',
      to: email,
      subject: "R√©initialisation de votre mot de passe",
      html: `<p>Bonjour,</p><p>Voici votre lien : <a href="${resetLink}">R√©initialiser mon mot de passe</a></p>`,
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Email envoy√© !" });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Erreur d'envoi d'email" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/reset-password/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { token, newPassword } = await req.json();
  if (!token || !newPassword) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs requis' }, { status: 400 });

  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE reset_token = $1 AND reset_expiry > NOW()", [token]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Lien invalide ou expir√©' }, { status: 400 });

  const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
  const password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(newPassword, salt);

  await pool.query("UPDATE users SET password_hash = $1, reset_token = NULL, reset_expiry = NULL WHERE id = $2", [password_hash, user.rows[0].id]);

  return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
}

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (e) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

FICHIER : src/app/api/pacts/create/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { PutObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const data = await req.formData();
    const title = data.get('title') as string;
    const description = data.get('description') as string;
    const pubkey = data.get('pubkey') as string;
    const file = data.get('file') as File;

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    let filePath = null;
    if (file) {
      const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const filename = `pact-${Date.now()}-${file.name}`;
      await r2Client.send(new PutObjectCommand({
        Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET,
        Key: filename,
        Body: buffer,
        ContentType: file.type,
      }));
      filePath = `${process.env.AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3}/${process.env.S3_BUCKET}/${filename}`;
    }

    const pact = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO pacts (title, description, creator_id, file_path) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4) RETURNING id",
      [title, description, user.rows[0].id, filePath]
    );
    
    // Le cr√©ateur signe automatiquement
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO pact_signatures (pact_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)", [pact.rows[0].id, user.rows[0].id]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ id: pact.rows[0].id });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/pacts/sign/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { pact_id, pubkey, receiver_handle } = await req.json();
  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    // 1. Enregistrer la signature
    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO pact_signatures (pact_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", 
      [pact_id, user.rows[0].id]
    );

    // 2. V√©rifier si le pacte est complet (le cr√©ateur + le destinataire ont sign√©)
    const signatures = await pool.query(`
      SELECT u.handle FROM pact_signatures s 
      JOIN users u ON s.user_id = u.id 
      WHERE s.pact_id = $1
    `, [pact_id]);

    const signataires = signatures.rows.map(r => r.handle);
    const creator = await pool.query("SELECT u.handle as creator FROM pacts p JOIN users u ON p.creator_id = u.id WHERE p.id = $1", [pact_id]);
    const creatorHandle = creator.rows[0].creator;

    // Si le cr√©ateur et le destinataire ont sign√©, on scelle le pacte !
    if (signataires.includes(creatorHandle) && signataires.includes(receiver_handle)) {
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      await pool.query("UPDATE pacts SET status = 'scelle', created_at = $1 WHERE id = $2", [now, pact_id]);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/pacts/[id]/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const pact = await pool.query(`
    SELECT p.*, u.handle as creator 
    FROM pacts p JOIN users u ON p.creator_id = u.id 
    WHERE p.id = $1
  `, [id]);
  
  if (pact.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

  const signatures = await pool.query("SELECT u.handle FROM pact_signatures s JOIN users u ON s.user_id = u.id WHERE s.pact_id = $1", [id]);
  
  return NextResponse.json({ 
    ...pact.rows[0], 
    signataires: signatures.rows.map(r => r.handle) 
  });
}

FICHIER : src/app/signup/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { ed25519 } from '@noble/curves/ed25519';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Signup() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleSignup() {
    if (!handle || !email || !password) return setStatus("Tous les champs sont requis");
    setStatus("Cr√©ation en cours...");
    
    try {
      const secret = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
      const pubkey = ed25519.getPublicKey(secret);
      let pubkeyStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < pubkey.length; i++) pubkeyStr += String.fromCharCode(pubkey[i]);
      let secretStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < secret.length; i++) secretStr += String.fromCharCode(secret[i]);
      const pubkeyB64 = btoa(pubkeyStr);

      localStorage.setItem('sceau_secret', btoa(secretStr));

      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/signup', { 
        method: 'POST', 
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
        body: JSON.stringify({ handle, email, password, pubkey: pubkeyB64 }) 
      });
      
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (res.ok) {
        localStorage.setItem('sceau_pubkey', pubkeyB64);
        localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', handle);
        setStatus("‚úÖ Compte cr√©√© !");
        setTimeout(() => router.push('/feed'), 500);
      } else {
        setStatus("‚ùå " + (data.error || "Une erreur est survenue"));
      }
    } catch (e) { setStatus("‚ùå Impossible de contacter le serveur. V√©rifiez votre connexion."); }
  }

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSignup(); };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cr√©er un compte</h1>
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Pseudo</label>
      <input type="text" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={handle} onChange={(e) => setHandle(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Email</label>
      <input type="email" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Mot de passe</label>
      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-3 opacity-50">{showPassword ? "üôà" : "üëÅÔ∏è"}</button>
      </div>
      
      <button onClick={handleSignup} className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white p-4 rounded-xl font-bold">S'inscrire</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      
      <p className="text-center mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">D√©j√† un compte ? <Link href="/login" className="underline font-bold">Se connecter</Link></p>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : src/app/seal/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export default function Seal() {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'file' | 'text' | 'media'>('file');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [textContent, setTextContent] = useState('');
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState('');
  const [cat, setCat] = useState('Art');
  const [isHosted, setIsHosted] = useState(true);
  const [isPublic, setIsPublic] = useState(true);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleSeal() {
    if (!title) return setStatus("Le titre est requis");
    if (mode === 'file' && !file) return setStatus("Choisis un fichier");
    if (mode === 'text' && !textContent) return setStatus("√âcris quelque chose");
    if (mode === 'media' && !file) return setStatus("Prends une photo ou vid√©o");

    setStatus("Calcul de l'empreinte...");
    
    try {
      let fileToSeal = file;

      if (mode === 'text') {
        const blob = new Blob([textContent], { type: 'text/plain' });
        fileToSeal = new File([blob], `${title.replace(/\s+/g, '-').toLowerCase()}.txt`, { type: 'text/plain' });
      }

      if (!fileToSeal) return;

      const buffer = await fileToSeal.arrayBuffer();
      const hash = sha256(new Uint8Array(buffer));
      let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);
      const hashB64 = btoa(hashStr);
      
      const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
      if (!pubkey) return setStatus("Cr√©ez un compte d'abord !");

      setStatus("Ancrage dans le r√©seau Bitcoin (OpenTimestamps)...");

      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('hash', hashB64);
      formData.append('title', title);
      formData.append('description', desc);
      formData.append('category', cat);
      formData.append('isHosted', isHosted.toString());
      formData.append('isPublic', isPublic.toString());
      formData.append('pubkey', pubkey);
      formData.append('file', fileToSeal);

      const res = await fetch('/api/seal', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (res.ok) {
        setStatus("‚úÖ Scell√© ! Preuve OpenTimestamps g√©n√©r√©e.");
        setTitle(''); setDesc(''); setFile(null); setTextContent('');
      } else {
        setStatus("‚ùå Erreur : " + data.error);
      }
    } catch (e) { setStatus("‚ùå Erreur syst√®me"); }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 font-serif">
      <div className="text-center mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>L'Atelier.</h1>
        <p className="italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Cr√©ez et scellez en un clic.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4 max-w-md mx-auto bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">
        
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4 text-xs font-mono">
          <button onClick={() => setMode('file')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'file' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üìÅ Fichier</button>
          <button onClick={() => setMode('text')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'text' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>‚úçÔ∏è Texte</button>
          <button onClick={() => setMode('media')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'media' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üì∏ Direct</button>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Titre</label>
          <input type="text" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={title} onChange={e=>setTitle(e.target.value)} />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Description</label>
          <textarea className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent" rows={2} value={desc} onChange={e=>setDesc(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Cat√©gorie</label>
            <select className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={cat} onChange={e=>setCat(e.target.value)}>
              <option>Art</option><option>Musique</option><option>Code</option><option>√âcrit</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">{mode === 'media' ? 'Capture' : mode === 'file' ? 'Fichier' : 'Texte'}</label>
            {mode === 'file' && (
              <input type="file" className="w-full text-sm mt-2 opacity-80" onChange={e=>setFile(e.target.files?.[0]||null)} />
            )}
            {mode === 'media' && (
              <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" capture="environment" className="w-full text-sm mt-2 opacity-80" onChange={e=>setFile(e.target.files?.[0]||null)} />
            )}
            {mode === 'text' && <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2">Zone texte ‚Üì</div>}
          </div>
        </div>

        {mode === 'text' && (
          <div className="mt-2">
            <textarea className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-4 outline-none bg-transparent min-h-[150px] font-mono text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} placeholder="√âcrivez votre chef-d'≈ìuvre ici..." value={textContent} onChange={e=>setTextContent(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] space-y-2 mt-4">
          <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={isHosted} onChange={e=>setIsHosted(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
            <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>H√©berger le fichier</span>
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={isPublic} onChange={e=>setIsPublic(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
            <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Rendre public (Feed)</span>
          </label>
        </div>
        
        <button onClick={handleSeal} className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white p-4 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg mt-4">
          SCELLER MON ≈íUVRE
        </button>
        {status && <p className="text-center font-bold mt-4 text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { QRCodeCanvas } from 'qrcode.react';
import { generateCertificate } from '@/lib/pdf';

export default function Feed() {
  const [seals, setSeals] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedSeal, setSelectedSeal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const qrRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/feed').then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
      setSeals(data.seals || []);
      setLoading(false);
    });
  }, []);

  const downloadPdf = () => {
    if (!selectedSeal || !qrRef.current) return;
    const canvas = qrRef.current.querySelector('canvas');
    const qrUrl = canvas ? canvas.toDataURL() : '';
    generateCertificate(selectedSeal.title, selectedSeal.author, selectedSeal.timestamp, selectedSeal.hash, qrUrl);
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen p-6 font-serif max-w-3xl mx-auto">
      <h1 className="text-4xl font-black mb-8" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51] italic">.</span></h1>
      
      {loading ? (
         <p className="text-center font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Chargement du r√©seau...</p>
      ) : seals.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-10 rounded-3xl mt-10 shadow-sm">
          <div className="text-5xl mb-4">üå±</div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Le r√©seau attend sa premi√®re ≈ìuvre.</h2>
          <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-6 italic">Personne n'a encore publi√© de cr√©ation.</p>
          <Link href="/seal" className="inline-block bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-[#F4A261] transition">
            Sceller ma premi√®re ≈ìuvre
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="grid gap-6 mb-10">
            {seals.map((seal) => (
              <div key={seal.id} onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(seal)} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                  <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</h2>
                  {seal.is_hosted && <span className="text-xs font-mono bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] px-2 py-1 rounded">üìÅ H√©berg√©</span>}
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2">
                  Par <Link href={`/u/${seal.author}`} onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} className="font-bold text-[#E76F51] hover:underline">@{seal.author}</Link> ‚Ä¢ {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}
                </p>
                {seal.description && <p className="text-sm italic mb-4 opacity-80" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>"{seal.description}"</p>}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          {/* Le fameux message d'encouragement en bas */}
          <div className="text-center p-6 border-2 border-dashed border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl">
            <p className="italic text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">Tu as aim√© ces cr√©ations ?</p>
            <Link href="/seal" className="inline-block bg-[#2C1810] text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:opacity-80 transition">
              Sceller une ≈ìuvre √† mon tour
            </Link>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {selectedSeal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(null)}>
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-lg rounded-3xl p-8 relative max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <button onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(null)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-2xl opacity-50">‚úï</button>
            
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{selectedSeal.title}</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-6">Par @{selectedSeal.author}</p>
            
            <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] mb-6">
              <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1">Empreinte SHA-256</p>
              <p className="text-xs font-mono break-all" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{selectedSeal.hash}</p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 mb-4">
              {selectedSeal.is_hosted && (
                <a href={selectedSeal.file_path} target="_blank" className="w-full text-center bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-[#F4A261] transition">
                  üìÇ Ouvrir l'≈ìuvre originale
                </a>
              )}
              <button onClick={downloadPdf} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] py-3 rounded-xl font-bold opacity-80 hover:opacity-100 transition" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                üìÑ T√©l√©charger le Certificat PDF
              </button>
            </div>

            <div ref={qrRef} className="hidden">
              <QRCodeCanvas value={`https://sceau-app.vercel.app/verify?hash=${encodeURIComponent(selectedSeal.hash)}`} size={256} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}


=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/verify/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export default function Verify() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('file');
  const [result, setResult] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function verifyFile(file: File) {
    setLoading(true);
    const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
    const hash = sha256(new Uint8Array(buffer));
    let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash: btoa(hashStr) }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  async function verifyHash(input: string) {
    if(!input) return; setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash: input }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2 text-center" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>V√©rifier.</h1>
      <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-6 text-center">Prouvez l'authenticit√©.</p>
      
      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-6">
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('file');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='file'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>√Ä partir du fichier</button>
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('hash');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='hash'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>√Ä partir du Hash</button>
      </div>

      {tab === 'file' ? (
        <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-[#4A90C2] bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-xl text-center">
          <input type="file" onChange={e=>e.target.files?.[0] && verifyFile(e.target.files[0])} className="w-full text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <input type="text" placeholder="Collez le hash (ex: b2e7...)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none font-mono bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} onChange={e=>verifyHash(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      )}

      {loading && <p className="text-center mt-4 font-mono">Recherche...</p>}
      
      {result && (
        <div className={`mt-8 p-6 rounded-2xl border-2 ${result.found ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-[#7A9B5A]' : 'bg-[#FDF4F0] border-[#D64545]'}`}>
          {result.found ? (
            <div>
              <div className="text-center mb-6">
                <div className="text-3xl mb-2">‚úÖ</div>
                <h2 className="font-bold text-xl" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>FICHIER AUTHENTIQUE</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">{result.seals.length} preuve(s) trouv√©e(s)</p>
              </div>
              
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {result.seals.map((seal: any, index: number) => (
                  <div key={index} className={`p-4 rounded-xl border ${index === 0 ? 'border-[#E76F51] bg-[var(--bg-main)]' : 'border-[var(--border-color)]'}`}>
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">
                          Scell√© par <span className="font-bold text-[#E76F51]">@{seal.author}</span> le {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      {index === 0 && <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] px-2 py-1 rounded">PREMIER</span>}
                    </div>
                    {seal.is_hosted && (
                      <a href={seal.file_path} target="_blank" className="mt-3 inline-block bg-[#4A90C2] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">‚¨áÔ∏è T√©l√©charger l'≈ìuvre</a>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-center font-bold text-[#A02E2E]">‚ùå Fichier inconnu. Il n'est pas dans le registre SCEAU.</p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
