'use client';
import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';

export default function Messages() {
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState('');
  const [activeContact, setActiveContact] = useState<any>(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('');
  
  // √âtats pour les pactes
  const [showPactModal, setShowPactModal] = useState(false);
  const [pactTitle, setPactTitle] = useState('');
  const [pactDesc, setPactDesc] = useState('');
  const [pactFile, setPactFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [activePact, setActivePact] = useState<any>(null);

  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Charger les contacts au d√©marrage
  useEffect(() => {
    setMyHandle(localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle') || '');
    fetch('/api/messages').then(r => r.json()).then(d => setContacts(d.contacts || []));
  }, []);

  // Rafra√Æchissement automatique (toutes les 2 secondes)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeContact || !myHandle) return;
    
    const load = () => {
      fetch(`/api/messages/${activeContact.handle}?me=${myHandle}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(data => {
          setMessages(data.messages || []);
        });
    };
    
    load(); // Premier chargement imm√©diat
    const interval = setInterval(load, 2000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [activeContact, myHandle]);

  // Scroll automatique vers le bas quand un nouveau message arrive
  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
  }, [messages]);

  async function sendMessage() {
    if (!newMessage.trim() || !activeContact) return;
    
    const msgToSend = newMessage;
    setNewMessage(''); // On vide le champ tout de suite
    
    // MISE √Ä JOUR OPTIMISTE : On l'affiche direct chez soi
    const optimisticMsg = { 
      content: msgToSend, 
      sender: myHandle, 
      created_at: new Date().toISOString() 
    };
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, optimisticMsg]);

    // On envoie au serveur en arri√®re-plan
    await fetch('/api/messages', { 
      method: 'POST', 
      body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: myHandle, receiver_handle: activeContact.handle, content: msgToSend }) 
    });
  }

  async function createPact() {
    if (!pactTitle) return;
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('title', pactTitle);
    formData.append('description', pactDesc);
    formData.append('pubkey', pubkey!);
    if (pactFile) formData.append('file', pactFile);

    const res = await fetch('/api/pacts/create', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: myHandle, receiver_handle: activeContact.handle, content: `[PACTE]${data.id}|${pactTitle}` }) });
    
    setShowPactModal(false);
    setPactTitle(''); setPactDesc(''); setPactFile(null);
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 h-[calc(100vh-140px)] flex gap-4 font-serif max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      {/* LISTE CONTACTS */}
      <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-4 overflow-y-auto ${activeContact ? 'hidden md:block md:w-1/3' : 'w-full md:w-1/3'}`}>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Messages</h1>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {contacts.filter(c => c.handle !== myHandle).map(c => (
            <div key={c.id} onClick={() => setActiveContact(c)} className="p-3 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm">{c.handle[0].toUpperCase()}</div>
              <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{c.handle}</div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* CHAT */}
      {activeContact ? (
        <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl flex flex-col overflow-hidden ${activeContact ? 'w-full md:w-2/3' : 'hidden md:flex'}`}>
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-center">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button onClick={() => setActiveContact(null)} className="md:hidden text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>‚Üê</button>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{activeContact.handle}</h2>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => setShowPactModal(true)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg">+ Pacte</button>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto space-y-3">
            {messages.map((m, i) => {
              if (m.content.startsWith('[PACTE]')) {
                const [id, title] = m.content.replace('[PACTE]', '').split('|');
                return (
                  <div key={i} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] border-2 border-[#E76F51] p-4 rounded-xl text-center my-4">
                    <div className="font-bold text-lg mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>ü§ù {title}</div>
                    <button onClick={() => setActivePact(id)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">Voir / Signer le Pacte</button>
                  </div>
                );
              }
              return (
                <div key={i} className={`max-w-[80%] p-3 rounded-xl text-sm ${m.sender === myHandle ? 'bg-[#E76F51] text-white ml-auto rounded-br-none' : 'bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-bl-none'}`} style={{color: m.sender === myHandle ? '#FFF' : 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                  {m.content}
                </div>
              );
            })}
            <div ref={messagesEndRef} /> {/* Ancre pour le scroll */}
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] flex gap-2">
            <input type="text" value={newMessage} onChange={e => setNewMessage(e.target.value)} placeholder="√âcrire..." className="flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && sendMessage()} />
            <button onClick={sendMessage} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 rounded-xl font-bold">‚Üí</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="hidden md:flex flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl items-center justify-center text-[var(--text-muted)]">
          S√©lectionnez un contact.
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODALE CR√âATION PACTE */}
      {showPactModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cr√©er un Pacte</h2>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Titre de l'accord" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={pactTitle} onChange={e => setPactTitle(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea placeholder="D√©tails du contrat..." className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={pactDesc} onChange={e => setPactDesc(e.target.value)} />
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1">Fichier joint (optionnel)</label>
            <input type="file" className="w-full mb-4 text-sm" onChange={e => setPactFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowPactModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={createPact} className="flex-1 bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Envoyer</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODALE SIGNATURE PACTE */}
      {activePact && <PactModal pactId={activePact} receiverHandle={activeContact.handle} onClose={() => setActivePact(null)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function PactModal({ pactId, receiverHandle, onClose }: { pactId: string, receiverHandle: string, onClose: () => void }) {
  const [pact, setPact] = useState<any>(null);
  
  const loadPact = () => fetch(`/api/pacts/${pactId}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setPact(d));
  useEffect(() => { loadPact(); }, [pactId]);

  async function signPact() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    await fetch('/api/pacts/sign', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ pact_id: pactId, pubkey, receiver_handle: receiverHandle }) });
    loadPact();
  }

  if (!pact) return null;
  const myHandle = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
  const hasSigned = pact.signataires.includes(myHandle);
  const isSealed = pact.status === 'scelle';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-8 border-2 ${isSealed ? 'border-[#7A9B5A]' : 'border-[#E76F51]'}`}>
        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{pact.title}</h2>
          {isSealed && <span className="bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-bold">üîí SCELL√â</span>}
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm mb-4 text-[var(--text-muted)]">Cr√©√© par @{pact.creator}</p>
        <p className="italic mb-4 p-4 bg-[var(--bg-main)] rounded-xl" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{pact.description}</p>
        
        {pact.file_path && (
          <a href={pact.file_path} target="_blank" className="block text-center bg-[#2C1810] text-white py-2 rounded-xl mb-6 font-bold text-sm">
            üìÇ T√©l√©charger le document
          </a>
        )}
        
        <div className="mb-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Signatures :</h3>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {pact.signataires.map((s: string) => (
              <span key={s} className="bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] border border-[#A8C77E] px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold">‚úÖ @{s}</span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button onClick={onClose} className="flex-1 py-3 font-bold opacity-50" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Fermer</button>
          {!hasSigned && !isSealed && <button onClick={signPact} className="flex-1 bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold">‚úçÔ∏è Contresigner</button>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : src/app/profil/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export default function Profil() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  async function loadProfil() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) { router.push('/login'); return; }
    
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/profil?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`);
      const d = await res.json();
      
      if (res.ok && d.handle) {
        setData({ ...d, seals: d.seals || [] }); // S√©curit√© si seals est null
      } else {
        setError("Profil introuvable. D√©connecte-toi et reconnecte-toi.");
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setError("Erreur de connexion au serveur.");
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => { loadProfil(); }, [router]);

  async function deleteSeal(id: string) {
    if (!confirm("Supprimer cette ≈ìuvre ?")) return;
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    await fetch(`/api/seal/delete?id=${id}&pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey!)}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    loadProfil();
  }

  if (error) return (
    <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">
      <p className="text-red-500 font-bold mb-4">{error}</p>
      <button onClick={() => { localStorage.clear(); router.push('/login'); }} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Se d√©connecter</button>
    </div>
  );
  
  if (!data) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Chargement...</div>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif">
      <div className="text-center mb-8">
        <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-3xl mx-auto mb-4 shadow-lg">
          {data.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-1" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{data.handle}</h1>
        <p className="text-sm font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">Identit√© souveraine</p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-6 rounded-2xl justify-around mb-8 shadow-sm">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.sealed}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">Scell√©s</div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.hosted}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">H√©berg√©s</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üìö Mon Coffre</h2>
      <div className="space-y-3 mb-10">
        {data.seals.length === 0 && <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic text-sm p-4 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl text-center">Ton coffre est vide.</p>}
        {data.seals.map((seal: any) => (
          <div key={seal.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-center shadow-sm">
            <div>
              <div className="font-bold text-lg" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] font-mono mt-1">{new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</div>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => deleteSeal(seal.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 p-2 text-xl">üóëÔ∏è</button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <button onClick={() => { localStorage.clear(); router.push('/login'); }} className="w-full py-4 text-red-500 font-bold border border-red-500 rounded-xl hover:bg-red-50 transition">
        Se d√©connecter
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : src/app/login/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Login() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleLogin() {
    if (!email || !password) return setStatus("Email et mot de passe requis");
    setStatus("Connexion...");
    
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    if (res.ok) {
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_pubkey', data.pubkey);
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', data.handle);
      setStatus("‚úÖ Connect√© !");
      setTimeout(() => router.push('/feed'), 500);
    } else {
      setStatus("‚ùå " + data.error);
    }
  }

  // La fonction magique pour "Entr√©e"
  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleLogin(); };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Se connecter</h1>
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Email</label>
      <input type="email" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-1 font-mono">Mot de passe</label>
      <div className="relative mb-2">
        <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-3 opacity-50">{showPassword ? "üôà" : "üëÅÔ∏è"}</button>
      </div>
      
      <div className="text-right mb-6">
        <Link href="/forgot-password" className="text-sm text-[#E76F51] hover:underline font-bold">Mot de passe oubli√© ?</Link>
      </div>
      
      <button onClick={handleLogin} className="w-full bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl font-bold">Connexion</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      
      <p className="text-center mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Pas de compte ? <Link href="/signup" className="underline font-bold">Cr√©er un compte</Link></p>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [link, setLink] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  async function handleReset() {
    setLink(''); setError('');
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/forgot-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ email }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) setLink(data.link);
    else setError(data.error);
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[#FFFCF7] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[#EFE0CA] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4 font-serif">Mot de passe oubli√©</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-[#7A5A3A] mb-6">Entre ton email pour recevoir un lien de r√©initialisation.</p>
      
      <input type="email" placeholder="Ton email" className="w-full border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4" value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={handleReset} className="w-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold mb-4">Envoyer le lien</button>
      
      {link && (
        <div className="bg-[#F5F9EE] border border-[#7A9B5A] p-4 rounded-xl text-sm break-all">
          <p className="font-bold text-[#5D7A3A] mb-2">‚úÖ (Mode Test) Voici ton lien :</p>
          <a href={link} className="text-blue-600 underline">{link}</a>
        </div>
      )}
      {error && <p className="text-red-500 font-bold text-center">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : src/app/reset-password/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState, Suspense } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

function ResetForm() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const token = searchParams.get('token');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleReset() {
    if (!password) return setStatus('Mot de passe requis');
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/reset-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ token, newPassword: password }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) {
      setStatus('‚úÖ Mot de passe modifi√© !');
      setTimeout(() => router.push('/login'), 1500);
    } else setStatus('‚ùå ' + data.error);
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[#FFFCF7] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[#EFE0CA] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif">Nouveau mot de passe</h1>
      <div className="relative mb-4">
        <input type={show ? "text" : "password"} placeholder="Nouveau mot de passe" className="w-full border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10" value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button onClick={() => setShow(!show)} className="absolute right-3 top-3">{show ? "üôà" : "üëÅÔ∏è"}</button>
      </div>
      <button onClick={handleReset} className="w-full bg-[#2C1810] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold">Valider</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold">{status}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ResetPassword() {
  return <Suspense fallback={<div>Chargement...</div>}><ResetForm /></Suspense>;
}

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';

export default function PublicProfile() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (params.handle) {
      fetch(`/api/user-profile/${params.handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setData(d));
    }
  }, [params.handle]);

  if (!data) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;
  if (data.error) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">{data.error}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <div className="text-center mb-10">
        <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-3xl mx-auto mb-4">
          {data.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold font-serif">@{data.handle}</h1>
        <p className="text-sm font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">Profil Public</p>
      </div>

      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold font-serif mb-6 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] pb-2">Timeline</h2>
      
      <div className="border-l-2 border-[var(--border-color)] pl-6 space-y-8 ml-4">
        {data.seals.map((seal: any) => (
          <div key={seal.id} className="relative">
            <div className="absolute -left-[31px] top-2 w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-[#E76F51]"></div>
            <div className="text-sm font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1">{new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</div>
            <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold">{seal.title}</h3>
              {seal.description && <p className="text-sm italic opacity-80 mt-1">{seal.description}</p>}
              {seal.is_hosted && <span className="inline-block mt-2 text-xs bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] px-2 py-1 rounded">üìÅ Fichier h√©berg√©</span>}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
        {data.seals.length === 0 && <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Aucune ≈ìuvre publique.</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export const pool = new Pool({
  connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL_POOLED || process.env.DATABASE_URL,
  ssl: {
    rejectUnauthorized: false // Force l'acceptation du certificat SSL de Neon
  }

FICHIER : src/db/schema.sql

-- 1. La table des utilisateurs
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  handle TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  pubkey BYTEA NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 2. La table des sceaux (mise √† jour avec tes nouvelles id√©es)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS seals (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  hash BYTEA NOT NULL,
  timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  author_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  title TEXT,
  category TEXT DEFAULT 'Art',
  is_public BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
  is_hosted BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  file_path TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  CONSTRAINT unique_seal UNIQUE (hash, author_id)
);

FICHIER : src/lib/pdf.ts
=========================================

import { jsPDF } from "jspdf";

export function generateCertificate(title: string, author: string, date: string, hash: string, qrCodeDataUrl: string) {
  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: 'landscape', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });

  // Fond
  doc.setFillColor(253, 248, 240);
  doc.rect(0, 0, 297, 210, 'F');

  // Bordure
  doc.setDrawColor(231, 111, 81);
  doc.setLineWidth(2);
  doc.rect(10, 10, 277, 190);

  // Titre
  doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold");
  doc.setFontSize(36);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("CERTIFICAT D'ANT√âRIORIT√â", 148.5, 40, { align: "center" });

  doc.setFontSize(16);
  doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  doc.text("D√©livr√© par le protocole SCEAU", 148.5, 50, { align: "center" });

  // Infos
  doc.setFontSize(14);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text(`Titre : ${title}`, 30, 80);
  doc.text(`Cr√©ateur : @${author}`, 30, 90);
  doc.text(`Date de scellement : ${new Date(date).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}`, 30, 100);

  // Hash (coup√© pour pas d√©border)
  doc.setFont("courier", "normal");
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.text(`Empreinte SHA-256 :`, 30, 120);
  doc.text(hash.slice(0, 80), 30, 125);
  doc.text(hash.slice(80), 30, 130);

  // QR Code (√† droite)
  doc.addImage(qrCodeDataUrl, 'PNG', 210, 70, 60, 60);

  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.text("Scannez pour v√©rifier", 240, 140, { align: "center" });

  doc.save(`SCEAU-${title.replace(/\s+/g, '-')}.pdf`);
}

    accessKeyId: process.env.AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID || "",
    secretAccessKey: process.env.AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY || "",
  },
  forcePathStyle: true, // N√©cessaire pour certains stockages compatibles S3
});

#!/bin/bash
OUTPUT="dossier_complet_pour_ia.txt"
echo "G√©n√©ration du fichier de code..." > $OUTPUT
find src public *.json *.ts *.mjs *.css *.env* -type f ! -path "*/node_modules/*" ! -path "*/.next/*" ! -name "package-lock.json" | while read file; do
  echo -e "\n\n=========================================\nFICHIER : $file\n=========================================\n" >> $OUTPUT
  cat "$file" >> $OUTPUT
done
echo "‚úÖ Fichier '$OUTPUT' g√©n√©r√© avec succ√®s !"

# üèõÔ∏è SCEAU Protocol (v1.0)

Le protocole de preuve d'ant√©riorit√© de SCEAU.
Permet d'ancrer l'empreinte cryptographique d'un fichier sur la blockchain Bitcoin.

## üöÄ Utilisation via l'API publique (Terminal)

Vous pouvez utiliser l'API REST gratuite pour sceller un document instantan√©ment avec `curl` :

```bash
curl -X POST https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/v1/public/seal \
  -F "file=@mon-document.pdf"

# SCEAU Protocol ‚Äî Architecture G√©n√©rale

## 1. Le Principe
SCEAU est un protocole de preuve d'ant√©riorit√©. Il s√©pare strictement **la preuve** (le hash et son ancrage) de **l'objet** (le fichier).

## 2. Les 3 Composants
1. **Le Hash (SHA-256)** : Une empreinte unique du fichier. Si un seul bit change, le hash change.
2. **L'Ancrage (OpenTimestamps)** : Le hash est envoy√© √† des serveurs "Calendar" qui l'agr√®gent et l'√©crivent dans la blockchain Bitcoin.
3. **La Preuve (.ots)** : Un fichier cryptographique t√©l√©chargeable qui prouve math√©matiquement que le hash existait au moment du bloc Bitcoin.

=========================================
FICHIER : sceau-protocol/spec/02-stockage.md
=========================================

# SCEAU Protocol ‚Äî Le Mod√®le de Stockage

Le protocole ne stocke que des hashes. O√π est le fichier ? Il y a 3 fa√ßons de faire selon le besoin.

## Mod√®le A : Stockage par l'Application SCEAU (Centralis√©)
L'utilisateur d√©pose le fichier sur SCEAU. L'application calcule le hash, l'ancre sur Bitcoin, et stocke le fichier sur un serveur cloud (S3/R2).
* Lien de v√©rification : L'application associe le hash (Bitcoin) au `file_path` (le serveur cloud) dans sa base de donn√©es.

## Mod√®le B : Stockage par l'API (D√©veloppeurs)
Un site externe utilise l'API SCEAU. Il envoie son fichier, re√ßoit le hash et la preuve `.ots`.
* Lien de v√©rification : C'est le site externe qui h√©berge le fichier sur son propre serveur.

## Mod√®le C : Le Web3 et IPFS (D√©centralis√©)
Le fichier est stock√© sur le r√©seau IPFS. Sur IPFS, le nom du fichier EST son hash cryptographique.
* Lien de v√©rification : Le hash SCEAU (Bitcoin) et le hash IPFS du fichier sont associ√©s.

# SCEAU Protocol ‚Äî API Publique v1

L'API permet √† n'importe quel site web de sceller un document sans authentification.

## Endpoint
`POST https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/v1/public/seal`

## Param√®tres (Form-Data)
- `file` (obligatoire) : Le fichier binaire √† sceller.

## R√©ponse (JSON)
```json
{
  "success": true,
  "protocol": "SCEAU v1.0",
  "document": {
    "name": "mon-contrat.pdf",
    "hash_sha256": "4b227777d4dd1..."
  },
  "bitcoin_anchor": {
    "secured": true,
    "ots_proof_base64": "AE9wZW5UaW1lc3RhbXBz..."
  }
}

=========================================
FICHIER : sceau-protocol/src/index.ts
=========================================

import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

const CALENDARS = [
  'https://alice.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://bob.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://finney.calendar.eternitywall.com',
];

/**
 * Calcule l'empreinte SHA-256 d'un fichier (Buffer ou Uint8Array).
 */
export function hashDocument(content: Uint8Array): string {
  const hash = sha256(content);
  return Buffer.from(hash).toString('hex');
}

/**
 * Ancre un hash sur la blockchain Bitcoin via OpenTimestamps.
 * Retourne la preuve .ots en base64, pr√™te √† √™tre stock√©e.
 */
export async function anchorToBitcoin(hashHex: string): Promise<{ otsProof: string | null, calendars: string[] }> {
  const hashBytes = Buffer.from(hashHex, 'hex');
  const successes: string[] = [];
  let proof: Buffer | undefined;

  for (const url of CALENDARS) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${url}/digest`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream' },
        body: hashBytes,
      });
      if (!res.ok) continue;

      const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
      const magic = Buffer.from([0x00,0x4f,0x70,0x65,0x6e,0x54,0x69,0x6d,0x65,0x73,0x74,0x61,0x6d,0x70,0x73,0x00,0x00,0x50,0x72,0x6f,0x6f,0x66,0x00]);
      
      if (!buf.subarray(0, magic.length).equals(magic)) continue;

      successes.push(url);
      if (!proof) proof = buf;
    } catch (e) {
      // Calendar indisponible, on tente le suivant
    }
  }

  return {
    otsProof: proof ? proof.toString('base64') : null,
    calendars: successes
  };
}

FICHIER : sdks/php/sceau.php
=========================================

<?php
class SceauClient {
    private $apiUrl = "https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/v1/public/seal";

    public function sealFile($filePath) {
        $cFile = new CURLFile($filePath);
        $data = array('file' => $cFile);
        
        $ch = curl_init();
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_URL, $this->apiUrl);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_POST, 1);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, $data);
        curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER, true);
        
        $response = curl_exec($ch);
        curl_close($ch);
        
        return json_decode($response, true);
    }
}
?>

=========================================
FICHIER : sdks/python/sceau.py
=========================================

import requests

class SceauClient:
    def __init__(self, api_url="https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/v1/public/seal"):
        self.api_url = api_url

    def seal_file(self, file_path):
        with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
            response = requests.post(self.api_url, files={'file': f})
        return response.json()

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const files = {
  'src/db/pool.ts': `import { Pool } from 'pg';
export const pool = new Pool({ connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL });`,

  'src/db/schema.sql': `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  handle TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  pubkey BYTEA NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS seals (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  hash BYTEA NOT NULL,
  timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  author_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  title TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  CONSTRAINT unique_seal UNIQUE (hash, author_id)
);`,

  'src/app/api/signup/route.ts': `import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { handle, pubkey } = await req.json();
    const result = await pool.query("INSERT INTO users (handle, pubkey) VALUES ($1, decode($2, 'base64')) RETURNING id, handle", [handle, pubkey]);
    return NextResponse.json(result.rows[0]);
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}`,

  'src/app/api/seal/route.ts': `import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { hash, title, pubkey } = await req.json();
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const protocolRes = await fetch('http://localhost:8080/v1/seal', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash, title }) });
    const protocolData = await protocolRes.json();
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO seals (hash, timestamp, author_id, title) VALUES (decode($1, 'base64'), $2, $3, $4)", [hash, protocolData.timestamp, user.rows[0].id, title]);
    return NextResponse.json(protocolData);
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}`,

  'src/app/signup/page.tsx': `'use client';

export default function Signup() {
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState('');

  async function handleSignup() {
    if (!handle) { setStatus("Pseudo requis"); return; }

export default function Seal() {

  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const sealId = searchParams.get('seal_id');
  if (!sealId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Manquant' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT a.id, a.type, a.content, a.media_url, a.created_at, u.handle as author
      FROM attachments a JOIN users u ON a.user_id = u.id
      WHERE a.seal_id = $1 ORDER BY a.created_at ASC
    `, [sealId]);
    return NextResponse.json({ attachments: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, content, type, media_url } = await req.json();
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO attachments (seal_id, user_id, content, type, media_url) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)",
      [seal_id, user.rows[0].id, content, type || 'text', media_url || null]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/forgot-password/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import crypto from 'crypto';
import { Resend } from 'resend';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { email } = await req.json();
  if (!email) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email requis' }, { status: 400 });

  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

  const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
  const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 3600000); 

  await pool.query("UPDATE users SET reset_token = $1, reset_expiry = $2 WHERE email = $3", [token, expiry, email]);

  const resetLink = `${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || 'http://localhost:3000'}/reset-password?token=${token}`;
  
  try {
    const resendKey = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY;
    if (!resendKey) {
      // Si pas de cl√©, on simule l'envoi pour le d√©veloppement
      console.log("‚ö†Ô∏è Pas de cl√© Resend. Lien de test :", resetLink);
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Lien g√©n√©r√© (voir console)" });
    }

    const resend = new Resend(resendKey);
    await resend.emails.send({
      from: 'SCEAU <onboarding@resend.dev>',
      to: email,
      subject: "R√©initialisation de votre mot de passe",
      html: `<p>Bonjour,</p><p>Voici votre lien : <a href="${resetLink}">R√©initialiser mon mot de passe</a></p>`,
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Email envoy√© !" });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Erreur d'envoi d'email" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { email, password } = await req.json();

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email ou mot de passe incorrect' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.rows[0].password_hash);
    if (!isValid) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email ou mot de passe incorrect' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    // On renvoie le handle et la cl√© publique (convertie en base64)
    return NextResponse.json({
      handle: user.rows[0].handle,
      pubkey: Buffer.from(user.rows[0].pubkey).toString('base64')
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/reset-password/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { token, newPassword } = await req.json();
  if (!token || !newPassword) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs requis' }, { status: 400 });

  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE reset_token = $1 AND reset_expiry > NOW()", [token]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Lien invalide ou expir√©' }, { status: 400 });

  const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
  const password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(newPassword, salt);

  await pool.query("UPDATE users SET password_hash = $1, reset_token = NULL, reset_expiry = NULL WHERE id = $2", [password_hash, user.rows[0].id]);

  return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/auth/signup/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { handle, email, password, pubkey } = await req.json();

    if (!handle || !email || !password || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Tous les champs sont requis' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
    const password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(password, salt);

    const result = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO users (handle, email, password_hash, pubkey) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, decode($4, 'base64')) RETURNING id, handle", 
      [handle, email, password_hash, pubkey]
    );
    
    return NextResponse.json(result.rows[0]);
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Erreur d'inscription:", error);
    if (error.code === '23505') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ce pseudo ou cet email est d√©j√† pris' }, { status: 409 });
    }
    // On renvoie le VRAI message d'erreur pour le d√©bogage
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message || 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FICHIER : src/app/api/challenges/[id]/route.ts

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  try {
    const challenge = await pool.query("SELECT * FROM challenges WHERE id = $1", [id]);
    if (challenge.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    const entries = await pool.query(`
      SELECT e.id as entry_id, s.id as seal_id, s.title, s.description, s.file_path, s.category, 
      CASE WHEN $2 = true THEN 'Anonyme' ELSE u.handle END as author,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM challenge_votes WHERE entry_id = e.id) as votes
      FROM challenge_entries e
      JOIN seals s ON e.seal_id = s.id
      JOIN users u ON e.user_id = u.id
      WHERE e.challenge_id = $1
      ORDER BY votes DESC
    `, [id, challenge.rows[0].is_anonymous]);

    return NextResponse.json({ challenge: challenge.rows[0], entries: entries.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    // Date limite dans 7 jours par d√©faut
    const deadline = new Date();
    deadline.setDate(deadline.getDate() + 7);

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO challenges (title, description, creator_id, deadline, is_anonymous) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)",
      [title, description, user.rows[0].id, deadline, is_anonymous]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { challenge_id, entry_id, pubkey } = await req.json();
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const voterId = user.rows[0].id;

    // V√©rifier qu'il ne vote pas pour lui-m√™me
    const entry = await pool.query("SELECT user_id FROM challenge_entries WHERE id = $1", [entry_id]);
    if (entry.rows.length > 0 && entry.rows[0].user_id === voterId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Tu ne peux pas voter pour ta propre ≈ìuvre !' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO challenge_votes (challenge_id, voter_id, entry_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",
      [challenge_id, voterId, entry_id]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

// Lire les commentaires d'une ≈ìuvre
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const sealId = searchParams.get('seal_id');
  if (!sealId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'ID manquant' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT c.id, c.content, c.created_at, u.handle as author
      FROM comments c JOIN users u ON c.user_id = u.id
      WHERE c.seal_id = $1 ORDER BY c.created_at ASC
    `, [sealId]);
    return NextResponse.json({ comments: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

// Ajouter un commentaire
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, content } = await req.json();
  if (!seal_id || !pubkey || !content) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const myId = user.rows[0].id;

    await pool.query("INSERT INTO comments (seal_id, user_id, content) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [seal_id, myId, content]);

    // Cr√©er une notification pour l'auteur de l'≈ìuvre
    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id, title FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [seal_id]);
    if (seal.rows.length > 0 && seal.rows[0].author_id !== myId) {
      await pool.query(
        "INSERT INTO notifications (user_id, actor_id, type, message, link) VALUES ($1, $2, 'comment', $3, $4)",
        [seal.rows[0].author_id, myId, `a comment√© ton ≈ìuvre "${seal.rows[0].title}"`, `/o/${seal_id}`]
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/download/[id]/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { GetObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { Readable } from 'node:stream';
import { extractS3Key } from '@/lib/safe-key';
import { apiError } from '@/lib/api-error';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';
export const maxDuration = 30;

function encodeContentDisposition(filename: string): string {
  const asciiFallback = filename
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '_')
    .replace(/["\\]/g, '_');
  return `attachment; filename="${asciiFallback}"; filename*=UTF-8''${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`;
}

async function toWebStream(body: any): Promise<ReadableStream> {
  if (body instanceof Readable) return Readable.toWeb(body) as unknown as ReadableStream;
  if (body && typeof body.getReader === 'function') return body as ReadableStream;
  if (body && typeof body[Symbol.asyncIterator] === 'function') {
    return new ReadableStream({
      async start(controller) {
        try {
          for await (const chunk of body) controller.enqueue(chunk);
          controller.close();
        } catch (e) { controller.error(e); }
      },
    });
  }
  throw new Error('Body S3 non support√©');
}

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const viewerPubkey = searchParams.get('viewer');

  try {
    const res = await pool.query(
      "SELECT file_path FROM seals WHERE id = $1 AND visibility = 'public'",
      [id]
    );
    if (res.rows.length === 0 || !res.rows[0].file_path) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const key = extractS3Key(res.rows[0].file_path);
    if (!key) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cl√© S3 invalide' }, { status: 500 });

    const obj = await r2Client.send(new GetObjectCommand({
      Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET,
      Key: key,
    }));
    if (!obj.Body) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Fichier introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    if (viewerPubkey) {
      pool.query(
        "INSERT INTO access_log (seal_id, viewer_id, action, context) SELECT $1, id, 'download', 'public' FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $2",
        [id, viewerPubkey]
      ).catch((e) => console.warn('[download] log √©chou√©', e));
    }

    const webStream = await toWebStream(obj.Body);
    const filename = key.split('/').pop() || 'file';

    return new NextResponse(webStream, {
      status: 200,
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': obj.ContentType || 'application/octet-stream',
        'Content-Disposition': encodeContentDisposition(filename),
        'Content-Length': obj.ContentLength ? String(obj.ContentLength) : '',
        'Cache-Control': 'private, max-age=0, must-revalidate',
      },
    });
  } catch (e) {
    return apiError(e, 'Erreur lors du t√©l√©chargement');
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cl√© manquante' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ favorites: [] });
    
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.category, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM favorites f
      JOIN seals s ON f.seal_id = s.id
      JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE f.user_id = $1 ORDER BY f.created_at DESC
    `, [user.rows[0].id]);
    return NextResponse.json({ favorites: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ favorites: [] }); }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, action } = await req.json(); // action: 'add' | 'remove'
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    if (action === 'remove') {
      await pool.query("DELETE FROM favorites WHERE user_id = $1 AND seal_id = $2", [user.rows[0].id, seal_id]);
    } else {
      await pool.query("INSERT INTO favorites (user_id, seal_id) VALUES ($1, $2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [user.rows[0].id, seal_id]);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/feed/friends/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');

  if (!myHandle) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });

  try {
    // 1. On trouve l'ID de l'utilisateur
    const me = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
    if (me.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });
    const myId = me.rows[0].id;

    // 2. On trouve les gens avec qui j'ai discut√© (mes contacts)
    const contactsQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT DISTINCT receiver_id as contact_id FROM messages WHERE sender_id = $1
      UNION
      SELECT DISTINCT sender_id as contact_id FROM messages WHERE receiver_id = $1
    `, [myId]);

    if (contactsQuery.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });
    const contactIds = contactsQuery.rows.map(r => r.contact_id);

    // 3. On r√©cup√®re leurs derni√®res ≈ìuvres publiques (5 max)
    const seals = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.author_id = ANY($1) AND s.is_public = TRUE
      ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 5
    `, [contactIds]);

    return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: seals.rows });
  } catch (error) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}

export async function GET() {

             encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author,

      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.visibility = 'public'
        AND s.is_mystery = FALSE
        AND s.is_ephemeral = FALSE
      ORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT 1
    `);
    if (result.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ seal: null, reason: 'empty' });
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ seal: result.rows[0] });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[random]', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ seal: null, reason: 'error' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/feed/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');
  const category = searchParams.get('category');

  try {
    let params: any[] = [];
    let whereClause = "s.visibility = 'public'";

    if (category && category !== 'all') {
      params.push(category);
      whereClause += ` AND s.category = $${params.length}`;
    }

    if (myHandle) {
      const me = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
      if (me.rows.length > 0) {
        const myId = me.rows[0].id;
        const friendsRes = await pool.query(`
          SELECT requester_id as id FROM friendships WHERE addressee_id = $1 AND status = 'accepted'
          UNION
          SELECT addressee_id as id FROM friendships WHERE requester_id = $1 AND status = 'accepted'
        `, [myId]);
        const friendIds = friendsRes.rows.map(r => r.id);
        if (friendIds.length > 0) {
          params.push(friendIds);
          whereClause = `(s.visibility = 'public' OR (s.visibility = 'friends' AND s.author_id = ANY($${params.length})))`;
          if (category && category !== 'all') {
             whereClause += ` AND s.category = $${params.length - 1}`; // On garde le filtre cat√©gorie
          }
        }
      }
    }

    const sql = `
      SELECT s.id, 
        CASE WHEN s.is_mystery = true THEN 'üîí Sceau Myst√®re' ELSE s.title END as title,
        CASE WHEN s.is_mystery = true AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id) < s.unlock_likes THEN null ELSE s.file_path END as file_path,
        s.is_mystery, s.unlock_likes, s.is_flash, s.is_ephemeral, s.ots_proof,
        s.description, s.category, s.timestamp, s.visibility, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author,
        s.project_id, s.version_number,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'like') as likes_like,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'wow') as likes_wow,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'inspired') as likes_inspired
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE ${whereClause} ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 30
    `;
    
    const result = await pool.query(sql, params);
    return NextResponse.json({ seals: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

export async function GET() {

    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.category, s.timestamp, s.location, u.handle as author,

      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/friends/respond/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { me, target, action } = await req.json(); // action: 'accept' | 'reject'
  try {
    const meId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0].id;
    const targetId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [target])).rows[0].id;

    if (action === 'accept') {
      await pool.query(`
        UPDATE friendships SET status = 'accepted' 
        WHERE requester_id = $1 AND addressee_id = $2
      `, [targetId, meId]);
    } else {
      await pool.query(`
        DELETE FROM friendships WHERE requester_id = $1 AND addressee_id = $2
      `, [targetId, meId]);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/friends/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    let me = searchParams.get('me');
    const search = searchParams.get('search');
    
    if (!me) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non connect√©' }, { status: 401 });
    // NOUVEAU : On nettoie le pseudo au cas o√π il y aurait un @
    me = me.replace('@', '');

    const myIdRes = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me]);
    if (myIdRes.rows.length === 0) {
      // Message plus clair pour le d√©bogage
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Ton compte n'existe pas dans la base de donn√©es. D√©connecte-toi et reconnecte-toi." }, { status: 404 });
    }
    const myId = myIdRes.rows[0].id;

    if (search) {
      const users = await pool.query(`
        SELECT handle, role FROM users 
        WHERE handle ILIKE $1 AND id != $2 LIMIT 10
      `, [`%${search.replace('@', '')}%`, myId]);
      return NextResponse.json({ searchResults: users.rows });
    }

    const friendsQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT 
        u.id, 
        u.handle,
        (SELECT title FROM seals WHERE author_id = u.id AND visibility = 'public' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_seal,
        (SELECT id FROM seals WHERE author_id = u.id AND visibility = 'public' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_seal_id
      FROM friendships f
      JOIN users u ON u.id = CASE WHEN f.requester_id = $1 THEN f.addressee_id ELSE f.requester_id END
      WHERE (f.requester_id = $1 OR f.addressee_id = $1) AND f.status = 'accepted'
    `, [myId]);

    const receivedQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT u.handle FROM friendships f 
      JOIN users u ON u.id = f.requester_id
      WHERE f.addressee_id = $1 AND f.status = 'pending'
    `, [myId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      friends: friendsQuery.rows, 
      received: receivedQuery.rows 
    });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Erreur API Friends:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Erreur base de donn√©es : " + error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { me, target } = await req.json();
    
    // Nettoyage des pseudos
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me.replace('@', '')])).rows[0]?.id;
    const targetId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [target.replace('@', '')])).rows[0]?.id;
    
    if (!myId || !targetId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur introuvable dans la base' }, { status: 404 });

    await pool.query(`
      INSERT INTO friendships (requester_id, addressee_id, status) 
      VALUES ($1, $2, 'pending') ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
    `, [myId, targetId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error: any) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/github/sync/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { pubkey, github_username } = await req.json();
    
    // 1. Sauvegarder le pseudo GitHub
    await pool.query("UPDATE users SET github_username = $1 WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $2", [github_username, pubkey]);
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;

    // 2. R√©cup√©rer les derniers commits publics via l'API GitHub
    const ghRes = await fetch(`https://api.github.com/users/${github_username}/events/public`, {
      headers: { 'User-Agent': 'SCEAU-App' }
    });
    const events = await ghRes.json();
    
    let commitsScelles = 0;
    for (const event of events) {
      if (event.type === 'PushEvent' && event.payload.commits.length > 0) {
        for (const commit of event.payload.commits) {
          // On cr√©e un hash bas√© sur le message et l'ID du commit
          const hashStr = `${commit.sha}-${commit.message}`;
          const hash = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(hashStr));
          let hashB64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...hash));

          // On ins√®re dans la base (ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING pour √©viter les doublons)
          await pool.query(
            `INSERT INTO seals (hash, timestamp, author_id, title, category, work_type, is_public) 
             VALUES (decode($1, 'base64'), $2, $3, $4, 'Code', 'commit', true) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`,
            [hashB64, event.created_at, userId, `Commit: ${commit.message.slice(0, 50)}`]
          );
          commitsScelles++;
        }
      }
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, commitsScelles });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur GitHub' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { name, creator_pubkey } = await req.json();
  try {
    const creator = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [creator_pubkey]);
    if (creator.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const creatorId = creator.rows[0].id;

    const group = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO groups (name, creator_id) VALUES ($1, $2) RETURNING id",
      [name, creatorId]
    );
    const groupId = group.rows[0].id;

    // Le cr√©ateur est automatiquement membre
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO group_members (group_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)", [groupId, creatorId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ group_id: groupId });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const me = searchParams.get('me');
  if (!me) return NextResponse.json({ groups: [] });

  try {
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0]?.id;
    if (!myId) return NextResponse.json({ groups: [] });

    const groups = await pool.query(`
      SELECT g.id, g.name, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM group_members WHERE group_id = g.id) as members_count
      FROM groups g
      JOIN group_members gm ON g.id = gm.group_id
      WHERE gm.user_id = $1 ORDER BY g.created_at DESC
    `, [myId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ groups: groups.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, reaction_type } = await req.json();
  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
  const myId = user.rows[0].id;

  try {
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO likes (seal_id, user_id, reaction_type) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [seal_id, myId, reaction_type || 'like']);
    
    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id, title FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [seal_id]);
    if (seal.rows.length > 0 && seal.rows[0].author_id !== myId) {
      await pool.query(
        "INSERT INTO notifications (user_id, actor_id, type, message, link) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)",
        [seal.rows[0].author_id, myId, 'like', `a r√©agi √† ton ≈ìuvre "${seal.rows[0].title}"`, `/o/${seal_id}`]
      );
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (e) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

FICHIER : src/app/api/messages/[handle]/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ handle: string }> }) {
  const { handle } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');

  try {
    const myIdResult = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
    const otherIdResult = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [handle]);
    
    if (myIdResult.rows.length === 0 || otherIdResult.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 400 });
    
    const myId = myIdResult.rows[0].id;
    const otherId = otherIdResult.rows[0].id;

    const messages = await pool.query(`
      SELECT m.content, m.created_at, u.handle as sender
      FROM messages m
      JOIN users u ON m.sender_id = u.id
      WHERE (m.sender_id = $1 AND m.receiver_id = $2) OR (m.sender_id = $2 AND m.receiver_id = $1)
      ORDER BY m.created_at ASC
    `, [myId, otherId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ messages: messages.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

FICHIER : src/app/api/messages/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const me = searchParams.get('me');
  
  try {
    if (!me) {
      const users = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users ORDER BY handle ASC");
      return NextResponse.json({ contacts: users.rows });
    }

    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0]?.id;
    if (!myId) return NextResponse.json({ contacts: [] });

    // On renvoie tous les utilisateurs, avec un flag pour savoir si on a d√©j√† discut√©
    const users = await pool.query(`
      SELECT u.id, u.handle,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM messages m WHERE (m.sender_id = u.id AND m.receiver_id = $1) OR (m.sender_id = $1 AND m.receiver_id = u.id)) > 0 as has_chat
      FROM users u 
      WHERE u.id != $1
      ORDER BY has_chat DESC, u.handle ASC
    `, [myId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ contacts: users.rows });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { sender_handle, receiver_handle, content } = await req.json();
    if (!sender_handle || !receiver_handle || !content) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const sender = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [sender_handle]);
    const receiver = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [receiver_handle]);
    
    if (sender.rows.length === 0 || receiver.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur inconnu' }, { status: 404 });
    }

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO messages (sender_id, receiver_id, content) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)",
      [sender.rows[0].id, receiver.rows[0].id, content]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error: any) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/api/messages/upload-audio/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { PutObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const data = await req.formData();
    const pubkey = data.get('pubkey') as string;
    const audio = data.get('audio') as File;

    if (!pubkey || !audio) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (audio.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Audio trop lourd (max 2 Mo)' }, { status: 413 });
    }

    const user = await pool.query(
      "SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1",
      [pubkey]
    );
    if (user.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const buffer = Buffer.from(await audio.arrayBuffer());
    const hash = sha256(new Uint8Array(buffer));
    const hashHex = Buffer.from(hash).toString('hex').slice(0, 16);
    const filename = `audio-${hashHex}-${Date.now()}.webm`;

    await r2Client.send(new PutObjectCommand({
      Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET,
      Key: filename,
      Body: buffer,
      ContentType: audio.type || 'audio/webm',
    }));

    const url = `${process.env.AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3}/${process.env.S3_BUCKET}/${filename}`;
    return NextResponse.json({ url });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[upload-audio]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur upload' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FICHIER : src/app/api/notifications/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const me = searchParams.get('me');
  if (!me) return NextResponse.json({ notifications: [], unread: 0 });

  try {
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0]?.id;
    if (!myId) return NextResponse.json({ notifications: [], unread: 0 });

    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT n.id, n.type, n.message, n.link, n.is_read, n.created_at, u.handle as actor
      FROM notifications n JOIN users u ON n.actor_id = u.id
      WHERE n.user_id = $1 ORDER BY n.created_at DESC LIMIT 20
    `, [myId]);

    const unread = result.rows.filter(n => !n.is_read).length;
    return NextResponse.json({ notifications: result.rows, unread });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ notifications: [], unread: 0 }); }
}

FICHIER : src/app/api/o/[id]/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const viewerPubkey = searchParams.get('viewer');

  try {
    const result = await pool.query(
      `SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.category, s.timestamp,
              s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.ots_proof,
              s.version_number, s.project_id,
              encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash,
              encode(s.hash, 'hex') as hash_hex,
              u.handle as author
       FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
       WHERE s.id = $1 AND s.visibility = 'public'`,
      [id]
    );

    if (result.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    }

    if (viewerPubkey) {
      const viewer = await pool.query(
        "SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1",
        [viewerPubkey]
      );
      if (viewer.rows.length > 0) {
        await pool.query(
          "INSERT INTO access_log (seal_id, viewer_id, action, context) VALUES ($1, $2, 'view', 'public')",
          [id, viewer.rows[0].id]
        );
      }
    }

    const { ots_proof, ...seal } = result.rows[0];
    return NextResponse.json({
      seal: { ...seal, has_ots_proof: !!ots_proof },
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[o/[id]]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
