'use client';
import './globals.css'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation'
import Manifeste from '@/components/Manifeste'

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(false);
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [unread, setUnread] = useState(0);
  const [showNotifs, setShowNotifs] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHandle(localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'));
    const theme = localStorage.getItem('sceau_theme');
    if (theme === 'dark') { setIsDark(true); document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); }
  }, [pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!handle) return;
    const fetchNotifs = () => {
      fetch(`/api/notifications?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
        setNotifications(d.notifications || []);
        setUnread(d.unread || 0);
      });
    };
    fetchNotifs();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchNotifs, 10000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [handle]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    const next = !isDark; setIsDark(next);
    if (next) { document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); localStorage.setItem('sceau_theme', 'dark'); }
    else { document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark'); localStorage.setItem('sceau_theme', 'light'); }
  };

  const markAsRead = () => {
    if (unread > 0 && handle) {
      fetch('/api/notifications/read', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: handle }) });
      setUnread(0);
    }
  };

  const isActive = (path: string) => pathname === path;
  const isToolsActive = pathname === '/seal' || pathname === '/verify';
  const showManifeste = ['/profil', '/seal', '/verify', '/settings'].includes(pathname);

  return (
    <html lang="fr">
        <link rel="manifest" href="/manifest.json" />
        <meta name="theme-color" content="#E76F51" />
      <head>
        <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
        <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossOrigin="anonymous" />
        <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Instrument+Serif:ital@0;1&family=Outfit:wght@300;400;500;600;700&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />
      </head>
      <body className="min-h-screen pb-24 font-sans">
        <nav className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-b border-[var(--border-color)] px-6 py-4 flex justify-between items-center sticky top-0 z-50">
          <Link href="/feed" className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51]">.</span></Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <button onClick={toggleTheme} className="text-xl cursor-pointer">{isDark ? '‚òÄÔ∏è' : 'üåô'}</button>
            
            {handle && (
              <div className="relative">
                <button onClick={() => { setShowNotifs(!showNotifs); if (!showNotifs) markAsRead(); }} className="text-xl relative">
                  üîî
                  {unread > 0 && <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-red-500 text-white text-[0.6rem] font-bold w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full">{unread}</span>}
                </button>
                
                {showNotifs && (
                  <div className="absolute right-0 mt-4 w-72 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden z-50">
                    <div className="p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] font-bold text-sm">Notifications</div>
                    <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
                      {notifications.length === 0 ? <p className="p-4 text-center text-xs opacity-50">Aucune notification.</p> : 
                        notifications.map((n: any) => (
                          <Link key={n.id} href={n.link} onClick={() => setShowNotifs(false)} className={`block p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition ${!n.is_read ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0]' : ''}`}>
                            <p className="text-xs" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}><span className="font-bold text-[#E76F51]">@{n.actor}</span> {n.message}</p>
                            <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">{new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')}</span>
                          </Link>
                        ))
                      }
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {!handle ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Link href="/pricing" className="text-sm font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--text-main)] transition hidden sm:block">Tarifs</Link>
                <Link href="/signup" className="text-sm font-bold bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg">Cr√©er un compte</Link>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <Link href="/profil" className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{handle}</Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </nav>
        
        <main className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">{children}</main>
        {showManifeste && <Manifeste />}

        <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-t border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-around py-3 z-50">
          <Link href="/feed" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isActive('/feed') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">üåê</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Feed</span>
          </Link>
          <Link href="/friends" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isActive('/friends') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">üë•</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Communaut√©</span>
          </Link>
          <Link href="/seal" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isToolsActive ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">üõ†Ô∏è</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Outils</span>
          </Link>
          <Link href="/messages" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isActive('/messages') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">üí¨</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Messages</span>
          </Link>
          <Link href="/profil" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isActive('/profil') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">üë§</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Profil</span>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </body>
    </html>
  )
}

FICHIER : src/app/login/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Login() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleLogin() {
    if (!email || !password) return setStatus("Email et mot de passe requis");
    setStatus("Connexion...");
    
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    if (res.ok) {
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_pubkey', data.pubkey);
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', data.handle);
      setStatus("‚úÖ Connect√© !");
      setTimeout(() => router.push('/feed'), 500);
    } else {
      setStatus("‚ùå " + data.error);
    }
  }

  // La fonction magique pour "Entr√©e"
  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleLogin(); };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Se connecter</h1>
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Email</label>
      <input type="email" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Mot de passe</label>
      <div className="relative mb-2">
        <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-3 opacity-50">{showPassword ? "üôà" : "üëÅÔ∏è"}</button>
      </div>
      
      <div className="text-right mb-6">
        <Link href="/forgot-password" className="text-sm text-[#E76F51] hover:underline font-bold">Mot de passe oubli√© ?</Link>
      </div>
      
      <button onClick={handleLogin} className="w-full bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl font-bold">Connexion</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      
      <p className="text-center mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Pas de compte ? <Link href="/signup" className="underline font-bold">Cr√©er un compte</Link></p>
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/messages/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState, useRef, Suspense } from 'react';
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import { generatePactCertificate } from '@/lib/pdf';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';

function MessagesInner() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const targetUser = searchParams.get('user');

  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState('');
  const [myPubkey, setMyPubkey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [friendHandles, setFriendHandles] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const [activeChat, setActiveChat] = useState<any>(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('');

  const [showGroupModal, setShowGroupModal] = useState(false);
  const [groupName, setGroupName] = useState('');
  const [activePact, setActivePact] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isSealingChat, setIsSealingChat] = useState(false);
  const [showSharedVault, setShowSharedVault] = useState(false);

  // Audio
  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [recordingTime, setRecordingTime] = useState(0);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const audioChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);
  const recordingIntervalRef = useRef<any>(null);

  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const prevMessagesLength = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handle = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle') || '';
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    setMyHandle(handle);
    setMyPubkey(pubkey);

    if (handle) {
      fetch(`/api/messages?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setContacts(d.contacts || []));
      fetch(`/api/groups/list?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setGroups(d.groups || []));
      fetch(`/api/friends?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
        const handles = new Set<string>((d.friends || []).map((f: any) => f.handle));
        setFriendHandles(handles);
      }).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (targetUser) setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: targetUser, name: targetUser });
  }, [targetUser]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeChat || !myHandle) return;
    const load = () => {
      const endpoint = activeChat.type === 'dm'
        ? `/api/messages/${activeChat.id}?me=${myHandle}`
        : `/api/groups/messages?id=${activeChat.id}`;

      fetch(endpoint).then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
        const serverMessages = data.messages || [];
        setMessages(prev => {
          const tempMessages = prev.filter(m => m.isTemp);
          const merged = [...serverMessages];
          tempMessages.forEach(temp => {
            if (!serverMessages.some((sm: any) => sm.content === temp.content && sm.sender === temp.sender)) merged.push(temp);
          });
          return merged;
        });
      });
    };
    load();
    const interval = setInterval(load, 3000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [activeChat, myHandle]);

  useEffect(() => { prevMessagesLength.current = 0; }, [activeChat]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (messages.length > prevMessagesLength.current) {
      messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      prevMessagesLength.current = messages.length;
    }
  }, [messages]);

  async function sendMessage() {
    if (!newMessage.trim() || !activeChat) return;
    const msg = newMessage;
    setNewMessage('');
    const tempId = `temp-${Date.now()}`;
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, { content: msg, sender: myHandle, created_at: new Date().toISOString(), isTemp: true, id: tempId }]);

    try {
      if (activeChat.type === 'dm') {
        await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: myHandle, receiver_handle: activeChat.id, content: msg }) });
      } else {
        await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: activeChat.id, sender_handle: myHandle, content: msg }) });
      }
      setTimeout(() => setMessages(prev => prev.filter(m => m.id !== tempId)), 500);
    } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  }

  async function startRecording() {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      mediaRecorderRef.current = mediaRecorder;
      audioChunksRef.current = [];
      setRecordingTime(0);
      recordingIntervalRef.current = setInterval(() => setRecordingTime(t => t + 1), 1000);

      mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (event) => {
        if (event.data.size > 0) audioChunksRef.current.push(event.data);
      };

      mediaRecorder.onstop = async () => {
        clearInterval(recordingIntervalRef.current);
        const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunksRef.current, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        if (audioBlob.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) return alert("Audio trop lourd (max 2 Mo).");
        if (!myPubkey) return;

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
        formData.append('audio', new File([audioBlob], `voice-${Date.now()}.webm`, { type: 'audio/webm' }));

        const res = await fetch('/api/messages/upload-audio', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || !data.url) return alert('Erreur upload audio');

        const msg = `[AUDIO]${data.url}`;
        if (activeChat.type === 'dm') {
          await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: myHandle, receiver_handle: activeChat.id, content: msg, is_audio: true }) });
        } else {
          await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: activeChat.id, sender_handle: myHandle, content: msg, is_audio: true }) });
        }
      };

      mediaRecorder.start();
      setIsRecording(true);
    } catch (err) { alert("Impossible d'acc√©der au micro."); }
  }

  function stopRecording() {
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && isRecording) {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
      setIsRecording(false);
      clearInterval(recordingIntervalRef.current);
    }
  }

  async function sealDiscussion() {
    if (!messages.length || !myPubkey) return;
    const confirm = window.confirm(`Sceller les ${Math.min(messages.length, 50)} derniers messages dans ton Coffre Priv√© ?`);
    if (!confirm) return;

    setIsSealingChat(true);
    const lastMessages = messages.slice(-50);
    const textToSeal = lastMessages.map(m => `[@${m.sender} - ${new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleString()}]\n${m.content.startsWith('[AUDIO]') ? 'Message vocal' : m.content}`).join('\n\n');

    const hash = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(textToSeal));
    let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('hash', btoa(hashStr));
    formData.append('title', `Discussion avec ${activeChat.name} ‚Äî ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    formData.append('description', textToSeal);
    formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
    formData.append('visibility', 'private');
    formData.append('isHosted', 'false');
    formData.append('isFlash', 'false');
    formData.append('isMystery', 'false');
    formData.append('isEphemeral', 'false');

    const res = await fetch('/api/seal', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    if (res.ok) alert("‚úÖ Discussion scell√©e ! Retrouve-la dans ton Coffre Priv√©.");
    else alert("Erreur lors du scellement.");
    setIsSealingChat(false);
  }

  async function createGroup() {
    if (!groupName || !myPubkey) return;
    const res = await fetch('/api/groups/create', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ name: groupName, creator_pubkey: myPubkey }) });
    if (res.ok) { alert("Groupe cr√©√© !"); setShowGroupModal(false); window.location.reload(); }
  }

  const startPact = () => router.push(`/seal?pact=true&chat_id=${activeChat.id}&chat_type=${activeChat.type}`);

  const getHandleColor = (handle: string) => {
    if (handle === myHandle) return 'var(--text-main)';
    return friendHandles.has(handle) ? '#4A90C2' : '#C75A22'; // Amis = Bleu, Inconnus = Orange
  };

  const sharedPacts = Array.from(
    new Map(
      messages
        .filter(m => m.content.startsWith('[PACTE]'))
        .map(m => {
          const [id, title] = m.content.replace('[PACTE]', '').split('|');
          return [id, { id, title }];
        })
    ).values()
  );

  const activeContacts = contacts.filter(c => c.has_chat);
  const suggestions = contacts.filter(c => !c.has_chat);

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 h-[calc(100vh-140px)] flex gap-4 font-serif max-w-5xl mx-auto relative">

      {/* LISTE */}
      <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-4 overflow-y-auto ${activeChat ? 'hidden md:block md:w-1/3' : 'w-full md:w-1/3'}`}>
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Messages</h1>
          <button onClick={() => setShowGroupModal(true)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-lg">+ Groupe</button>
        </div>

        {groups.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-2">Groupes</h2>
            {groups.map(g => (
              <div key={g.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'group', id: g.id, name: g.name })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#2C1810] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm">G</div>
                <div className="font-bold text-sm truncate text-[#2C1810]">{g.name}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-2">Discussions</h2>
        <div className="text-[0.55rem] text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-2">üîµ Amis ¬∑ üü† Inconnus</div>
        {activeContacts.map(c => {
          const isFriend = friendHandles.has(c.handle);
          return (
            <div key={c.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: c.handle, name: c.handle })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm text-white" style={{ background: isFriend ? '#4A90C2' : '#C75A22' }}>
                {c.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
              </div>
              <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: isFriend ? '#4A90C2' : '#C75A22'}}>@{c.handle}</div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
        {activeContacts.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-4">Aucune discussion active.</p>}

        {suggestions.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-6 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] pt-4">
            <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-2">Suggestions</h2>
            {suggestions.map(c => (
              <div key={c.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: c.handle, name: c.handle })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 opacity-70 hover:opacity-100">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm text-[#A89070] bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)]">
                  {c.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
                </div>
                <div className="font-bold text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">@{c.handle}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* CHAT */}
      {activeChat ? (
        <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl flex flex-col overflow-hidden w-full md:w-2/3 relative`}>
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button onClick={() => setActiveChat(null)} className="md:hidden text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>‚Üê</button>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                {activeChat.type === 'group' ? `üë• ${activeChat.name}` : `@${activeChat.name}`}
              </h2>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowSharedVault(true)} className="bg-[#FDF8F0] text-[#A88620] border border-[#E9C46A] text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg">üìÇ Coffre</button>
              <button onClick={sealDiscussion} disabled={isSealingChat} className="bg-[#2C1810] text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50">{isSealingChat ? '‚è≥...' : 'üîí Sceller'}</button>
              <button onClick={startPact} className="bg-[#4A90C2] text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg">+ Pacte</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto space-y-3">
            {messages.map((m, i) => {
              if (m.content.startsWith('[PACTE]')) {
                const [id, title] = m.content.replace('[PACTE]', '').split('|');
                return (
                  <div key={i} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] border-2 border-[#4A90C2] p-4 rounded-xl text-center my-4">
                    <div className="font-bold text-lg mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>ü§ù {title}</div>
                    <button onClick={() => setActivePact(id)} className="bg-[#4A90C2] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">Voir / Signer</button>
                  </div>
                );
              }

              const isAudio = m.content.startsWith('[AUDIO]');
              const audioSrc = isAudio ? m.content.replace('[AUDIO]', '') : null;

              return (
                <div key={i} className={`max-w-[80%] p-3 rounded-xl text-sm ${m.sender === myHandle ? 'bg-[#E76F51] text-white ml-auto rounded-br-none' : 'bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-bl-none'} ${m.isTemp ? 'opacity-60' : ''}`}>
                  {activeChat.type === 'group' && m.sender !== myHandle && (
                    <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold mb-1" style={{ color: getHandleColor(m.sender) }}>@{m.sender}</div>
                  )}
                  {isAudio ? <audio controls src={audioSrc!} className="h-10 w-56" /> : m.content}
                </div>
              );
            })}
            <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] flex gap-2 items-center">
            <button onClick={isRecording ? stopRecording : startRecording} className={`p-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-xl ${isRecording ? 'bg-red-500 text-white animate-pulse' : 'bg-[var(--bg-main)] text-[var(--text-main)]'}`}>
              {isRecording ? `${recordingTime}s ‚èπ` : 'üéôÔ∏è'}
            </button>
            <input type="text" value={newMessage} onChange={e => setNewMessage(e.target.value)} placeholder="√âcrire..." className="flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && sendMessage()} />
            <button onClick={sendMessage} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">‚Üí</button>
          </div>

          {showSharedVault && (
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] z-20 flex flex-col rounded-2xl">
              <div className="p-4 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-center">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-[#A88620]">üìÇ Coffre Partag√©</h3>
                <button onClick={() => setShowSharedVault(false)} className="text-xl opacity-50">‚úï</button>
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto">
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-4">Retrouvez ici tous les pactes et accords √©chang√©s dans cette discussion.</p>
                {sharedPacts.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-center text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Aucun pacte pour le moment.</p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {sharedPacts.map(p => (
                      <div key={p.id} onClick={() => { setShowSharedVault(false); setActivePact(p.id); }} className="bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#E9C46A] p-4 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition">
                        <div className="font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>ü§ù {p.title}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-[#A88620] mt-1">Cliquez pour voir les d√©tails et signer</div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="hidden md:flex flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl items-center justify-center text-[var(--text-muted)]">
          S√©lectionnez une discussion.
        </div>
      )}

      {showGroupModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cr√©er un Groupe</h2>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Nom du groupe" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={groupName} onChange={e => setGroupName(e.target.value)} />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowGroupModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={createGroup} className="flex-1 bg-[#4A90C2] text-white py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Cr√©er</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {activePact && <PactModal pactId={activePact} onClose={() => setActivePact(null)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function PactModal({ pactId, onClose }: { pactId: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [pact, setPact] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isDownloading, setIsDownloading] = useState(false);
  
  const loadPact = () => fetch(`/api/pacts/${pactId}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setPact(d));
  useEffect(() => { loadPact(); }, [pactId]);

  async function signPact() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    const res = await fetch('/api/pacts/sign', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ pact_id: pactId, pubkey }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.success) loadPact();
  }

  async function downloadCertificate() {
    if (!pact) return;
    setIsDownloading(true);
    const verifyUrl = `${window.location.origin}/o/${pact.id}`;
    const qrDataUrl = await QRCode.toDataURL(verifyUrl, { width: 300, margin: 1 });
    generatePactCertificate(
      pact.title, pact.description, pact.creator, pact.signataires || [], 
      pact.hash_str || 'Hash non disponible', qrDataUrl, pact.id
    );
    setIsDownloading(false);
  }

  if (!pact) return null;
  const myHandle = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
  const hasSigned = pact.signataires?.includes(myHandle);
  const isSealed = pact.status === 'scelle';

  // Parse s√©curis√© des m√©dias
  let mediaUrls: string[] = [];
  try {
    if (pact.media_urls) mediaUrls = JSON.parse(pact.media_urls);
  } catch (e) {
    if (pact.file_path) mediaUrls = [pact.file_path];
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className={`bg-[#FDF8F0] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-8 border-4 shadow-2xl relative max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto ${isSealed ? 'border-[#E9C46A]' : 'border-[#4A90C2]'}`}>
        
        {isSealed && (
          <div className="absolute -top-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-[#E9C46A] text-[#2C1810] px-6 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold tracking-widest shadow-md border-2 border-white">
            Certificat Scell√©
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <div className="text-5xl mb-3">{isSealed ? 'üèÜ' : 'ü§ù'}</div>
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-serif font-bold" style={{color: '#2C1810'}}>{pact.title}</h2>
          <p className="text-xs font-mono text-[#A88620] italic mt-2">Propos√© par @{pact.creator}</p>
        </div>

        <p className="italic mb-6 p-4 bg-white/50 border border-[#EFE0CA] rounded-xl text-sm text-center" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
          "{pact.description}"
        </p>

        {/* LA GALERIE DE M√âDIAS */}
        {mediaUrls.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mb-6">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#A89070] font-mono mb-2">M√©dias joints ({mediaUrls.length})</p>
            <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto snap-x hide-scrollbar pb-2">
              {mediaUrls.map((url: string, i: number) => {
                const isImage = url.match(/\.(jpeg|jpg|gif|png|webp)/i);
                const isVideo = url.match(/\.(mp4|webm|ogg|mov)/i);
                return (
                  <div key={i} className="snap-start shrink-0 w-32 h-32 bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#EFE0CA] rounded-xl overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center relative group">
                    {isImage ? (
                      <img src={url} alt="media" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                    ) : isVideo ? (
                      <video src={url} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                    ) : (
                      <a href={url} target="_blank" className="text-center p-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#2C1810] hover:text-[#E76F51]">üìÑ<br/>Fichier</a>
                    )}
                    <a href={url} target="_blank" rel="noopener" className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 flex items-center justify-center text-white text-xs font-bold transition">Ouvrir</a>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mb-6">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A88620] mb-3 text-center">
            Signatures officielles ({pact.signataires?.length || 0} / {pact.required_signatures})
          </h3>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
            {(pact.signataires || []).map((s: string) => (
              <span key={s} className="bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] border border-[#A8C77E] px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold shadow-sm">‚úÖ @{s}</span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 mt-8">
          {isSealed ? (
            <button onClick={downloadCertificate} disabled={isDownloading} className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E9C46A] to-[#F4A261] text-[#2C1810] py-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-lg shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transition flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
              {isDownloading ? 'G√©n√©ration...' : 'üìú T√©l√©charger le Certificat PDF'}
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button onClick={signPact} disabled={hasSigned} className="w-full bg-[#4A90C2] text-white py-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-lg shadow-lg hover:bg-[#2D6A9E] transition disabled:opacity-50">
              {hasSigned ? '‚úÖ Vous avez sign√©' : '‚úçÔ∏è Contresigner le Pacte'}
            </button>
          )}
          <button onClick={onClose} className="w-full py-2 font-bold opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition" style={{color: '#2C1810'}}>Fermer</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Messages() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>}>
      <MessagesInner />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

FICHIER : src/app/o/[id]/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { generateCertificate } from '@/lib/pdf';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';

export default function PublicSealPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [seal, setSeal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshMsg, setRefreshMsg] = useState('');
  const [versions, setVersions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [currentVersion, setCurrentVersion] = useState(1);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!params.id) return;

    fetch(`/api/seal/${params.id}/versions`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        setVersions(d.versions || []);
        setCurrentVersion(d.current_version || 1);
      })
      .catch(() => {});

    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    const qs = pubkey ? `?viewer=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}` : '';
    fetch(`/api/o/${params.id}${qs}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        if (d.seal) setSeal(d.seal);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [params.id]);

  async function refreshOts() {
    setRefreshMsg('Interrogation des calendars Bitcoin...');
    const res = await fetch(`/api/seal/${params.id}/ots/refresh`, { method: 'POST' });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.status === 'upgraded') setRefreshMsg('‚úÖ Preuve upgrad√©e avec confirmation Bitcoin');
    else setRefreshMsg(d.message || '‚è≥ Pas encore confirm√©.');
  }

  async function downloadCertificate() {
    if (!seal) return;
    const verifyUrl = `${window.location.origin}/o/${seal.id}`;
    const qrDataUrl = await QRCode.toDataURL(verifyUrl, { width: 300, margin: 1 });
    generateCertificate(
      seal.title,
      seal.author,
      seal.timestamp,
      seal.hash,
      qrDataUrl,
      seal.hash_hex,
      seal.id
    );
  }

  function downloadUrl() {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return '#';
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    const qs = pubkey ? `?viewer=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}` : '';
    return `/api/download/${params.id}${qs}`;
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;
  if (!seal) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono text-red-500">≈íuvre introuvable.</div>;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-main)] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 font-serif">
      <div className="w-full max-w-xl bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]"></div>

        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <span className="inline-block bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#EFE0CA] text-[#A89070] text-xs font-mono font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full italic mb-4">
            Preuve d'ant√©riorit√©
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{seal.title}</h1>
          <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)]">
            Cr√©√© par{' '}
            <Link href={`/u/${seal.author}`} className="font-bold text-[#E76F51] hover:underline">
              @{seal.author}
            </Link>
          </p>
        </div>

        {seal.description && (
          <p className="text-center italic mb-6 opacity-80" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            "{seal.description}"
          </p>
        )}

        {versions.length > 1 && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#4A90C2] bg-[#F0F6FC]">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
              <span className="text-xl">üìö</span>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-[#2D6A9E]">Dossier Miroir</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#2D6A9E]">{versions.length} versions ‚Äî celle-ci est la V{currentVersion}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              {versions.map((v: any) => (
                <a
                  key={v.id}
                  href={`/o/${v.id}`}
                  className={`flex justify-between items-center p-2 rounded-lg text-xs ${v.id === seal.id ? 'bg-white border border-[#4A90C2] font-bold' : 'hover:bg-white'}`}
                >
                  <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>V{v.version_number} ¬∑ {v.title}</span>
                  <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[#2D6A9E]">
                    {new Date(v.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')} {v.has_ots && '‚õìÔ∏è'}
                  </span>
                </a>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] mb-6 text-center">
          <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1">SHA-256</p>
          <p className="text-xs font-mono break-all" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            {seal.hash_hex || seal.hash}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2">
            Scell√© le {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className={`mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl border-2 ${seal.has_ots_proof ? 'border-[#7A9B5A] bg-[#F5F9EE]' : 'border-[#F4D0A8] bg-[#FCEAD6]'}`}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
            <span className="text-2xl">‚õìÔ∏è</span>
            <div>
              <p className={`text-sm font-bold ${seal.has_ots_proof ? 'text-[#5D7A3A]' : 'text-[#C44A2E]'}`}>
                Ancrage Bitcoin (OpenTimestamps)
              </p>
              <p className={`text-xs ${seal.has_ots_proof ? 'text-[#7A9B5A]' : 'text-[#C44A2E]'}`}>
                {seal.has_ots_proof ? 'Preuve .ots disponible' : 'Indisponible'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          {seal.has_ots_proof && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-3">
              <a href={`/api/seal/${seal.id}/ots`} download className="bg-[#5D7A3A] text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
                ‚¨áÔ∏è T√©l√©charger .ots
              </a>

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export default function AccessLogModal({ sealId, onClose }: { sealId: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    fetch(`/api/seal/${sealId}/access-log?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey || '')}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => { setEntries(d.entries || []); setLoading(false); });
  }, [sealId]);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üìñ Journal des acc√®s</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-xl opacity-50">‚úï</button>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {loading ? <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Chargement...</p> :
           entries.length === 0 ? <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)] py-8">Aucun acc√®s enregistr√© pour le moment.</p> :
           <div className="space-y-2">
             {entries.map((e, i) => (
               <div key={i} className="p-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] bg-[var(--bg-main)]">
                 <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline">
                   <span className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{e.viewer}</span>
                   <span className={`text-[0.6rem] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${e.action === 'download' ? 'bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E]' : 'bg-[#DCE8F5] text-[#2D6A9E]'}`}>{e.action}</span>
                 </div>
                 <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">
                   {new Date(e.at).toLocaleString('fr-FR')} ¬∑ compte de {e.viewer_profile.account_age_days} jours ¬∑ {e.viewer_profile.seals_count} sceaux
                 </div>
               </div>
             ))}
           </div>
          }
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/profil/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { generatePortfolioPDF } from '@/lib/pdf';
import AccessLogModal from './AccessLogModal';

export default function Profil() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('public');

  const [showPortfolioModal, setShowPortfolioModal] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSeals, setSelectedSeals] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('all');
  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState('');
  const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState('');
  const [accessLogSealId, setAccessLogSealId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showAccessLogList, setShowAccessLogList] = useState(false);

  async function loadProfil() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) { router.push('/login'); return; }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/profil?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`);
      const d = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && d.handle) setData({ ...d, seals: d.seals || [], badges: d.badges || [], favorites: d.favorites || [] });
      else setError("Profil introuvable.");
    } catch (e) { setError("Erreur serveur."); }
  }

  useEffect(() => { loadProfil(); }, [router]);

  const openPortfolioModal = () => {
    setShowPortfolioModal(true);
    setSelectedSeals(data.seals.map((s: any) => s.id));
    setFilterType('all');
    setStartDate('');
    setEndDate('');
  };

  const applyFilters = (type: string, start: string, end: string) => {
    let filtered = data.seals;
    if (type !== 'all') {
      if (type === 'flash') filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => s.is_flash);
      else filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => s.visibility === type && !s.is_flash);
    }
    if (start) filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => new Date(s.timestamp) >= new Date(start));
    if (end) filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => new Date(s.timestamp) <= new Date(end));
    setSelectedSeals(filtered.map((s: any) => s.id));
  };

  const handleTypeChange = (type: string) => { setFilterType(type); applyFilters(type, startDate, endDate); };
  const handleStartDate = (date: string) => { setStartDate(date); applyFilters(filterType, date, endDate); };
  const handleEndDate = (date: string) => { setEndDate(date); applyFilters(filterType, startDate, date); };

  const toggleSeal = (id: string) => {
    if (selectedSeals.includes(id)) setSelectedSeals(selectedSeals.filter(sid => sid !== id));
    else setSelectedSeals([...selectedSeals, id]);
  };

  const generatePDF = () => {
    const sealsToInclude = data.seals.filter((s: any) => selectedSeals.includes(s.id));
    if (sealsToInclude.length === 0) return alert("S√©lectionne au moins une ≈ìuvre !");
    generatePortfolioPDF(data.handle, sealsToInclude, startDate, endDate, data.is_pro);
    setShowPortfolioModal(false);
  };

  async function changeVisibility(sealId: string, newVisibility: string) {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    await fetch('/api/seal/update-visibility', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ seal_id: sealId, pubkey, visibility: newVisibility })
    });
    loadProfil();
  }

  if (error) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono text-red-500">{error}</div>;
  if (!data) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;

  const counts = {
    public: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.visibility === 'public' && !s.is_flash).length,
    friends: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.visibility === 'friends' && !s.is_flash).length,
    private: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.visibility === 'private' && !s.is_flash).length,
    flash: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.is_flash).length,
  };

  // ONGLET FAVORIS
  if (activeTab === 'favorites') {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-sans">
        <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('public')} className="text-sm font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-4 hover:underline">‚Üê Retour au coffre</button>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üìå Mes Favoris</h2>
        <div className="grid gap-3">
          {data.favorites?.map((fav: any) => (
            <Link href={`/o/${fav.id}`} key={fav.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] block hover:border-[#E76F51]">
              <div className="font-bold text-lg" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{fav.title}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{fav.author} ‚Ä¢ {new Date(fav.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</div>
            </Link>
          ))}
          {(!data.favorites || data.favorites.length === 0) && <p className="italic text-[var(--text-muted)] text-center py-10">Aucune ≈ìuvre sauvegard√©e.</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const filteredSeals = data.seals.filter((seal: any) => {
    if (activeTab === 'flash') return seal.is_flash === true;
    if (activeTab === 'public') return seal.visibility === 'public' && !seal.is_flash;
    if (activeTab === 'friends') return seal.visibility === 'friends' && !seal.is_flash;
    if (activeTab === 'private') return seal.visibility === 'private' && !seal.is_flash;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif relative">
      
      <div className="text-center mb-6 relative">
        <Link href="/settings" className="absolute top-0 right-0 text-2xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition" title="Param√®tres">‚öôÔ∏è</Link>
        
        <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-3xl mx-auto mb-4 shadow-lg relative">
          {data.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
          {data.sealed >= 10 && <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 bg-green-500 border-2 border-white rounded-full w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center text-xs">‚úì</div>}
        </div>
        
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
          @{data.handle}
          {data.is_pro && <span className="text-sm bg-[#2C1810] text-[#E9C46A] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-[#E9C46A] tracking-widest">üëë PRO</span>}
          {data.streak > 0 && <span className="text-sm bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-[#FCE0D6]">üî• {data.streak} jours</span>}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">{data.role || 'Identit√© souveraine'}</p>
        {data.bio && <p className="mt-3 text-sm italic opacity-80 max-w-md mx-auto" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>"{data.bio}"</p>}
      </div>

      {data.badges.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mb-8 bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#EFE0CA] p-4 rounded-2xl">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] italic mb-3 text-center">Badges</h2>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
            {data.badges.map((badge: any, i: number) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-white border border-[#EFE0CA] w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl shadow-sm cursor-help" title={badge.desc}>
                <span className="text-2xl">{badge.icon}</span>
                <span className="text-[0.55rem] font-bold text-[#7A5A3A] mt-1">{badge.name}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-6 rounded-2xl justify-around mb-8 shadow-sm">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.sealed}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">Scell√©s</div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.hosted}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">H√©berg√©s</div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.pacts}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">Pactes</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-4 mb-8">
        <button onClick={openPortfolioModal} className="flex-1 bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-md hover:opacity-80 transition">üìÑ Exporter mon Portfolio</button>
        <button onClick={() => setShowAccessLogList(true)} className="flex-1 bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] border border-[#C9D8E8] p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-sm hover:bg-[#E0EDF8] transition">üìñ Journal des acc√®s</button>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>üìö Mon Coffre</h2>
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap gap-1">
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('public')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'public' ? 'bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üåç Public ({counts.public})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('friends')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'friends' ? 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üë• Amis ({counts.friends})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('private')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'private' ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üîí Priv√© ({counts.private})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('flash')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'flash' ? 'bg-[#2C1810] text-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>‚ö° Flash ({counts.flash})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('favorites')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'favorites' ? 'bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üìå Favoris ({data.favorites?.length || 0})</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3 mb-10">
        {filteredSeals.length === 0 && <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic text-sm p-4 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl text-center">Aucune ≈ìuvre dans ce compartiment.</p>}
        
        {filteredSeals.map((seal: any) => (
          <div key={seal.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between sm:items-center shadow-sm gap-3">
            <div className="flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                <div className="font-bold text-lg" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                {seal.is_flash && <span className="text-xs bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] px-2 py-0.5 rounded">Flash</span>}
              </div>
              {seal.description && <div className="text-xs italic text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2">"{seal.description}"</div>}
              <div className="flex gap-3 text-xs font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                <span>{new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</span>
                <span>‚ù§Ô∏è {seal.likes_count || 0}</span>
                <span>üí¨ {seal.comments_count || 0}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setAccessLogSealId(seal.id)} className="text-xs font-bold text-[#4A90C2] hover:underline px-2" title="Voir le journal des acc√®s">üìñ</button>
              <select value={seal.is_flash ? 'flash' : seal.visibility} onChange={(e) => changeVisibility(seal.id, e.target.value)} className="text-xs border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-2 bg-[var(--bg-main)] font-bold outline-none cursor-pointer" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} disabled={seal.is_flash}>
                <option value="public">üåç Public</option>
                <option value="friends">üë• Amis</option>
                <option value="private">üîí Priv√©</option>
                {seal.is_flash && <option value="flash">‚ö° Flash</option>}
              </select>
              <button onClick={async () => {
                if (!confirm("Supprimer d√©finitivement cette ≈ìuvre ?")) return;
                const p = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
                await fetch(`/api/seal/delete?id=${seal.id}&pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(p!)}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
                loadProfil();
              }} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 p-2 text-xl">üóëÔ∏è</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {showPortfolioModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6 flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Configurer le Portfolio</h2>
            <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-3 text-[0.65rem] font-mono font-bold">
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('all')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'all' ? 'bg-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Tout</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('public')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'public' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#5D7A3A]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Public</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('private')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'private' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#C44A2E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Priv√©</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('flash')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'flash' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#C44A2E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Flash</button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
              <div className="flex-1">
                <label className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 block">Du</label>
                <input type="date" value={startDate} onChange={e => handleStartDate(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded-lg text-xs bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1">
                <label className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 block">Au</label>
                <input type="date" value={endDate} onChange={e => handleEndDate(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded-lg text-xs bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 mb-4 pr-1">
              {data.seals.map((seal: any) => (
                <label key={seal.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] cursor-pointer transition">
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={selectedSeals.includes(seal.id)} onChange={() => toggleSeal(seal.id)} className="w-5 h-5 accent-[#E76F51]" />
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="font-bold text-sm truncate" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                    <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">{new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</div>
                  </div>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowPortfolioModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-3 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={generatePDF} className="flex-1 bg-[var(--text-main)] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md">G√©n√©rer ({selectedSeals.length})</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {accessLogSealId && <AccessLogModal sealId={accessLogSealId} onClose={() => setAccessLogSealId(null)} />}

      {/* MODALE DE S√âLECTION DU JOURNAL DES ACC√àS */}
      {showAccessLogList && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Journal des acc√®s</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setShowAccessLogList(false)} className="text-2xl opacity-50">‚úï</button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-4">S√©lectionne une ≈ìuvre pour voir qui l'a consult√©e ou t√©l√©charg√©e.</p>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2">
              {data.seals.map((seal: any) => (
                <div 
                  key={seal.id} 
                  onClick={() => { setShowAccessLogList(false); setAccessLogSealId(seal.id); }} 
                  className="p-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] cursor-pointer transition flex justify-between items-center"
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">{new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</div>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[#4A90C2] text-sm font-bold">Voir ‚Üí</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : src/app/reset-password/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState, Suspense } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

function ResetForm() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const token = searchParams.get('token');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleReset() {
    if (!password) return setStatus('Mot de passe requis');
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/reset-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ token, newPassword: password }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) {
      setStatus('‚úÖ Mot de passe modifi√© !');
      setTimeout(() => router.push('/login'), 1500);
    } else setStatus('‚ùå ' + data.error);
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[#FFFCF7] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[#EFE0CA] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif">Nouveau mot de passe</h1>
      <div className="relative mb-4">
        <input type={show ? "text" : "password"} placeholder="Nouveau mot de passe" className="w-full border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10" value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button onClick={() => setShow(!show)} className="absolute right-3 top-3">{show ? "üôà" : "üëÅÔ∏è"}</button>
      </div>
      <button onClick={handleReset} className="w-full bg-[#2C1810] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold">Valider</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold">{status}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ResetPassword() {
  return <Suspense fallback={<div>Chargement...</div>}><ResetForm /></Suspense>;
}

'use client';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export default function Reset() {
  const router = useRouter();
  
  useEffect(() => {
    // On vide toute la m√©moire du t√©l√©phone
    localStorage.clear();
    // On attend 2 secondes et on renvoie vers l'accueil
    setTimeout(() => router.push('/'), 2000);
  }, [router]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[60vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center font-serif">
      <div className="text-5xl mb-4 animate-spin">‚è≥</div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Nettoyage en cours...</h1>
      <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Fermeture de la session pr√©c√©dente. Redirection...</p>
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : src/app/seal/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useState, useRef, useEffect, Suspense } from 'react';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';

function SealContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const isPactMode = searchParams.get('pact') === 'true';
  const chatId = searchParams.get('chat_id');
  const chatType = searchParams.get('chat_type');
  const challengeIdParam = searchParams.get('challenge_id');

  const [isFlashMode, setIsFlashMode] = useState(false);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'file' | 'text' | 'media' | 'audio'>('file');
  
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState('');
  const [textContent, setTextContent] = useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [pactFiles, setPactFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  
  const [visibility, setVisibility] = useState('public');
  const [revealAt, setRevealAt] = useState('');
  const [isHosted, setIsHosted] = useState(true);
  const [isMystery, setIsMystery] = useState(false);
  const [isEphemeral, setIsEphemeral] = useState(false);
  const [requiredSignatures, setRequiredSignatures] = useState(2);
  
  const [mySeals, setMySeals] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedProjectId, setSelectedProjectId] = useState<string>('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const [sealedResult, setSealedResult] = useState<any>(null);

  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [audioUrl, setAudioUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const audioChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const p = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!p || isPactMode) return;
    fetch(`/api/profil?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(p)}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        const roots = (d.seals || []).filter((x: any) => !x.is_flash && !x.is_ephemeral);
        setMySeals(roots);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, [isPactMode]);

  async function startRecording() {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      mediaRecorderRef.current = mediaRecorder;
      audioChunksRef.current = [];
      mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (event) => { if (event.data.size > 0) audioChunksRef.current.push(event.data); };
      mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
        const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunksRef.current, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        setFile(new File([audioBlob], `audio-${Date.now()}.webm`, { type: 'audio/webm' }));
        setAudioUrl(URL.createObjectURL(audioBlob));
      };
      mediaRecorder.start(); setIsRecording(true);
    } catch (err) { alert("Impossible d'acc√©der au micro."); }
  }

  function stopRecording() {
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && isRecording) {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
      setIsRecording(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSeal() {
    if (!title) return setStatus("Un titre est requis");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'file' && !file) return setStatus("Choisis un fichier");
    if ((isFlashMode || mode === 'text') && !textContent && !title) return setStatus("√âcris quelque chose");
    if (!isFlashMode && mode === 'media' && !file) return setStatus("Prends une photo ou vid√©o");
    if (!isFlashMode && mode === 'audio' && !file) return setStatus("Enregistre un audio");

    setStatus(isPactMode ? "Pr√©paration..." : "Calcul de l'empreinte...");
    try {
      let hashContent: Uint8Array;

      if (isPactMode) {
        const metaString = `${title}-${desc}-${pactFiles.map(f => f.name).join(',')}`;
        hashContent = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(metaString));
      } else if ((isFlashMode && mode === 'text') || mode === 'text') {
        hashContent = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(textContent || title));
      } else {
        if (!file) return setStatus("Fichier manquant");
        const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
        hashContent = sha256(new Uint8Array(buffer));
      }

      let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hashContent.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hashContent[i]);
      sendToServer(btoa(hashStr));
    } catch (e) { setStatus("‚ùå Erreur de gravure"); }
  }

  async function sendToServer(hashB64: string) {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return setStatus("Connecte-toi !");

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('hash', hashB64);
    formData.append('title', title);
    formData.append('description', mode === 'text' ? textContent : desc);
    formData.append('pubkey', pubkey);
    
    let endpoint = '/api/seal';

    if (isPactMode) {
      endpoint = '/api/pacts/create';
      formData.append('group_id', chatType === 'group' ? (chatId as string) : '');
      formData.append('required_signatures', requiredSignatures.toString());
      formData.append('isHosted', 'true');
      pactFiles.forEach(f => formData.append('files', f));
    } else {
      formData.append('isFlash', isFlashMode.toString());
      formData.append('visibility', visibility);
      formData.append('reveal_at', revealAt);
      formData.append('isMystery', isMystery.toString());
      formData.append('isEphemeral', isEphemeral.toString());
      
      if (selectedProjectId) formData.append('project_id', selectedProjectId);
      if (challengeIdParam) formData.append('challenge_id', challengeIdParam);
      
      if (!isFlashMode && file) formData.append('file', file);
      formData.append('isHosted', (!isFlashMode && isHosted).toString());
    }

    setStatus("Ancrage Bitcoin...");
    const res = await fetch(endpoint, { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    if (res.ok) {
      if (isPactMode) {
        const msg = `[PACTE]${data.id}|${data.title}`;
        if (chatType === 'dm') {
          await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'), receiver_handle: chatId, content: msg }) });
        } else {
          await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: chatId, sender_handle: localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'), content: msg }) });
        }
        window.location.href = '/messages';
      } else {
        setStatus("‚úÖ Scell√© !");
        setSealedResult({ title, hash: hashB64, date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR'), isFlash: isFlashMode });
      }
    } else {
      setStatus("‚ùå " + data.error);
    }
  }

  if (sealedResult) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 font-serif text-center flex flex-col items-center">
        <div className="text-6xl mb-4">{sealedResult.isFlash ? '‚ö°' : '‚úÖ'}</div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2">C'est grav√©.</h1>
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl border w-full max-w-sm text-left shadow-lg mt-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold mb-2">{sealedResult.title}</h3>
          <p className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono break-all bg-gray-50 p-2 rounded">{sealedResult.hash}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-right mt-3">Scell√© le {sealedResult.date}</p>
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => window.location.reload()} className="mt-8 bg-black text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold w-full max-w-sm">Nouveau</button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 font-sans max-w-md mx-auto">
      <div className="text-center mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2">{isPactMode ? "Nouveau Pacte." : (isFlashMode ? "Flash." : "L'Atelier.")}</h1>
      </div>

      {!isPactMode && (
        <div className="flex bg-[#F9F6F0] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4">
          <button onClick={() => { setIsFlashMode(false); setMode('file'); }} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm ${!isFlashMode ? 'bg-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üìÅ Normal</button>
          <button onClick={() => { setIsFlashMode(true); setMode('text'); }} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm ${isFlashMode ? 'bg-black text-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>‚ö° Flash</button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-4 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm">
        
        <div className="flex bg-[#F9F6F0] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4 text-xs font-mono">
          {!isFlashMode && !isPactMode && <button onClick={() => setMode('file')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold ${mode === 'file' ? 'bg-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üìÅ</button>}
          <button onClick={() => setMode('text')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold ${mode === 'text' ? 'bg-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>‚úçÔ∏è Texte</button>
          <button onClick={() => setMode('media')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold ${mode === 'media' ? 'bg-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üì∏ Photo</button>
          <button onClick={() => setMode('audio')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold ${mode === 'audio' ? 'bg-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üéôÔ∏è Audio</button>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold opacity-50 uppercase">Titre</label>
          <input type="text" className="w-full border-2 rounded-xl p-3 outline-none" value={title} onChange={e=>setTitle(e.target.value)} />
        </div>

        {isPactMode && (
          <div className="bg-[#FDF8F0] p-4 rounded-xl border">
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold block mb-2">üìÅ M√©dias (Multi-fichiers)</label>
            <input type="file" multiple className="w-full text-sm" onChange={e => setPactFiles(Array.from(e.target.files || []))} />
          </div>
        )}

        {mode === 'text' && <textarea className="w-full border-2 rounded-xl p-4 outline-none min-h-[100px]" value={textContent} onChange={e=>setTextContent(e.target.value)} />}
        {mode === 'file' && !isFlashMode && <input type="file" onChange={e=>setFile(e.target.files?.[0]||null)} />}
        {mode === 'media' && <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" capture="environment" onChange={e=>setFile(e.target.files?.[0]||null)} />}
        
        {mode === 'audio' && (
          <div>
            {!isRecording && !audioUrl && <button onClick={startRecording} className="w-full bg-red-500 text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold">üî¥ Enregistrer</button>}
            {isRecording && <button onClick={stopRecording} className="w-full bg-black text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold animate-pulse">‚èπ Arr√™ter</button>}
            {audioUrl && !isRecording && <audio controls src={audioUrl} className="w-full mt-2" />}
          </div>
        )}

        {!isFlashMode && !isPactMode && (
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-4 border-t">
            <div className="flex bg-white rounded-xl border p-1">
              <button onClick={() => setVisibility('public')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded ${visibility === 'public' ? 'bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üåç Public</button>
              <button onClick={() => setVisibility('friends')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded ${visibility === 'friends' ? 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üë• Amis</button>
              <button onClick={() => setVisibility('private')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded ${visibility === 'private' ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>üîí Priv√©</button>
            </div>
            <input type="date" value={revealAt} onChange={e=>setRevealAt(e.target.value)} className="w-full border-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-yellow-50 text-sm" placeholder="Capsule Temporelle" />
          </div>
        )}

        {isPactMode && (
          <input type="number" min="2" className="w-full border-2 p-2 rounded-lg text-center font-bold" value={requiredSignatures} onChange={e=>setRequiredSignatures(parseInt(e.target.value))} />
        )}

        <button onClick={handleSeal} className="w-full p-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-white bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] mt-4">SCELLER</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Seal() {
  return <Suspense fallback={<div className="p-10 text-center">Chargement...</div>}><SealContent /></Suspense>;
}
